Conclusion: 没有单一对齐方法具有绝对优势，缩小奖励-质量差距需要更丰富的奖励设计和对更多方言资源的持续投入。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly \emph{understand} dialectal English, yet still \emph{produce} only standard, US-leaning English, leaving dialectal generation, the harder half of the problem, largely unaddressed. We introduce \textbf{DiaLLM}, which continually pretrains three open-weight language model families on the International Corpus of English and applies implicit and explicit post-training paradigms, each combined with three model alignment strategies, giving the first controlled comparison of these components across Australian, Indian, and Northern British English. Our results reveal that dialectal robustness and generation are \emph{dissociated}: benchmarks are shaped by continual pretraining and SFT, while alignment visibly reshapes generation in ways benchmarks do not capture. Explicit variety-targeted adaptation produces output reliably recognised as dialectal and preferred over broad alignment, yet the method that most aggressively optimises the dialectal reward is not preferred by human evaluators. Independent linguistic analysis corroborates this reward-quality gap, most clearly on two of the three families. No single alignment method dominates, and closing the gap will require richer reward designs and continued investment in dialectal resources. We release all code, checkpoints, and preference datasets.

</details>


### [106] [Does Bielik Know What It Doesn't Know? Activation Dispersion Separates Entity Familiarity from Factual Reliability Across Model Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07670)
*Grzegorz Brzezinka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型在生成答案前，其激活值是否能反映对实体的熟悉程度，以及该信号能否预测事实可靠性。在四种波兰Bielik模型上，通过无监督的离散度指标（逆参与比和谱熵）和监督线性探针，发现模型能高精度区分熟悉实体与虚构实体（AUROC 0.95-1.00），且该信号在模型规模较小时已达饱和。然而，事实可靠性（正确回答率）随模型规模显著提升，且与熟悉度信号呈分离状态。模型内部虽能感知实体熟悉度，但几乎不会主动拒绝回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在生成答案之前，其内部激活状态是否已经编码了对实体的熟悉程度，以及这种内在信号能否作为预测模型事实可靠性的指标，从而理解幻觉产生的机制。

Method: 在四种波兰Bielik模型（1.5B-11B参数）上，对四个实体领域（运动员、城市、作家、音乐家）各选取42个知名、42个冷门但真实和42个虚构实体，每个实体提出一个单句问题（共504个提示）。使用两种无监督单向前向传播离散度指标（逆参与比和谱熵）分析后SwiGLU MLP激活值，并训练有监督线性探针。同时评估模型的回答正确率，并对比五样本语义熵基线。

Result: 无监督离散度指标以AUROC 0.95-1.00区分已知与虚构实体，线性探针达到0.99-1.00。信号在1.5B参数时已达饱和，但事实正确率随规模急剧提升：1.5B、4.5B、7B、11B模型分别正确回答42个知名运动员中的0、2、10、19个。在已知实体内区分正确与幻觉答案，探针仅达0.93，五样本语义熵基线仅0.71-0.83。2520个回答中仅有2次拒绝和1次模糊回答。

Conclusion: 实体熟悉度信号和事实可靠性是两种不同的现象，遵循不同的规模缩放曲线。模型内部能感知实体熟悉度，但这种认知并未转化为可靠的回答行为或拒绝机制，解释了为何模型明知实体却仍会产生幻觉。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate most about entities they have never seen. We ask whether a model's activations betray entity familiarity before a single answer token is generated, and whether that signal predicts the factual reliability of the answers. On four Polish Bielik models (1.5B-11B parameters), we probe four entity domains (athletes, cities, writers, musicians), each with 42 well-known, 42 obscure-but-real, and 42 fabricated entities addressed by a one-sentence question (504 prompts per model). Two unsupervised, single-forward-pass dispersion measures over post-SwiGLU MLP activations, inverse participation ratio and spectral entropy, separate known from fabricated entities at AUROC 0.95-1.00 across all domains and scales; a supervised linear probe reaches 0.99-1.00. Both clear selection-aware permutation floors of about 0.70-0.74 (empirical p<=1e-3), survive held-out layer selection (0.93-0.99), and persist on real names (known vs. obscure-but-real: 0.96-1.00). The signal transfers across entity types (mean off-diagonal AUROC 0.92-0.99); a matched-template counterfactual shows the only large drops are template-caused, not entity-type effects, and the signal is diffuse across heads. This representational signal is already at ceiling at 1.5B, whereas behavioral factual reliability scales sharply: 0, 2, 10, and 19 of 42 known athletes are answered fully correctly by the 1.5B, 4.5B, 7B, and 11B models under a strict judge. Within known entities, separating correct from hallucinated answers is much harder (probe 0.93; dispersion no better than a first-token-entropy baseline). A five-sample semantic-entropy baseline reaches only 0.71-0.83 at 5x the inference cost. Despite this internal awareness, the models almost never abstain: an audit of 2,520 answers finds 2 refusals and 1 hedge. Entity familiarity and factual reliability are distinct phenomena on different scaling curves.

</details>


### [107] [From Noisy Traces to Root Causes: Structural Trajectory Analysis and Causal Extraction for Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07702)
*Ying Chang,Jiahang Xu,Xuan Feng,Chenyuan Yang,Peng Cheng,Yuqing Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STRACE是一个通过因果提取和轨迹分析构建高信噪比优化上下文的框架，在长周期智能体优化任务中显著优于基线方法，在形式化验证任务上实现1.4倍成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反射机制的长周期智能体优化面临双重困境：批量轨迹冗余异构导致优化效率低且过拟合于低价值失败；单条轨迹包含大量无关步骤，而简单的截断或滑动窗口会丢弃因果关键证据，产生误导性优化信号。

Method: STRACE在批量层面挖掘失败模式以过滤冗余轨迹并保留代表性失败；在单条轨迹层面，基于文本依赖图进行因果定位，移除非因果步骤并识别真正的根因模块。

Result: 在VeruSAGE-Bench形式化验证任务上，STRACE成功优化人类专家设计的智能体，将成功率从42.5%提升至58.5%（1.4倍提升），显著优于标准上下文过滤基线。

Conclusion: STRACE通过层级化的因果提取策略有效解决了长周期智能体优化中的信噪比问题，为基于LLM的反射优化提供了更精确高效的范式。

Abstract: The optimization of long-horizon agents increasingly relies on reflection-based mechanisms, where a large language model (LLM) acts as an optimizer to diagnose agent failures and improve agent policies. However, real execution traces are difficult to use directly for optimization: large trace collections are often redundant and heterogeneous, making optimization inefficient and prone to overfitting to low-value failures; meanwhile, each individual trajectory also contains many irrelevant steps, while naive context reduction methods such as truncation or sliding windows can discard causally important evidence and produce misleading optimization signals. To resolve this dilemma, we introduce STRACE (Structural TRajectory Analysis and Causal Extraction), a framework that constructs high signal-noise optimization contexts for more precise and effective optimization. At the batch level, STRACE mines failure patterns to filter redundant traces and retain representative failures; within each selected trace, it performs causal localization over a textual dependency graph to remove non-causal steps and identify the true root-cause module for optimization. Empirical results demonstrate that STRACE significantly outperforms standard context-filtering baselines. Notably, on a challenging formal verification task (VeruSAGE-Bench), it successfully optimizes human-expert designed agents, delivering $1.4\times$ success-rate improvement (42.5% to 58.5%). The code is available at https://github.com/moomight/STRACE .

</details>


### [108] [Co-LMLM: Continuous-Query Limited Memory Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07707)
*Yair Feldman,Linxi Zhao,Nathan Godey,Dongyoung Go,Yilun Hua,Kilian Q. Weinberger,Jennifer J. Sun,Yoav Artzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CO-LMLM，一种连续查询的有限记忆语言模型，通过连续向量键-文本值知识库实现高效外部知识检索，在困惑度和事实准确性上优于传统LMLM和LLM，360M模型即可达到与gpt-4o-mini相当的事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LMLM依赖关系型知识库和离散查询，限制了知识表示的灵活性和应用范围。现有方法局限于Wikipedia等结构化数据源，且知识查询方式不够高效。CO-LMLM旨在通过连续向量查询打破这些限制，实现更灵活、高效的知识外部化。

Method: 提出CO-LMLM架构：知识库采用连续向量键（continuous keys）与文本知识值（textual knowledge values）的配对方式；设计注释流水线（annotation pipeline）对任意文本中的自由形式事实片段进行标注，突破Wikipedia限制；模型在预训练阶段通过连续查询从知识库检索知识并融入生成过程。

Result: 在Wikipedia和FineWeb-Edu上预训练，多个模型规模下，CO-LMLM在困惑度和事实精确度上均优于先前LMLM和传统LLM。360M参数模型的困惑度低于使用40倍数据预训练的模型；SimpleQA验证的性能与gpt-4o-mini相当，高于Claude Sonnet 4.5。

Conclusion: CO-LMLM通过连续查询机制显著提升了LMLM的知识表示灵活性和检索效率，在更小模型规模下实现了与更大模型相当甚至更优的事实准确性，验证了外部化知识范式的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Limited memory language models (LMLMs) externalize factual knowledge during pretraining to a knowledge base (KB), rather than memorizing it in their weights. During generation, the model then fetches knowledge from the KB as needed. This recently introduced paradigm provides multiple advantages, including knowledge control capabilities that remain beyond conventional LLMs. We propose continuous-query LMLM (CO-LMLM), where the KB pairs continuous keys with textual knowledge values, a significant departure from prior reliance on relational KB and queries. CO-LMLM generates flexible vector queries at minimal cost, while still integrating human-readable and attributable retrieved knowledge into its generation. We pair this design with an annotation pipeline that tags free-form factual spans in arbitrary text, removing prior work's restriction to Wikipedia. Across pretraining on Wikipedia and FineWeb-Edu and at multiple model scales, CO-LMLM outperforms prior LMLMs and vanilla LLMs in both perplexity and factual precision. At 360M scale, this includes lower perplexity than models pretrained on 40x more data, and SimpleQA-verified performance that is in line with gpt-4o-mini and higher than Claude Sonnet 4.5.

</details>


### [109] [Accurate, Interdisciplinary and Transparent Structure-property Understanding with Deep Native Structural Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07708)
*Chen Tang,Yizhou Wang,Jianyu Wu,Lintao Wang,Shixiang Tang,Pengze Li,Encheng Su,Jun Yao,Jiabei Xiao,Yuqi Shi,Jielan Li,Hongxia Hao,Zhangyang Gao,Fang Wu,Ben Fei,Xiangyu Yue,Pan Tan,Bozitao Zhong,Jinouwen Zhang,Aoran Wang,Yan Lu,Jiaheng Liu,Xinzhu Ma,Liang Hong,Mingyue Zheng,Phil Torr,Bowen Zhou,Wanli Ouyang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SciReasoner是一个多模态科学基础模型，通过将坐标、拓扑和周期性连接离散化为统一的结构感知词汇，实现蛋白质、小分子和无机晶体的原生结构推理，在86个基准测试的67个任务上达到最优性能，且专家评估中98%的推理轨迹优于或相当于前沿大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构-性质关系是生物学、化学和材料科学的基础，但将人工智能应用于这一过程面临表征与推理的双重挑战：模型必须保留领域原生的结构信息，同时展示具体证据如何在科学原理和物理约束下支持预测。

Method: 将坐标、拓扑和周期性连接离散化为统一的结构感知词汇，将结构令牌作为可寻址的证据单元进行推理，从而在科学约束下实现多模态结构推理。

Result: 在基因本体预测中，低同源性和孤儿蛋白的细胞组分标注F_max从0.42提升至0.55；单步逆合成准确率从0.63提升至0.72，并生成片段级断键和前体验证轨迹；材料科学中有效分离元素相和化合物相并区分高/低带隙区域；在86个基准测试中67个任务达到最优；98%的推理轨迹在专家评估中优于或相当于前沿大语言模型。

Conclusion: 通过将结构作为可检查的推理基质，SciReasoner将准确预测与可解释的科学推理联系起来，在科学约束下实现了高性能的结构理解与推理能力。

Abstract: Structure-property relationships are foundational to biology, chemistry and materials science, where function, reactivity and physical response emerge from spatial, chemical and periodic organization. Mechanistically explaining these relationships requires interpreting structural evidence through scientific principles and physical constraints, from stereochemistry and bonding to symmetry, energetics and periodic order. However, applying artificial intelligence to this process presents a joint challenge of representation and reasoning: models must preserve domain-native structural information while showing how specific evidence supports predictions under these constraints. Here we introduce SciReasoner, a multimodal scientific foundation model for native structural reasoning across proteins, small molecules and inorganic crystals. SciReasoner discretizes coordinates, topologies and periodic connectivities into a unified structure-aware vocabulary, treating structural tokens as addressable evidence units during reasoning. In homology-controlled Gene Ontology prediction, SciReasoner improves Cellular Component annotation for low-homology and orphan-like proteins, increasing $F_{\max}$ from 0.42 to 0.55. In chemistry, it raises single-step retrosynthesis accuracy from 0.63 to 0.72 while generating fragment-level disconnection and precursor-verification traces. In materials science, its representations separate elemental and compound phases and resolve high- and low-band-gap regimes. Across 86 benchmarks, SciReasoner achieves state-of-the-art performance on 67 tasks. Double-blind expert evaluation rates its reasoning traces as preferred or at least comparable to those of a frontier large language model in 98% of cases. By making structure an inspectable substrate for reasoning under scientific constraints, SciReasoner connects accurate prediction with interpretable scientific inference.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Digital Fragmentation and Generative AI Use Across 103 Million Application Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06681)
*Sumer S. Vaid,Ashley V. Whillans*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对1017名员工1.03亿个应用事件的分析，发现数字碎片化（每天数千次的应用切换）主要源于个体内部日间波动（44.6%），而非员工个体差异（35.8%）或组织差异（19.6%）；碎片化在工作周内逐渐上升，周末/假期后重置；沟通应用使用与更高碎片化相关，而生成式AI使用后则出现更窄、更长、更可预测的应用使用模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识工作者每天在应用间切换数千次，每年近十分之一的工作时间消耗在这一数字化碎片化过程中，但学界对其成因（是个体特质、组织环境还是日常波动所致）尚不清楚。

Method: 分析来自8个组织（主要为知识工作者，如法律、金融服务）的1017名员工第二级别的1.03亿个应用事件，采用方差分解方法评估个体间、个体内（日间）和组织间三个层面的碎片化变异贡献。

Result: 个体内部日间波动解释了44.6%的碎片化变异，个体间差异为35.8%，组织间差异仅19.6%；碎片化在工作周中逐渐上升并在周末/假期后重置；高沟通应用使用与更高碎片化相关；生成式AI使用日碎片化更高，但AI使用后表现为更窄、更长、更可预测的应用使用模式。

Conclusion: 工作日是理解和干预数字碎片化的关键层面，生成式AI可能有助于结构化碎片化工作而非仅仅加剧碎片化。

Abstract: Knowledge workers switch between applications thousands of times per day, spending nearly a tenth of the work year transitioning between digital applications in a process called digital fragmentation. Whether this fragmentation reflects who an employee is, where they work, or what kind of day they are having, has remained an open question. We analyzed 103 million application events recorded second-by-second from 1,017 employees across eight organizations that largely employ knowledge workers (e.g., law, financial services). Day-to-day variation in fragmentation within individual employees accounted for 44.6% of the variation in digital fragmentation, slightly exceeding stable individual differences between employees (35.8%), and far exceeding variation between organizations (19.6%). Fragmentation rose over the work week and reset after weekends and holidays. Higher-than-typical use of communication applications coincided with more fragmented work. Generative AI use also occurred on more fragmented days, but the period following AI use was marked by narrower, longer, and more predictable application use. These findings identify the workday as a key level for understanding and intervening on digital fragmentation and suggest that AI may help structure fragmented work rather than merely intensify it.

</details>


### [111] [Exploring the Interaction of Explanation Styles, Context, and Trust of AI Privacy Redaction in AI-mediated Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06687)
*Roshni Kaushik,Maarten Sap,Koichi Onoue*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究调查了AI中介通信中，对敏感内容进行遮蔽（红action）操作时，不同详细程度的解释如何影响用户信任。通过用户研究（N=180）发现，提供解释能显著提升用户对系统隐私保护效果的评价，且在大量遮蔽时解释更有帮助；同时用户对解释的偏好受个体差异（年龄、AI熟悉度）和情境因素影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐私敏感领域中，AI中介通信需要平衡信息传递与隐私保护。当AI系统遮蔽或保留信息时，用户如何感知这些干预，以及解释系统行为能否提升信任，是尚未充分研究的问题。

Method: 设计一个验证过的系统，从消息中移除敏感内容并生成不同详细程度的解释；通过180名参与者的用户研究，分析不同遮蔽量和不同解释详细程度下用户信任和偏好的变化。

Result: 提供解释时，参与者认为系统在隐私保护方面更有效（p<0.05, Cohen's d ~ 0.3）；当系统进行大量遮蔽时，参与者更依赖解释且认为解释更有帮助（p<0.05, Cohen's f ~ 0.2）；用户对解释的偏好受年龄、AI熟悉度等个体差异影响。

Conclusion: 在AI中介通信中平衡透明度和隐私保护至关重要且具有挑战性，自适应的、上下文感知的解释对于设计隐私感知且值得信赖的AI系统不可或缺。

Abstract: AI-mediated communication is increasingly being utilized to help facilitate interactions; however, in privacy sensitive domains, an AI mediator has the additional challenge of considering how to preserve privacy. In these contexts, a mediator may redact or withhold information, raising questions about how users perceive these interventions and whether explanations of system behavior can improve trust. In this work, we investigate how explanations of redaction operations can affect user trust in AI-mediated communication. We devise a scenario where a validated system removes sensitive content from messages and generates explanations of varying detail to communicate its decisions to recipients. We then conduct a user study with 180 participants that studies how user trust and preferences vary for cases with different amounts of redacted content and different levels of explanation detail. Our results show that participants believed our system was more effective at preserving privacy when explanations were provided (p<0.05, Cohen's d ~ 0.3). We also found that contextual factors had an impact; participants relied more on explanations and found them more helpful when the system performed extensive redactions (p<0.05, Cohen's f ~ 0.2). We also found that explanation preferences depended on individual differences as well, and factors such as age and baseline familiarity with AI affected user trust in our system. These findings highlight the importance and challenge of balancing transparency and privacy in AI-mediated communications and suggest that adaptive, context-aware explanations are essential for designing privacy-aware, trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [112] [Flowcode: An AI-Powered Programming Environment for Scaffolding Iteration in Creative Computing Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06721)
*Tiffany Tseng,Liliana Hanem Seoror,Jeevika Adda,Meitalia Factor,Rona Darabi,Kiley R Matschke,Tiffany Fu,Annie Lin,Alekhya Maram,Arya Sinha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Flowcode是一个创造性编程环境，集成了流程图可视化代码结构和聊天界面，帮助新手创意编程者通过"氛围编码"学习理解和扩展现有代码。经过两项研究迭代设计，探讨了可视化和摩擦在AI辅助编程教育中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创意编程社区中，基于现有示例学习编程是一种常见做法，但新手在理解现有代码工作原理以及通过编写代码扩展实现自己的想法方面面临挑战。现有工具未能充分支持这两个过程，特别是结合AI辅助的"氛围编码"方式。

Method: 设计并迭代了Flowcode编程环境，该环境集成了两个核心功能：1) 流程图可视化代码结构，帮助理解现有代码；2) 聊天界面支持学习编码。通过两项用户研究，与新手创意编程者进行迭代设计改进。

Result: 通过两项研究迭代改进了Flowcode的设计，探索了可视化（流程图）和摩擦（friction）在促进AI在编程教育中有效使用方面的作用。

Conclusion: 可视化和适度的摩擦（而非完全自动化）在AI辅助编程学习中具有重要价值，特别是在创意编程社区中帮助新手理解代码结构和学习编码实践。

Abstract: Building upon found examples is a popular way people learn to code, especially in creative coding communities where sharing projects and remixing are common practices. But effectively doing so requires being able to 1) understand how existing code works, and 2) extend it by writing code that implements your own ideas, practices that can be challenging for new creative coders. We explored how to support these two processes through the design of Flowcode, a creative coding programming environment that integrates a flowchart for visualizing code structure and a chat interface tailored to support learning to code over vibe coding. We share how we iterated on the design of Flowcode over two studies with new creative coders, reflecting on the roles visualization and friction may play in enabling productive AI-use in computing education.

</details>


### [113] [Creating a Mixed-Reality Installation with Families through Theatrical Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06754)
*Pavlos Panagiotidis,Roma Patel,Boriana Koleva,Steve Benford,Jocelyn Spence,Paul Tennent,Juan Pablo Martinez Avila,Laurence Cliffe*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项通过应用戏剧引导的家庭协同设计项目，最终产出全国巡展的混合现实装置。研究展示了戏剧化协同设计如何通过游戏性地位转换重新平衡儿童与成人的参与，以及如何将工作坊的动态关系重新编排到公共混合现实装置中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭协同环境可持续设计中存在两大挑战：一是家庭角色固化导致参与不均（尤其是成人与幼童之间），二是工作坊中的关系和互动方式难以延续到最终设计成品中。

Method: 采用应用戏剧引导的协同设计方法，组织三个工作坊和用户测试，家庭共同创作叙事、物品和互动角色，最终产出全国巡展的混合现实装置。

Result: 戏剧化协同设计通过游戏性地位转换重新平衡了儿童与成人之间的参与度；工作坊中的动态关系可以被重新编排并保留在公共混合现实装置中。

Conclusion: 提出参与式设计的戏剧化视角：设计师不仅处理物品和想法，还处理角色、节奏、权威以及支持集体想象的社会条件。

Abstract: Co-designing with families for environmental sustainability relies on participatory imagination, yet habitual family roles and uneven participation, especially between adults and young children, often constrain it. A second challenge is continuity: workshop relationships and embodied ways of working do not easily survive into the final design, where artefacts travel more readily than roles or interactional dynamics. We report on a nationally toured mixed-reality installation developed through applied-theatre-led co-design with families. Across three workshops and user testing, applied theatre methods supported families to co-create narratives, artefacts, and interactional roles that shaped the public event. We show how theatrical co-design can rebalance child-adult participation through playful status shifts, and how selected workshop dynamics can be re-staged within a public mixed-reality installation. We contribute a theatrical account of participatory design in which designers work not only with artefacts and ideas, but with roles, rhythms, authority, and the social conditions that support collective imagination.

</details>


### [114] [Devising Interactive Spaces: A Rehearsal-Oriented Tool for Creating Responsive Environments for Immersive Theatre](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06761)
*Pavlos Panagiotidis,Paul Tennent,Jocelyn Spence,Nils Jaeger*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一套彩排导向的系统，用于在戏剧创作工作坊中构建响应式演出环境。系统通过可视化无代码编程层，将定制传感模块（手势、位置、语音识别）与灯光、声音输出连接。该系统在八位专业表演者的六次工作坊中被开发、使用和完善，参与者创作了光影乐谱、手势/位置触发场景、响应式架构、参与式原型及多房间即兴表演。系统支持快速配置、可视化映射和室内测试，让表演者能以最少技术支持实验响应式空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在让传感与驱动技术作为作曲材料，在早期创意实验阶段即可用于沉浸式戏剧创作，而非提供生产级别的演出现场控制平台。

Method: 开发了一套由定制手势、位置、语音识别传感模块、灯光声音输出模块以及可视化无代码编程层组成的系统，通过六次工作坊迭代优化，支持快速配置、可视化映射和室内测试。

Result: 在八位专业表演者的六次工作坊中，参与者成功创作了光影乐谱、手势/位置触发场景、响应式架构、参与式原型及多房间即兴表演等多种响应式戏剧作品。

Conclusion: 该系统证明了将传感与驱动技术作为早期创意实验的作曲材料是可行的，其快速配置、可视化映射和最小技术支持的设计原则有效地将交互式传感融入了具身表演创作中。

Abstract: We present a rehearsal-oriented system for creating responsive built environments during theatre devising workshops. The system connects bespoke sensing modules for gesture, position, and speech recognition to light and sound outputs through a visual no-code programming layer. It was developed, used, and refined across six workshops with eight professional performance-makers, where participants created light-and-sound scores, gesture- and position-triggered scenes, responsive architectures, participatory prototypes, and a multi-room scratch performance. Rather than presenting a production-ready show-control platform, this demo focuses on how sensing and actuation can be made available as compositional materials during early-stage creative experimentation for immersive theatrical compositions. The system is designed to support quick configuration, visible mappings, and in-room testing, allowing performers to experiment with responsive spaces with minimal technical support. We describe the system architecture, its workshop use, and the practical conditions that helped integrate interactive sensing into embodied performance-making.

</details>


### [115] [Head, Gaze, or Finger? Comparing Object Selection Techniques in Augmented Reality for People with Low Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06778)
*Ruijia Chen,Tianyi Zhang,Sanbrita Mondal,Yukang Yan,Yuhang Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对20名低视力者和18名正常视力者进行混合方法研究，评估了在AR中三种目标选择技术（头部、注视和手指指向+停留确认）的性能与偏好。发现注视选择在坐姿时初始指向最快，头部选择最稳定且心理负荷最小，而中心视力损失者偏好手指选择以获得更强控制感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低视力者（PLV）在AR中通过基于选择的空间增强可以灵活获取相关信息并减少视觉混乱，但PLV因视觉和注视控制障碍，其在AR中进行目标选择的能力和偏好尚不清楚，缺乏适用于PLV的AR选择技术实证依据。

Method: 采用混合方法（定量+定性），招募20名低视力者和18名正常视力对照，在两种真实场景（坐姿和移动中）下，使用三种输入技术（头部指向、注视指向、手指指向+停留确认）执行目标选择任务，测量任务完成时间、初始指向时间、稳定性、心理负荷等指标，并结合主观偏好分析。

Result: 1) 注视选择在坐姿时初始指向最快，总体选择时间与头部选择相当；2) 头部选择在两种场景下最稳定且心理负荷最小；3) 中心视力损失者独特地偏好手指选择，报告更强的控制感；4) PLV的注视稳定性降低影响了注视选择的表现。

Conclusion: 头部选择对PLV而言最可靠稳定，注视选择在静态场景有速度优势但受注视稳定性影响，手指选择对特定视力障碍类型（如中心视力损失）具有独特价值。研究为PLV在AR中的可访问交互技术和基于选择的视觉增强提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Augmented reality (AR) can enhance visual perception for people with low vision (PLV) by overlaying multimodal information. Selection-based augmentation further allows users to flexibly choose and augment relevant information while reducing distraction and visual clutter. However, little is known about the ability and preferences of PLV in performing object selection techniques in AR, considering their potential visual and gaze control challenges. To understand what selection techniques are suitable for PLV to support selection-based AR augmentations, we conducted a mixed-methods study with 20 PLV and 18 sighted controls who performed target selection tasks using three input techniques -- head, gaze, and finger pointing with dwell-based confirmation -- in two real-world scenarios (sitting vs. on the go). We found that for PLV, gaze-based selection enabled the fastest initial pointing when sitting and comparable overall selection time to head-based selection in both scenarios; however, due to reduced gaze stability, head-based selection remained the most stable and the least mentally demanding. Uniquely, participants with central vision loss preferred finger-based selection, reporting a greater sense of control. Our results provide empirical insights into accessible AR interaction techniques and selection-based vision enhancements for PLV.

</details>


### [116] [New Cross-Sensory Approach to Designing Restorative Virtual Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06901)
*Rachel Masters,Francisco Ortega*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了虚拟现实自然环境中跨感官交互（如视觉与听觉）对压力缓解的影响，指出现有研究多将感官视为叠加效应，但实际存在更复杂的交叉感官作用，例如不协调的自然声音可能削弱压力恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助无法接触真实自然的人群通过VR自然场景缓解压力和恢复注意力，同时揭示当前多感官VR自然环境中被忽视的跨感官交互问题。

Method: 综述当前文献中关于虚拟森林视觉设计及多感官（尤其是视听）叠加实验的研究结果，分析其中矛盾现象（如声音不协调导致威胁感），指出跨感官交互的存在。

Result: 发现添加声音可增强压力缓解效果，但某些自然声音若与环境不匹配反而会产生威胁感；跨感官交互在VR自然环境中尚未被充分探索，挑战了现有的多感官叠加认知。

Conclusion: 未来需系统研究VR自然环境中的跨感官交互机制，以优化设计实现更有效的压力缓解效果。

Abstract: Virtual reality (VR) nature immersion is an increasingly popular field of research due to its potential to help people who do not have access to real nature. There are many questions surrounding how virtual forests can be designed to effectively reduce stress and restore attention. Many of these questions relate solely to visual aspects, but more recent literature has started exploring multisensory experiences. In these experiences, senses are treated as additive; however, certain results from the current literature may indicate that there are more complex, cross-sensory interactions occurring. For example, adding sound to visuals can increase stress reduction potential, but certain natural sounds can feel threatening if they are out of place within the virtual nature scene. Overall, cross-sensory interactions in VR nature environments (VNEs) are underexplored and challenge our current understanding of multisensory VNEs, and future explorations of these interactions are essential for designing optimal VNEs for stress reduction.

</details>


### [117] [Multimodal Smart Glove for Sign Language Recognition Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06996)
*Anh Thu Nguyen Ngoc,Tam Phong Truong,Thai Anh Nguyen Duong,Vu Linh Nguyen,Manh Duong Phung*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合可穿戴传感（柔性传感器+IMU）与深度学习（LSTM）的智能手套系统，用于手语识别，通过ESP32-C6传输数据并转换为TensorFlow Lite实现实时推理，整体识别准确率约95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语识别系统在实际部署中面临挑战，为改善聋哑人士与更广泛社区的沟通，需要一种可部署的、实时的解决方案。

Method: 智能手套集成柔性传感器和惯性测量单元（IMU）捕捉手指关节运动和手部动作，同时通过摄像头获取面部线索；传感器数据经ESP32-C6微控制器传输，使用长短期记忆（LSTM）网络对时序手势动态建模，训练后模型转换为TensorFlow Lite格式用于实时推理。

Result: 提出的模型整体识别准确率约为95%。

Conclusion: 该系统在实际手语翻译应用中具有可行性，为可部署的手语识别方案提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Sign language recognition technologies can improve communication between deaf individuals and the broader community, but many existing systems face challenges in real-world deployment. This paper presents a deployable smart glove system for sign language recognition that integrates wearable sensing and deep learning. The glove incorporates flex sensors and an inertial measurement unit (IMU) to capture finger articulation and hand motion, while facial cues are obtained through a camera. Sensor data are transmitted via an ESP32-C6 microcontroller and processed using a long short-term memory (LSTM) network to model temporal gesture dynamics. Experimental results show that the proposed model achieves an overall recognition accuracy of approximately 95%. The trained model is further converted to TensorFlow Lite for real-time inference. This demonstrates the feasibility of the system for practical sign language translation applications.

</details>


### [118] [ShapeTalk: Combining Natural Language and Sketch for Time-Series Pattern Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07073)
*Guoruizhe Sun,Yueqiao Chen,Emily Guo,Yutong Yao,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ShapeTalk 是一个结合自然语言和草图的双模态查询系统，用于单变量时间序列的模式搜索。自然语言支持语义化描述，草图支持几何精化，二者通过共享视觉上下文和可编辑特征表示协同工作，核心是基于LLM的语义解析管道，用户研究验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可视化查询工具难以支持模糊、复合或非精确的时间序列模式描述，通常需要用户通过精确草图或严格的结构化过滤器来表达意图，限制了查询的灵活性和可用性。

Method: ShapeTalk 采用协调的自然语言与草图双模态查询方式，两者作为分析意图的互补表示；核心是基于LLM的语义解析管道，将自由形式的自然语言查询翻译为可解释和可编辑的形状特征约束；通过共享视觉上下文、可编辑特征表示和同步结果视图连接两种模态。

Result: 通过两个使用场景、用户研究（含失败案例分析）以及LLM语义解析管道的评估，证明了ShapeTalk支持有效的时间序列模式搜索，自然语言作为易用的入口，草图为文本规格不足时提供补充精化和恢复机制。

Conclusion: 自然语言与草图作为互补模态能够有效支持时间序列模式搜索，LLM驱动的语义解析是可解释且可编辑的，双模态设计提升了查询的灵活性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Searching for time-series segments that match user-defined patterns is important in domains such as finance, climate science, and healthcare. However, existing visual query tools often struggle to support vague, composite, or fuzzy pattern descriptions, often requiring users to express their intent through precise sketches or rigid structured filters. We present ShapeTalk, a coordinated natural-language and sketch-based querying system for univariate time-series pattern search. Rather than treating text and sketch as a fused input stream, ShapeTalk uses them as complementary representations of analytic intent: natural language supports semantic and compositional pattern descriptions, while sketching supports direct geometric refinement. The two modalities are linked through a shared visual context, editable feature representations, and synchronized result views, enabling users to move between text and sketch during iterative query formulation. At its core is an LLM-based semantic parsing pipeline that translates free-form natural-language queries into interpretable and editable shape-feature constraints. We evaluate ShapeTalk through two usage scenarios, a user study with failure-case analysis, and an assessment of the LLM-based semantic parsing pipeline. The results show that ShapeTalk supports effective time-series pattern search, with natural language serving as an accessible entry point and sketching providing a complementary mechanism for refinement and recovery when textual specifications are insufficient.

</details>


### [119] [CompoVista: A Composition-Graph-Based Visual Analytics System for Compositional Analysis of Traditional Chinese Paintings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07105)
*Dekun Qian,Ruiqi Yu,Fengling Zheng,Li Ye,Yize Li,Weigui Zheng,Yigang Wang,Jinchang Li,Zhiguang Zhou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于场景图的构图表示方法（Composition Graph）和视觉分析系统（CompoVista），支持艺术史学者在大规模中国画数据集上进行构图驱动的探索、模式发现与证据追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统中国画构图分析以定性、解读驱动为主，难以在大规模画作集合中发现、比较和验证构图模式。为弥合这一方法论缺口，作者进行了文献综述和艺术史学家深度访谈，明确了系统化构图分析的需求。

Method: 提出Composition Graph，通过实体、关系、留白和语境四个层次建模画作构图；在此基础上开发CompoVista视觉分析系统，支持格式感知的画作队列构建、视觉查询与上下文查询、跨队列构图比较及聚合模式到单画的证据追溯。

Result: 通过12名领域用户的基于任务用户研究、两项案例研究和专家访谈，验证了CompoVista在队列构建、模式发现、迭代细化和证据检查方面的有效性；同时揭示了结果解释清晰度、模糊查询和历史管理等方面的未来需求。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了构图特定的结构化表示（Composition Graph）和集成的视觉分析工作流，实现了在大规模中国画收藏中开展构图驱动的系统性研究。

Abstract: Composition in Traditional Chinese Paintings (TCPs) carries spatial, narrative, and cultural-aesthetic meaning. Systematic compositional analysis is therefore important for understanding their visual language and artistic meaning. Traditional compositional analysis is mainly qualitative and interpretation-driven. It supports close reading of individual paintings, but it is difficult to discover, compare, and verify compositional patterns across large painting collections. To better understand these challenges, we conducted a literature review and in-depth interviews with two art historians. Based on these findings, we introduce the Composition Graph, a scene-graph-based representation for TCP composition. It models a painting through four layers: entities, relations, void space, and context. Based on this representation, we develop CompoVista, a canvas-based visual analytics system for composition-oriented exploration of TCPs. CompoVista allows art historians to construct and revise format-aware painting cohorts through visual queries and context queries. It also supports cohort-level inspection of entity distributions and relations, comparison of compositional differences across cohorts, and tracing aggregate patterns back to painting-level evidence.We evaluated CompoVista through a task-based user study with 12 domain participants, two case studies, and expert interviews. The results show that CompoVista supports composition-oriented cohort construction, pattern discovery, iterative refinement, and evidence inspection. The evaluation also reveals future needs, including clearer result explanations, fuzzier composition queries, and stronger exploration history management. Our work contributes a composition-specific structured representation and an integrated visual analytics workflow for studying TCP composition at collection scale.

</details>


### [120] [Bringing robustness to end-user programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07116)
*Mickaël Baron,Patrick Girard*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出GenBuild，一种将端用户编程与安全开发相结合的双模块环境，通过领域专家构建安全功能核心，端用户在此基础上进行交互式应用设计，并验证部分属性以实现安全的端用户编程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端用户编程工具缺乏安全性保障，而传统的安全开发技术（如软件工程、形式化方法）又过于复杂，远离端用户。作者希望将安全性交由专家处理，并将端用户编程连接到安全的传统开发流程上。

Method: 构建GenBuild环境，包含两个模块：Generator（供领域专家使用，用于设定安全的功能核心）和Builder（供端用户使用，是基于现有功能核心的纯交互式工具，允许开发完整交互式应用并验证部分属性）。

Result: 成功构建了GenBuild环境，端用户可在安全功能核心基础上设计独立交互式应用，并能够验证部分属性，这是迈向安全端用户编程的第一步。

Conclusion: 通过将安全性责任交由领域专家，端用户可在受限但安全的环境中进行编程，验证部分属性为实现安全的端用户编程提供了可行路径。

Abstract: In some cases, end-user programming allows the design of stand-alone applications. But none of the existing approaches is concerned by safety aspects of programming. Heavy techniques exist to develop safe applications, particularly in non-interactive domains. They involve software engineering techniques, and sometimes, formal methods. All these techniques are very far from end-users. Our idea is to let this part to experts, and to connect end-user programming onto this safe conventional development. Starting from an existing functional core, we built an interactive end-user programming environment called GenBuild, which allows designing interactive stand-alone applications. GenBuild is composed of two distinct modules. The Generator is the first one. It is a specialized tool developed for a domain expert who sets out a safe functional core. The Builder is the second module. It is a purely interactive tool that allows an end-user to develop some complete interactive application among an existing functional core. It allows the verification of some properties that are a first step towards the development of safe end-user programming.

</details>


### [121] [Clinical Translation of Brain-Computer Interface in China: A Landscape Analysis of Investigator-Initiated Trials, Registered Clinical Trials, and Regulatory Approval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07185)
*Long Chen,Wanyi Qing,Lifen Mo,Xiaoke Chai,Wenting Li,Fengyan Liang,Ming Yin,Yi Yang,Jizong Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文首次定量分析了中国脑机接口（BCI）转化生态系统，整合了研究者发起的试验（IITs）、注册临床试验和监管批准产品三个维度。截至2026年6月，共分析了134项注册临床试验、26项IITs和5个NMPA批准的BCI产品。结果显示，自2020年以来临床试验注册快速增长，研究中心集中在广东、上海和江苏；非侵入式系统占主导（79.1%），脑卒中康复是主要适应症（65.0%）；批准的5个产品包括全球首个半侵入式植入BCI、侵入式闭环DBS系统及三个非侵入式脑电康复系统。尽管中国BCI转化管道快速扩展，但长期植入稳定性、临床基础设施标准化和解码算法泛化性仍是关键障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑损伤影响数亿人口，现有疗法难以逆转运动或沟通功能丧失。脑机接口（BCI）为恢复这些功能提供了有前景的途径，但缺乏对中国BCI从早期临床研究到监管批准的完整转化生态的定量分析。

Method: 整合三个维度的证据：研究者发起的试验（IITs）、中国临床试验注册中心（ChiCTR）的注册临床试验（134项）、以及NMPA批准的BCI相关产品（5个），数据截止至2026年6月。

Result: 临床试验注册自2020年以来快速增长；研究中心集中于广东、上海、江苏；非侵入式系统占79.1%；脑卒中康复占65.0%；5个产品获批，包括全球首个半侵入式植入BCI、侵入式闭环DBS系统及三个非侵入式EEG康复系统。

Conclusion: 中国BCI转化管道快速扩展，已形成从早期临床研究到监管批准的完整链条。但长期植入稳定性、临床基础设施和工作流标准化、解码算法泛化性仍是临床广泛采用的关键障碍，需解决这些挑战以将BCI技术整合到常规临床实践中。

Abstract: Neurological injury affects hundreds of millions of people worldwide, yet the loss of motor or communication functions resulting from stroke, spinal cord injury, and neurodegenerative disease remains largely irreversible with existing therapies. Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) offer a promising pathway for restoring these functions by decoding neural activity into commands that control an external device. Here, we present the first quantitative analysis of China's BCI translational ecosystem, integrating evidence from three pillars: investigator-initiated trials (IITs), registered clinical trials, and regulatory-approved products. We analyzed 134 clinical trials from the Chinese Clinical Trial Registry (ChiCTR), 26 IITs, and five BCI-related products approved by the National Medical Products Administration as of June 2026. Results demonstrate that clinical trial registration has increased rapidly since 2020, with research centers concentrated primarily in Guangdong, Shanghai, and Jiangsu. Non-invasive systems predominated, accounting for 79.1% of registered studies, with stroke rehabilitation as the leading indication (65.0%). As of June 2026, five BCI-related products received regulatory approvals, including the world's first approved semi-invasive implantable BCI, an invasive closed-loop deep brain stimulation system with real-time local field potential recording, and three non-invasive EEG-based rehabilitation systems. Collectively, these findings characterize a rapidly expanding BCI translational pipeline in China, spanning from early clinical research to regulatory approval. However, long-term implant stability, standardization of clinical infrastructure and workflows, and generalizability of decoding algorithms remain critical barriers to widespread clinical adoption. Addressing these challenges will be essential for integrating BCI technologies into routine clinical practice.

</details>


### [122] [Should We Dangle a Carrot? The Effect of Performance-based Incentives in Visualization Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07463)
*Abhraneel Sarma,Matthew Kay,Sheng Long,Michael Correll,Alexander Lex*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过两项众包实验研究了可视化研究中金钱激励对实验结果的影响，发现无论是对低层感知任务还是推理任务，提供绩效奖金并未显著影响任务表现，表明金钱激励可能并不总是具有预期的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可视化实验设计中存在许多主观决策（如是否提供培训、金钱激励等），但缺乏关于这些决策如何影响实验结果的指导，本文旨在探究金钱激励这一关键设计决策对实验结论的影响。

Method: 开展了两个众包研究，分别探究金钱激励对（1）低层感知任务（散点图/平行坐标图中的相关性感知）和（2）推理任务（基于区间图或密度图的天气预报决策）的影响。在每个研究中，视觉表征和激励条件均作为组间变量进行操作。

Result: 两项研究中均未发现金钱激励对任务表现有显著影响——无论是对低层感知任务还是推理任务，提供绩效奖金均未改变实验结果和结论。

Conclusion: 绩效导向的金钱激励可能并不总是对参与者产生预期的影响，这需要研究者反思如何设计激励性实验；同时提醒当前结论仅基于两项研究，需要进一步重复验证。

Abstract: A perennial research question in visualization involves identifying which visual encodings for a particular dataset are most effective for users in performing a specific task. The relative effectiveness of the different encodings are commonly identified through controlled experiments. However, designing an experiment involves making many, often ad hoc, decisions about the experimental setup such as whether to include a training module, whether to provide performance-based incentives to participants, etc. Yet, there is limited guidance on how these decisions should be made, and we do not fully understand the impact of these subjective decisions on empirical results. In this paper, we investigate the impact of one such key design decision: monetary rewards. Specifically, we ask: does providing or not providing participants with performance-based financial incentives affect the results and the conclusions that we draw from visualization studies? We conducted two crowdsourced studies investigating the impact of incentives on (i) a low-level, perceptual task (perception of correlations in scatterplots or parallel coordinate plots), and (ii) a task involving reasoning (decision-making based on a weather forecast represented as intervals or density plots). In each of these studies, we manipulate both the visual representation and the presence of incentives as between-subject conditions. We expected to find no effect of incentives on the perceptual task, but to see an effect for the decision-making task. However, we found no effect on task performance in either study. While these are results of only two studies and should be replicated, they suggest that performance-based financial incentives may not always have the intended effect on participants that we presumed, and calls for a reflection of how incentivized studies should be designed.

</details>


### [123] [Creativity from Friction: Human-AI Interaction for Exploratory Structural Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07521)
*Ricardo Maia Avelino,Rita Sevastjanova,Tom Van Mele,Philippe Block,Mennatallah El-Assady*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了当前生成式AI追求消除摩擦、提供最终答案的目标与结构设计等创意领域需求之间的错位，提出了基于视觉-语言模型的约束性人机协同创建设计维度，并通过原型界面和专家研究展示了如何通过减少重复建模摩擦同时保留反思性设计摩擦来支持设计空间探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI直接生成最终答案的模式不适合创意领域（如结构设计），这些领域需要交互式系统帮助用户外化想法、探索替代方案和完善部分解。创意设计需要遵守空间配置、力学行为、材料用量和成本等多重约束，而这些约束产生的摩擦实际上能激发新的创造性解决方案。

Method: 提出了支持约束性人机协同创建的若干设计维度，利用视觉-语言模型使结构探索变得对话式、多模态，并能响应不断演化的人类意图。基于这些原则构建了试点设计界面，并开展了领域专家研究。

Result: 通过专家研究，展示了结构设计师如何感知交互式AI系统，以及这类系统如何通过减少重复性建模摩擦同时保留反思性设计摩擦来支持设计空间探索。

Conclusion: 生成式AI在设计领域的正确角色不应是消除所有摩擦，而应区分对待：减少重复性、机械性的建模摩擦，同时保留能激发创意的反思性设计摩擦。视觉-语言模型为实现这种有约束的人机协同创作提供了可行路径。

Abstract: AI agents that generate final answers based on user input often do not meet the needs of creative fields. Fields such as structural design and architecture need interactive systems that help users externalise and develop ideas, explore alternatives, and refine partial solutions. The final product of such designs needs to comply with many constraints concerning, e.g., spatial configuration, mechanical behaviour, material quantities, and costs. These constraints create friction in the design process, which can stimulate novel and creative solutions. In this paper, we discuss the misalignment between current generative AI goals to remove friction and provide final solutions and the needs of creators, such as structural designers, who develop ideas through iterative work. We present the design dimensions of systems allowing for constrained human-AI co-creation that rely on vision-language models making structural exploration conversational, multimodal, and responsive to evolving human intent in ways that follow and augment the discipline's creative process. Through a pilot design interface based on these principles and a study with experts in the field, this paper shows how structural designers perceive interactive AI systems and how such systems can support design space exploration by reducing repetitive modelling friction while preserving reflective design friction.

</details>


### [124] [Two-player Alternate Uses Test: A Controlled Testbed for Interactive Human-AI and Human-Human Co-Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07522)
*Babak Hemmatian,Anita Keshmirian,Yijun Lin,Shravan Ramamoorthy,Maryam Jahadakbar,Eli Khuri-Reid,Jingtong Wang,Sarah Hadjarab,Sindre Veum,Pranav Gupta,Deepak Somaya,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了交替用途测试（AUT）的双人交互扩展平台，在受控条件下比较人类-人类与人类-AI共同创作。通过62人实验发现，GPT-4伙伴与人类伙伴在原创性上统计等价，且个体特质（BAS Drive、认知外包）对合作效果有调节作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI创意研究要么使用独立智能体对比（缺乏生态效度），要么进行人机协作的实地研究（牺牲实验控制），缺乏既能保持实验控制又能真实比较人类-人类与人类-AI协作的研究方法。

Method: 设计了交替用途测试（AUT）的双人交互扩展版本，支持受控条件下比较人类-人类与人类-AI共同创作，并设置校准的非交互基线。平台将绩效分解为参与者特质、伙伴感知和内容动态三个通常混淆的因素。进行了62人线下实验验证。

Result: 在匹配时间限制下，GPT-4伙伴与人类伙伴在原创性上统计等价；BAS Drive（趋近动机）调节交互式伙伴关系对原创性的影响；自我报告的认知外包仅在人-人二元组中预测更低的原创性；预先接触高创意想法可提升后续表现。

Conclusion: 该平台为受控的人机共创研究提供了共享测试平台，揭示了人机协作中个体差异和交互动态的关键作用，并展示了"种子干预"策略的潜力。

Abstract: Controlled research on AI ideation typically compares independent agents, while field studies of human-AI collaboration sacrifice experimental control. We introduce a controlled, two-player extension of the Alternate Uses Test (AUT) that enables comparison of human-human and human-AI co-creation under matched interactive conditions, alongside calibrated non-interactive baselines. The platform supports decomposition of performance into three typically confounded factors: participant traits, partner perceptions, and content dynamics. An in-person pilot (N = 62) demonstrates its utility. Under matched time limits, originality with a GPT-4 partner is statistically equivalent to that with a human partner. Approach motivation (BAS Drive) moderates whether interactive partnership benefits originality, and self-reported cognitive outsourcing predicts lower originality specifically in human-human dyads. Prior exposure to highly creative ideas improves later performance, suggesting a "seeding" intervention. We release the platform, code, and dataset as a shared testbed for controlled studies of human-AI co-creation.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [TriRoute: Unified Learned Routing for Joint Adaptive Attention, Experts, and KV-Cache Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06601)
*Andrii Balashov,Olena Ponomarova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TriRoute提出了一种轻量级控制器，联合决策注意力模式、FFN专家选择和KV缓存位宽三个维度，在同等推理成本下优于独立组合MoD+MoE+KV量化的方法，并能保持对稀有实体的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的条件计算方法（MoE、MoD、KV缓存量化）各自独立地在单一维度上优化，但注意力分辨率、专家选择和缓存位宽这三个决策是强耦合的，需要联合优化。

Method: 提出TriRoute，一个共享轻量级控制器，对每个token每层联合输出三种策略：注意力模式（跳过/局部/全局）、稀疏FFN专家集（含空专家恢复MoD）和KV缓存位宽。使用异质松弛方法（Gumbel-Softmax+直通估计用于分类决策，负载均衡top-k门控用于专家）进行端到端训练，并引入拉格朗日预算约束。针对跨轴路由崩塌问题，提出逐轴归一化和耦合感知均衡损失。

Result: 在160M到1.3B参数的解码器模型上，TriRoute在相同推理FLOPs和内存下帕累托占优于独立MoD+MoE+KV量化组合，更好地保留了稀有实体、代码和算术上的尾部鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 联合路由决策能更有效地分配计算资源，控制器学到的策略具有可解释性：将全注意力和高精度缓存分配给句首位置、稀有子词和命名实体，而功能词则使用廉价路由。

Abstract: Conditional computation can decouple language model quality from per-token inference cost, yet leading techniques act on a single axis in isolation: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) sparsifies the FFN, Mixture-of-Depths (MoD) skips whole transformer blocks, and KV-cache quantization compresses attention memory. We argue these three decisions (attention resolution, expert selection, and cache bit-width) are strongly coupled and should be made jointly: a token rare enough to warrant full attention may also need high-precision caching regardless of which expert processes it. We introduce TriRoute, a single lightweight controller shared across all three axes that, for every token at every layer, emits a coordinated policy: (i) an attention mode (skip/local/full), (ii) a sparse set of FFN experts (with a null expert recovering MoD), and (iii) a KV-cache bit-width. The controller trains end-to-end via a heterogeneous relaxation (Gumbel-Softmax with straight-through estimation for categorical decisions and load-balanced top-k gating for experts) under a Lagrangian budget constraint that turns the average compute and memory cost into a controllable knob. We identify a cross-axis routing-collapse cascade in naive joint training, where collapse on one axis propagates to the others, and address it with per-axis normalization and a coupling-aware balancing loss. On decoder-only models from 160M to 1.3B parameters at compute-optimal token counts, TriRoute Pareto-dominates the best independent MoD+MoE+KV-quantization combination at matched inference FLOPs and memory, while better preserving tail-case robustness on rare entities, code, and arithmetic that pure perplexity optimization erodes. Post-hoc analysis reveals interpretable structure: the controller allocates full attention and high-precision cache to sentence-initial positions, rare subwords, and named entities, while cheaply routing function words.

</details>


### [126] [A Quiet Failure in Calibrated Virtual Screening: Marginal Conformal Prediction Under-Covers the Minority Class, and a Class-Conditional Fix Recovers It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06605)
*Muhammadjon Tursunbadalov,Mustafojon Tursunbadalov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了标准共形预测在药物发现中处理不平衡数据集时，对 minority 类别的覆盖存在严重且隐蔽的失效问题，并提出 class-conditional (Mondrian) 共形预测作为实用解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测被用于药物发现中以提供可靠的模型不确定性估计，但在高度不平衡的药物发现数据集中，标准（边际）共形预测对少数类的覆盖存在严重不足，且这种失效在总体指标上不易察觉，构成了实际应用中的安全隐患。

Method: 在四个药物发现数据集上，使用随机森林、图神经网络和化学语言模型三种模型，分别评估标准共形预测和 class-conditional (Mondrian) 共形预测的类别覆盖性能；提出守恒恒等式解释 minority 覆盖缺口与 imbalance ratio 的关系；通过 scaffold 划分、替代共形分数等实验验证失效的鲁棒性；利用成本模型评估实用影响。

Result: 标准共形预测在全局90%覆盖率达标的同时，minority 覆盖率在血脑屏障渗透数据集上降至64.8%，在临床试验毒性数据集上仅4.2%；三种模型均重现这一失效（p<0.001）；守恒恒等式预测的覆盖缺口与实际测量误差在1个百分点以内；Mondrian 共形预测在所有数据集上恢复 minority 覆盖率至目标水平，仅以预测集大小适度增加为代价；失效集中在苯环、吡啶等通用分子 scaffold 上；成本模型显示对受影响化合物进行 abstention 可将筛选项目从负效用转为正效用。

Conclusion: 标准共形预测在不平衡药物发现数据集中对少数类的覆盖失效是严重且隐蔽的，实际应用中必须采用 class-conditional (Mondrian) 共形预测来保证每类别的可靠性，并提出了诊断方法和实用协议。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is being adopted in drug discovery to put an honest number on model reliability: pick an error rate alpha, and the method returns prediction sets containing the true label with probability at least 1 - alpha. We show this guarantee can be dangerous on imbalanced datasets. Across four datasets, standard (marginal) conformal prediction hits its global 90% coverage target while leaving the minority class badly exposed: realized minority coverage falls to 64.8% on blood-brain-barrier penetration and to 4.2% on clinical-trial toxicity, where the rare class is nearly abandoned. The failure is not tied to one model: a random forest, a graph network, and a frozen chemical language model all reproduce it (p < 0.001 in every case), with severity tracking baseline calibration on rare labels rather than architecture. A conservation identity explains the effect: the minority's shortfall equals the majority's surplus amplified by the imbalance ratio, predicting the measured gap to within one point and ordering severity across datasets. The failure survives realistic scaffold splits and a second conformal score, while aggregate accuracy and overall coverage stay reassuringly high, which is exactly why it is easy to miss. Class-conditional (Mondrian) conformal prediction closes the gap on every dataset, restoring minority coverage to target for a modest increase in prediction-set size. We localize the failures to generic molecular scaffolds - plain benzene and pyridine cores occurring in both classes - propose a one-number diagnostic, and show with a cost model that abstaining on affected compounds flips a screening campaign from net-negative to net-positive utility. Our contribution is demonstrating on real chemistry how severe and invisible this known conformal-theory gap becomes under imbalance, and laying out a practical protocol restoring per-class reliability.

</details>


### [127] [NEST: Tackling Dataset-Level Distribution Shifts via Regime-Oriented Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06607)
*Lanhao Li,Bingshu Xie,Lijun Sun,Xin Xue,Haoyi Zhou,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NEST框架，通过两阶段密集MoE架构建模和重组复杂系统中的动态多元时间序列，利用无监督聚类在矩-熵空间划分运行机制，引入面向机制的router和几何调制，实现跨机制分布位移下的长期预测，在异构网络流量和物理现象等基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注局部时间位移，未能显式建模数据集由多个不同运行机制复合而成的全局结构挑战，导致长期预测在数据集级分布位移下性能不佳。

Method: 提出NEST框架：(1) 通过无监督聚类在矩-熵空间将数据集划分为不同运行机制；(2) 设计面向机制的router，基于时序内容生成初始专家权重，并通过几何调制向机制质心精炼；(3) 个体专家作为专用核函数，通过演化独特的变量注意力模式捕获机制特定动力学。

Result: 在异构网络流量和物理现象等多种基准上，NEST一致实现最先进的预测性能。

Conclusion: NEST通过显式建模和重组数据集中的不同运行机制，有效解决了长期预测中的分布位移问题，验证了结构专业化与机制重组合方法的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate long-term forecasting in complex systems is frequently compromised by dataset-level distribution shifts, where diverse underlying behavioral modes and evolving system states drive the dynamic multivariate time-series. While existing methods predominantly focus on local temporal shifts, they fail to explicitly model the global structural challenge where datasets are composites of distinct operational regimes. In this paper, we propose NEST, a specialized framework designed to model and recompose these evolving structures through a two-phase dense MoE architecture. NEST first facilitates structural specialization by partitioning the dataset into distinct operational regimes through unsupervised clustering in a principled moment-entropy space. We introduce a regime-oriented router mechanism that generates initial expert weights based on temporal content, subsequently refined through geometric modulation to regime centroids. Crucially, rather than acting as monolithic predictors, individual experts function as specialized kernels that capture regime-specific dynamics by evolving unique variate-attention patterns. Extensive evaluations on diverse benchmarks, including heterogeneous network traffic and physical phenomena, demonstrate that NEST consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/Aaralshin/NEST

</details>


### [128] [D2PO: Optimizing Diffusion Samplers via Dynamic Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06609)
*Jinkyu Kim,Jinyoung Choi,Bohyung Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: D2PO提出了一种基于动态直接偏好优化的框架，通过将扩散采样策略建模为能量模型，利用偏好对齐替代传统回归方法，在低NFE条件下实现了更优的感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学生-教师回归框架中，低NFE学生采样器被训练去模仿高NFE教师，导致在保留粗粒度全局结构的同时牺牲了高频纹理保真度，使采样器与感知质量不对齐。

Method: 将采样策略建模为能量模型(EBM)，利用DPO框架将偏好比较转化为可处理的能量差异；提出从预训练分数网络导出的新能量公式，在扰动空间中联合捕获结构一致性与细粒度细节；引入动态偏好机制，偏好样本随策略学习迭代改进。

Result: D2PO在低NFE约束下比传统回归调度器更忠实对齐感知质量，充分发挥高质量教师模型的潜力，持续优于传统方法。

Conclusion: D2PO通过偏好引导的迭代自改进机制替代刚性静态教师监督，为扩散采样策略优化提供了更有效的对齐信号。

Abstract: We propose D2PO (Dynamic Direct Preference Optimization), a principled framework for optimizing diffusion sampling policies with respect to timestep schedules and classifier-free guidance (CFG) weights. Our work is motivated by a fundamental limitation of existing student-teacher regression frameworks; low-NFE student samplers are trained to mimic high-NFEteachers, often sacrificing high-frequency texture fidelity while preserving coarse global structures, thereby misaligning the sampler with perceptual quality. D2PO addresses this challenge by reformulating sampler optimization as a preference-based alignment problem, leveraging the Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) framework. To make DPO applicable to diffusion samplers, we model the sampling policy as an energy-based model (EBM), transforming preference comparisons into tractable energy differences. We further introduce a novel energy formulation derived directly from the pretrained score network, enabling preference evaluation in perturbed spaces that jointly capture structural consistency and fine-grained details. Moreover, we introduce dynamic preferences, where the preferred samples used for alignment progressively improve as the sampling policies are learned. This self-improving mechanism replaces rigid static teacher supervision with an iterative, preference-guided refinement process, providing progressively stronger alignment signals. Extensive experiments demonstrate that D2PO aligns diffusion samplers with perceptual quality more faithfully, unlocking the full potential of high-quality teachers and consistently outperforming conventional regression-based schedulers under low-NFE constraints.

</details>


### [129] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Reliability Based Bi-Objective Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06610)
*Sounaq Das,Tanmay Sen,Raghu Nandan Sengupta,Aditya Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于深度强化学习的多目标可靠性投资组合优化框架（MORP-DRL），结合GARCH、极值理论和t-copula建模不确定性，使用PPO算法在交易成本等约束下优化收益与三种下行风险指标，在十种全球股指上验证了其优于传统方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可靠性的投资组合优化方法大多采用静态优化框架，未能捕捉序贯决策、尾部风险以及交易成本等市场摩擦，亟需一种能够处理多目标、动态决策的鲁棒优化方法。

Method: 构建深度强化学习框架，联合优化期望收益与方差、CVaR、EVaR三种下行风险指标；使用GARCH(1,1)、极值理论和t-copula对资产收益建模，通过拟蒙特卡洛模拟生成场景；基于PPO算法在交易成本和投资组合边界约束下进行策略优化，与NSGA-II进行对比。

Result: 在COVID前、中、后三种市场环境下对十种全球股指进行实验，MORP-DRL实现了有竞争力的风险-收益表现，在市场压力期间降低了下行风险，并具备高维投资组合的可扩展性。

Conclusion: MORP-DRL有效克服了静态可靠性优化方法的局限，在动态多目标投资组合优化中具有优越性和实用性。

Abstract: Portfolio optimization under uncertainty is inherently a multi-objective decision problem involving complex interactions among return, risk, market dynamics, and practical investment constraints. Existing reliability based portfolio optimization approaches primarily rely on static optimization frameworks and often fail to capture sequential decision making, tail risk, and market frictions such as transaction costs. To address these limitations, we propose a deep reinforcement learning framework for multi-objective reliability based portfolio optimization (MORP-DRL). The proposed framework jointly optimizes expected return and downside risk using three complementary risk measures: variance, Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR), and Entropic Value-at-Risk (EVaR). To model uncertainty and heavy-tailed market behavior, asset returns are represented using GARCH(1,1), Extreme Value Theory, and a t-copula dependence structure, while realistic scenarios are generated through quasi-Monte Carlo simulation. A Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) based strategy is developed under practical constraints including transaction costs and portfolio bounds, and is benchmarked against NSGA-II. Experiments on ten global equity indices across pre-COVID, COVID, and post-COVID market regimes demonstrate that MORP-DRL achieves competitive risk-return performance, reduced downside risk during periods of market stress, and scalability to high-dimensional portfolio settings.

</details>


### [130] [STAGformer: A Spatio-temporal Agent Graph Transformer for Micro Mobility Demand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06614)
*Ye Zihao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAGformer提出了一种基于智能体注意力的时空图Transformer，通过可学习的空间和时间智能体令牌将自注意力计算复杂度从O(N²T²)降至O(NT)，在纽约Citi-Bike和芝加哥Divvy-Bike数据集上实现了超越现有方法的站点级共享单车需求预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享单车系统中站点级需求预测对运营效率至关重要，但由于复杂的时空依赖关系和城市网络的庞大规模，现有方法在全局建模时面临计算效率低下的问题（标准自注意力呈二次复杂度）。

Method: 提出STAGformer，核心创新为两阶段智能体注意力机制：首先通过少量可学习的空间和时间智能体令牌聚合全局信息，再将信息广播回各站点和时间步。模型包含四大模块：时空编码器（融合动态节点特征与天气、时间、兴趣点等外部因素）、图传播模块（空间邻居聚合）、时序卷积模块（局部模式提取）以及智能体注意力模块（全局依赖建模）。

Result: 在两个真实数据集（纽约Citi-Bike和芝加哥Divvy-Bike）上，STAGformer在多个预测周期上均一致优于现有最先进基线方法，在RMSE和MAE指标上取得显著提升。消融实验验证了各组件贡献，其中智能体注意力机制对全局时空依赖建模至关重要。

Conclusion: STAGformer通过创新的智能体注意力机制实现了线性复杂度的全局时空依赖建模，在保证计算效率的同时有效捕捉长程交互，为大规模共享单车系统的站点级需求预测提供了高效且准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate station-level demand forecasting is essential for the efficient operation of bike-sharing systems, yet it remains challenging due to complex spatio-temporal dependencies and the large scale of urban networks. This paper presents STAGformer, a Spatio-Temporal Agent Graph Transformer that achieves efficient global modeling with linear computational complexity. The model introduces a two-step agent attention mechanism, where a small set of learnable spatial and temporal agent tokens first aggregate global information and then broadcast it back to individual stations and time steps, effectively capturing long-range interactions while reducing the quadratic cost of standard self-attention to O(NT). STAGformer integrates four core modules: a spatio-temporal encoder that fuses dynamic node features with external contextual factors (weather, time, points of interest), a graph propagation module for spatial neighbor aggregation, a temporal convolution module for local pattern extraction, and the agent attention module for global dependency modeling. Extensive experiments on two real-world datasets -- NYC Citi-Bike and Chicago Divvy-Bike -- demonstrate that STAGformer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across multiple prediction horizons, achieving significant improvements in both RMSE and MAE. Ablation studies validate the contribution of each component, with the agent attention mechanism proving critical for modeling global spatio-temporal dependencies.

</details>


### [131] [WHERE to Generate Matters: Budget-Aware Synthetic Augmentation for Label Skewed Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06616)
*Sangwoo Lee,Sunghwan Park,Jaewoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedEAS提出了一种熵自适应的每类生成预算分配策略，根据客户端本地标签分布动态决定生成样本的数量和分配去向，在恢复全类平衡大部分精度收益的同时，将生成预算降低94.1%，且在相同总预算下比均匀分配方法提升高达18.82%的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中标签偏移（Label skew）导致客户端漂移和全局模型精度下降，已有的合成数据增强方法虽能缓解不平衡，但全类平衡需要巨大的计算开销。

Method: 提出FedEAS策略，基于客户端本地标签分布计算熵自适应的每类生成预算，该预算同时决定每个客户端生成多少样本以及生成的样本分配到何处。总生成预算由各客户端预算汇总得出，而非预先固定。

Result: FedEAS恢复了全类平衡大部分精度增益，同时将生成预算降低94.1%；在相同总预算下，在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上比均匀分配方法提升高达18.82%。

Conclusion: 通过熵自适应预算分配，FedEAS能够以极低的生成成本有效缓解标签偏移带来的负面影响，在联邦学习的数据增强中实现精度与效率的较好权衡。

Abstract: Label skew in federated learning (FL) causes client drift and degrades global accuracy. Synthetic data augmentation can reduce this imbalance; however, full class balancing requires substantial computation cost. We propose FedEAS, a policy that assigns each client an entropy-adaptive per-class generation budget computed from its local label distribution. The budget jointly decides \emph{how much} each client generates and \emph{WHERE} the samples go. Accordingly, the total generation budget follows from the per-client budgets rather than being fixed in advance. FedEAS recovers most of the accuracy gain of full class balancing while reducing the generation budget by 94.1\%. At the same total generation budget, it outperforms Uniform allocation by up to 18.82\% across CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100.

</details>


### [132] [Inertia-1: An Open Exploration of Wearable Motion Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06617)
*Zongzhe Xu,Aakarsh Anand,Sarah Jiang,Chuntung Zhuang,Zitao Shuai,Sriram Sankararaman,Yuzhe Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Inertia-1，一个完全开放的可穿戴运动基础模型探索框架，基于超过1820万小时的全球加速度计数据，从数据选择、模型选择、训练选择三个维度系统研究了可穿戴运动基础模型的完整生命周期，并在15个数据集上进行了广泛评估，提供了构建通用运动基础模型的最新方案和实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴运动传感为人类行为和健康提供了连续、可扩展的窗口，是基础模型的天然应用场景，但目前其预训练和扩展原理尚不清晰。先前研究仅在固定设置和狭窄下游任务中研究孤立的单一设计选择，无法捕捉现实世界传感的多样性，因此亟需系统性的研究框架。

Method: 构建了Inertia-1框架，使用超过1820万小时的全球加速度计数据语料库，系统控制了数据选择（传感器模态、设备位置、采样率、窗口长度）、模型选择（架构和模型大小）和训练选择（预训练目标和数据规模）三个维度的变量，并在15个数据集上评估，涵盖人类活动识别、步态冻结检测和疾病预测等任务。

Result: 在15个数据集上的广泛评估揭示了构建跨任务、跨传感条件泛化的运动基础模型的关键发现，并为多种下游任务提供了最先进的方案。

Conclusion: Inertia-1不仅为不同下游任务提供了最新的构建方案，还作为一个全面、实用且开放的可穿戴运动表示学习指南，推动了该领域的发展。

Abstract: Wearable motion sensing provides a continuous and scalable window into human behavior and health, making it a natural fit for foundation models, yet its pretraining and scaling principles remain poorly understood. Prior work studies isolated design choices, such as sensor placement or sampling frequency, often under fixed settings and narrow downstream tasks that fail to capture real-world sensing diversity. We introduce Inertia-1, a fully open exploration of wearable motion foundation models. Using massive corpora of accelerometer data from global sources spanning more than 18.2M hours, we build a controlled framework for studying the full lifecycle of wearable motion foundation models, covering data choices such as sensor modality, device placement, sampling rate, window length; model choices such as architectures and model size; and training choices such as pretraining objective and data scale. Extensive evaluations across 15 datasets spanning human activity recognition, freezing-of-gait detection, and disease prediction reveal intriguing findings for building motion foundation models that generalize across tasks and sensing conditions. Collectively, Inertia-1 not only presents state-of-the-art recipes for diverse downstream tasks, but also serves as a comprehensive, practical, and open cookbook for wearable motion representation learning.

</details>


### [133] [Fingerprint, Not Blueprint: How Positional Schemes Set the Default Spectral Algebra of Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06621)
*Li Hengyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了注意力头中pre-softmax得分的双线性形式$score(i,j) = x_i^T M x_j$，发现算子M通常非对称（非正规），具有复数特征谱和非正交特征向量。通过静态、动态和因果三个层面分析前词元与归纳电路：静态上，RoPE位置编码的头呈现频谱旋转特性，而其他位置方案（绝对位置、ALiBi）表现为非旋转的内容型；动态上，每个头从随机矩阵（Ginibre）零点起源，旋转特征随行为出现而非之前；因果上，无频谱通道是必需的，约束训练可绕过任何限制。结论：位置方案设定了注意力头解的默认频谱代数，特征谱是功能形成后的指纹，而非硬性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究注意力头中非对称算子M（由$W_q^T W_k$构成）的复数特征谱所编码的信息，特别是在前词元与归纳电路中的表现，以及位置编码方案（RoPE、绝对位置、ALiBi）如何影响频谱结构。

Method: 在三个层面进行分析：1）静态分析：跨7个预训练模型（3种位置方案）检查最强前词元头的频谱旋转特性；2）动态分析：在公开Pythia检查点上追踪每个头从随机矩阵原点的演化；3）因果分析：在玩具规模上通过约束训练测试频谱通道的必要性。

Result: 静态上，RoPE模型的头呈现频谱旋转，非RoPE模型（绝对位置、ALiBi）为内容型，模型级分离完美（精确置换检验p=0.029）；动态上，旋转特征随行为出现而非之前，频谱轮廓是功能形成后的指纹；因果上，无频谱通道是必需的，约束训练可绕过任何限制但会延迟形成。

Conclusion: 位置方案设定了注意力头解的默认频谱代数，特征谱是功能形成后留下的指纹，而非对解决方案的硬性约束。对称性约束对不同方案有不同成本：对绝对位置模型延迟2.9倍，而RoPE头仍可通过相位通道定向路由。

Abstract: The pre-softmax score of an attention head is a bilinear form $score(i,j) = x_i^T M x_j$ in a learned operator $M = W_q^T W_k$. Because M is generally non-symmetric, hence non-normal, it has a complex eigenspectrum and non-orthogonal eigenvectors, the regime where non-Hermitian and random-matrix tools apply. We ask what this spectrum encodes, at three levels for previous-token and induction circuits. Statically, across seven pretrained models spanning three positional schemes, the strongest previous-token heads are spectrally rotational under RoPE and non-rotational, or content-like, where position enters outside QK (learned-absolute and ALiBi); the model-level separation is perfect at every top-k examined (exact permutation $p=0.029$), and zeroing the per-frequency RoPE phase $Im(M_t)$ eliminates induction on a pre-identified previous-token head in all three RoPE models. Dynamically, over public Pythia checkpoints every head originates at the random-matrix (Ginibre) null; the rotational signature emerges with the behavior, not before it, and the population-median suppression that yields the final profile follows circuit formation, so the profile is a consolidated fingerprint, not a precursor. Causally, and at toy scale, no spectral channel is necessary: constrained two-layer training reroutes around every ban with capability intact, albeit at a significant formation delay (four pre-registered contrasts, $q_BH <= 0.016$). The cost structure exposes each scheme's default: imposing symmetry slows learned-absolute models by a factor of 2.9, whereas a RoPE head with a fully symmetric static M still routes directionally via the phase channel, impossible under absolute positions. Within the settings examined, the positional scheme sets the default spectral algebra of an attention head's solution: a fingerprint sculpted after function, not a hard constraint upon it.

</details>


### [134] [LLM-Guided Task-Semantic Field Factorization for Industrial Process Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06623)
*Youcheng Zong,Runda Jia,Mingxuan Ren,Dakuo He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TSF框架，利用大语言模型离线构建任务语义场，将过程文档中的变量语义信息注入时间序列预测模型，在几乎不增加在线计算开销的情况下，平均降低MAE 6.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流程工业中，质量变量在线难测，依赖时间序列预测和软测量；但标注数据稀缺、操作工况频繁变化，重新训练或重建对齐管道成本高。现有方法忽略输入变量与预测目标之间的语义逻辑关系，时间序列骨干网络将输入视为匿名数值列，缺乏语义信息。

Method: TSF框架包含两阶段：离线阶段，利用LLM从任务协议和变量文档构建任务语义场；在线阶段，当前数值窗口激活对应变量语义，使语义信息参与每次预测，支持不同预测目标和操作迁移，而训练和推理仍使用传统时间序列骨干网络。

Result: 在多个复杂工业预测和软测量任务上，TSF在改进设置下平均降低MAE 6.4%，最大降低25.5%；仅增加约1.8-3.0k参数，在线推理额外开销小于0.008ms/step。

Conclusion: TSF能够将现有的过程文档转化为可量化的预测增益，跨骨干网络和语义生成器均有效，且轻量易部署。

Abstract: Process industries rely on time-series forecasting and soft sensing to estimate quality variables that are hard to measure online. Labeled data are scarce, operating regimes change frequently, and retraining models or rebuilding alignment pipelines for each scenario is costly. Such settings often provide variable tables and process documents that record variable names, units, physical meanings, and process roles. However, standard time-series backbones usually treat inputs as anonymous numerical columns. Existing text-enhanced methods also rarely make the semantic-logical relations between input variables and the prediction target available to the model within each numerical window. To address this problem, this article proposes Task-Semantic Field Factorization (TSF), a large language model (LLM)-guided framework. TSF builds a task-semantic field from task protocols and variable documents before training and uses the LLM only for offline semantic construction. Online training and inference remain with conventional time-series backbones. During training and inference, the current numerical window activates variable semantics, so semantic information participates in each prediction and supports adaptation to different prediction targets and operating shifts. On multiple complex industrial forecasting and soft-sensing tasks, TSF reduces MAE by 6.4\% on average in improved settings, with the largest reduction reaching 25.5\%. It adds only about 1.8--3.0k parameters, with less than 0.008 ms/step of additional online inference overhead. These results show that TSF turns existing process documents into measurable forecasting gains across backbones and semantic generators while remaining lightweight for deployment.

</details>


### [135] [Open-Ended Scenario Reasoning for Specialist Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06625)
*Youcheng Zong,Runda Jia,Ranmeng Lin,Mingxuan Ren,Dakuo He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ROAM框架，利用LLM的世界知识和推理能力，在不重新训练的情况下，通过低维可解释潜空间对冻结的专业工业模型进行自适应修正，在矿物浓缩和青霉素发酵数据集上MAE降低超20%，仅需839个额外参数和<0.02ms每步开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程工业积累的专业模型在新场景下因传感器漂移、原料变化和工况切换而性能退化，收集新标注数据重新训练成本高昂，现有自适应方法需大量标注数据修改模型参数，而直接使用LLM作为预测器存在幻觉和不可控输出风险，且无法融入领域非结构化场景知识。

Method: ROAM框架将修正限制在低维语义可解释的潜空间中；融合LLM生成的场景判断和在线观测数据于统一概率框架；采用风险约束机制在LLM证据不可靠或场景突变时抑制修正，回退到原始冻结模型。

Result: 在矿物浓缩过程和IndPenSim青霉素发酵数据集上，ROAM在隐藏偏移等主要偏移场景中MAE降低超过20%，仅引入839个额外参数，每步开销低于0.02ms。

Conclusion: LLM推理能力可以转化为保守的自适应信号，用于已在服役的工业模型，实现无需重训的高效场景自适应。

Abstract: Process industries have accumulated validated specialist models, yet sensor drift, feedstock variation, and regime switching cause these models to degrade systematically in new scenarios. Collecting new labeled data and retraining is costly, while continuing with the original model incurs persistent bias. Existing adaptation methods require modifying model parameters with sufficient labeled data, making rapid response on deployed systems difficult. Using LLMs as direct predictors risks hallucinations and uncontrollable outputs. Such predictors also cannot incorporate unstructured scenario knowledge from the field. To address these limitations, this article proposes Reasoning-Driven Open Adaptation for Specialist Models (ROAM), a framework that uses LLM world knowledge and reasoning to adapt frozen specialist models to unseen scenarios without retraining. ROAM confines all corrections to a low-dimensional, semantically interpretable latent space. LLM-generated scenario judgments and online observations are fused under a unified probabilistic framework. A risk-constrained mechanism suppresses corrections under unreliable LLM evidence or abrupt scenario shifts and falls back to the original frozen model when evidence is insufficient. Experiments on a mineral thickening process and the public IndPenSim penicillin fermentation dataset show that ROAM reduces MAE by over 20\% in major shift settings such as hidden shifts with only 839 additional parameters and under 0.02\,ms per-step overhead. These results indicate that LLM reasoning can be turned into a conservative adaptation signal for industrial models already in service.

</details>


### [136] [Reward Valuation in Vision Language Models: Causal Mechanisms Underlying Anhedonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06626)
*Melika Honarmand,Samin Mahdipour Aghabagher,Martin Schrimpf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究发现视觉语言模型（VLMs）中存在与人类伏隔核（NAc）功能相似的奖励预期神经单元，扰动这些单元会导致模型出现类似人类快感缺乏的行为表现——在努力型决策任务中偏向低努力低回报选项，且这种缺陷特异地影响奖励估值而非任务能力，与临床快感缺乏量表（DARS、MAP-SR）的测量结果一致，揭示了AI模型中存在与人类平行的奖励估值回路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉语言模型是否在奖励估值这一认知维度上与人类认知对齐，并利用神经科学中关于快感缺乏（anhedonia）与伏隔核及多巴胺奖励系统失调的临床框架，建立因果性机制分析。

Method: 基于临床快感缺乏评估测试（DARS、MAP-SR）构建机制框架，在视觉语言模型中功能性地识别奖励预期单元（类比NAc），并通过针对性扰动（perturbation）实验测试其因果作用，观察模型在努力型决策任务中的行为变化。

Result: 扰动NAc选择性单元后，模型在决策任务中转向低努力低回报选项，表现出类似人类快感缺乏的行为；当取消基于奖励的选择时，扰动模型的基线性能保持不变，表明缺陷特异地针对奖励估值而非任务能力；该诱导缺陷与临床快感缺乏和动机量表（DARS、MAP-SR）的测量结果一致。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型中存在与人类奖励估值回路功能相似的神经单元，AI模型与人类在奖励估值这一认知维度上表现出平行对齐关系，为理解AI模型中的情感/动机机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language Models capture increasingly complex aspects of human cognition. Here we ask whether this alignment extends to reward valuation, which we assess in a mechanistic framework built on clinical tests that were developed to evaluate anhedonia and motivational deficits in major depressive disorder. In the brain, anhedonia is frequently linked to dysregulation in the Nucleus Accumbens (NAc) and the broader dopaminergic reward system. While neuroimaging has localized these deficits, establishing a causal link between NAc activity and specific behavioral symptoms remains a challenge. We use these ideas from neuroscience to functionally identify reward-anticipatory units in vision language models, and test their causal role via targeted perturbations. Perturbing NAc-selective units induces behavioral effects that mirror human anhedonia: the model shifts toward low-effort, low-reward options in effort-based decision-making tasks. Crucially, our results reflect a specific deficit in reward valuation and anticipation rather than a loss of task capability: the perturbed model maintains baseline performance when reward-based choice is removed. This induced vulnerability further aligns with clinical anhedonia and motivation scales, including DARS and MAP-SR. Taken together, these results reveal reward valuation circuits in AI models that parallel those in humans.

</details>


### [137] [Cross-Trajectory Chimera Interventions Reveal Dissociable Roles of Weight Magnitude and Direction in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06628)
*Truong Xuan Khanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现，在不同随机种子训练的神经网络中，权重的方向（而非范数）携带了可迁移的电路身份信息：将捐赠者的方向植入接收者的范数，能使接收者转向捐赠者的电路结构（40/40例成功），而范数仅产生微弱的延迟效应，无身份信号。迁移具有阈值特性，其位置可由接收者的范数精确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究部分训练网络的哪些属性具有跨不同独立训练网络的因果可移植性，即验证单轨迹干预（同一运行内的必要性分析）是否足以解释跨运行的因果规律。

Method: 提出"跨轨迹嵌合体干预"方法：将两个不同随机种子训练轨迹的权重向量拆分为范数和单位方向，重新组合（一个轨迹的范数+另一个轨迹的方向）后继续训练。在两种模算术任务（具有"顿悟"现象）上进行实验，并与随机角度匹配的对照比较。

Result: 方向携带可迁移的电路身份：40/40例成功使接收者转向捐赠者电路；范数无身份信号，仅产生分布式延迟效应。迁移呈阈值状，阈值位置由接收者范数精确预测（20对中按范数类别完美分离，联合排列概率1.9e-4），自适应二分法定位精度达±1/64。

Conclusion: 方向索引了轨迹趋向的解决方案身份，范数则控制该身份被覆盖的易感性。跨轨迹的因果可移植性主要由方向编码，范数仅调节迁移阈值。

Abstract: Which properties of a partially trained network are causally portable to a different, independently trained network? Single-trajectory interventions show necessity within one run, not portability across runs. We introduce cross-trajectory chimera interventions: given two runs from different seeds, we split each weight vector into a norm and a unit direction, recombine one run's norm with the other's direction, and continue training. On two modular-arithmetic tasks that grok, the components dissociate. Direction carries a transferable, donor-specific circuit identity: implanting a donor's direction at the recipient's norm drives the run to the donor's circuit in 40/40 cases, while an angle-matched random control yields no shift. The transfer is threshold-like, and its location is predicted by the recipient's norm, separating perfectly by norm class over all 20 pairs (joint permutation probability 1.9e-4). Norm carries only a modest, distributed delay effect and no identity signal. An adaptive bisection procedure localizes the threshold to +/-1/64. Direction indexes which solution a trajectory approaches; norm governs how susceptible that identity is to being overwritten.

</details>


### [138] [STST-JEPA: Shallow-Target Spatio-Temporal Joint Embedding Prediction Architecture For EEG Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06629)
*Roy Segal,Yoni Svechinsky,Tomer Fekete*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STST-JEPA，一个基于自监督学习的EEG基础模型，在47,703个会话上预训练，通过结合潜在预测和信号重建目标，在年龄回归任务上达到3.06年MAE（r=0.924），并在性别分类、年龄预测和精神病理学回归中取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG脑年龄作为神经精神疾病的生物标志物具有巨大潜力，但现有模型面临跨站点导联异质性、小标注样本量、主体级非平稳性等挑战，且缺乏能从儿童到老年全年龄段有效工作的EEG基础模型。

Method: 采用自监督transformer架构，结合两种预训练目标：1）潜在预测（对掩码token表示进行预测，目标为tokenizer的EMA）；2）辅助信号重建。使用时空块掩码处理30秒多通道窗口。在brain.space和HBN语料库的47,703个会话（年龄5-81岁）上预训练。

Result: 冻结嵌入的轻量注意力探测头在3,367个会话上达到3.06年MAE（r=0.924），对比预测均值基线约10年MAE。微调后，在NeuralBench x brain.space EEG排行榜上：性别分类（平衡准确率0.911）、年龄预测（r=0.749）、精神病理学复合回归（r=0.215）均获第一。年龄预测残差与认知效率呈负相关。

Conclusion: STST-JEPA证明自监督预训练策略能有效解决EEG跨站点异质性和小样本挑战，在全年龄段实现可靠的年龄回归，且模型输出的年龄偏差具有行为学相关性，可作为有意义的神经认知生物标志物。

Abstract: Brain age -- the age inferred from a physiological recording -- is an emerging biomarker whose deviation from chronological age tracks neurological and psychiatric burden, and EEG is an attractive substrate for it because it is cheap, portable, and temporally rich. Yet EEG brain-age models must contend with cross-site montage heterogeneity, small labelled cohorts, and dominant subject-level non-stationarity, and few EEG foundation models have been shown to deliver competitive age regression across the full pediatric-to-older-adult range in which such a biomarker would actually be deployed. We introduce STST-JEPA, a self-supervised transformer for resting-state and task EEG, pretrained on 47,703 sessions spanning ages 5-81 from the brain.space and Healthy Brain Network (HBN) corpora. The model combines a latent-prediction objective - predicting masked-token representations against an EMA-of-tokenizer target - with an auxiliary signal-reconstruction term, applied to 30-second multi-channel windows under spatiotemporal block masks. A lightweight attentive probe trained on frozen pretrained embeddings achieves a best held-out-validation mean absolute error of 3.06 years (r = 0.924) for age regression on 3,367 sessions, against a predict-the-mean baseline of approximately 10 years MAE. With light task-specific fine-tuning of the model's final layers, the same pretrained encoder achieves rank-1 placements - with the model's native 30-second windows - on the public NeuralBench x brain.space EEG leaderboard for sex classification (balanced accuracy 0.911), age prediction (r = 0.749), and psychopathology composite regression (r = 0.215). We further show that the model's age-prediction residual is negatively correlated with cognitive efficiency over several tasks we examined.

</details>


### [139] [When Certificates Fail: A Unified Safety Framework for Embedded Neural Interface Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06630)
*Jasmeet Singh Bindra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示神经接口模型中形式鲁棒性证书与操作安全性之间存在根本性错位：在任务精度已显著下降时，Lipschitz证书仍保持有效。作者提出统一的经验审计框架，涵盖验证不足、代理-保真度分歧、潜在信息泄露三类对齐失败，实验证明仅依赖证书验证不足以保障神经接口的安全部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经接口模型的形式鲁棒性证书（如Lipschitz风格证书）在面对对抗攻击时可能仍然有效，但任务精度已严重下降。这种数学认证与操作安全性之间的脱节可能导致部署风险。作者认为这是训练目标与用户体验相背离的广义对齐失败的一个案例。

Method: 提出统一的经验审计框架，针对三类对齐失败：（1）验证不足（证书通过但任务行为退化）；（2）代理-保真度分歧（任务优化表示损害神经信号结构）；（3）潜在信息泄露（公开任务嵌入保留隐私属性）。在BCI Competition IV 2a和SEED-IV数据集上，使用EEGNet、CSP+LDA、FBCSP+LDA等多种深度学习与传统EEG解码器，采用官方会话级验证、零对照和配对统计检验。

Result: 扰动预算ε=0.25时，EEGNet在投影梯度攻击下分类精度下降25.7%，但Lipschitz证书对所有9名受试者仍有效。验证不足现象在EEGNet、CSP+LDA、FBCSP+LDA上均存在，显示架构无关性。时域辅助目标将重建MSE降低0.1132，但频谱log-MSE恶化。主体身份在公开任务嵌入中可恢复率达48.1%（随机基线6.7%）。

Conclusion: 操作安全审计（而非仅证书验证）是负责任的神经接口部署的必要条件。当前形式化认证方法在实际部署中存在严重局限，需引入经验性对齐审计框架以确保模型的行为安全性。

Abstract: Formal robustness certificates for embedded neural-interface models can pass while task accuracy collapses: at perturbation budget e=0.25, EEGNet classification accuracy drops by 25.7% under projected-gradient attack while the Lipschitz-style certificate remains valid for all 9 tested subjects. We argue that this gap between mathematical certification and operational safety is one instance of a broader alignment failure in neural interfaces, where training objectives diverge from user welfare.
