Motivation: 线性注意力模型因状态大小有限，在长上下文召回任务上落后于softmax注意力Transformer。增大状态大小虽能提升召回性能，但会导致FLOPs增加。如何在保持计算效率的同时大幅提升状态容量是核心问题。

Method: SDM扩展了Gated DeltaNet架构，用稀疏读写操作替代密集的键值外积，将大规模显式记忆与门控线性RNN结合。通过稀疏寻址方案实现状态容量数量级提升，并支持学习记忆的初始状态作为参数化记忆。

Result: 在isoFLOP约束和相同参数量下，更高状态记忆容量显著提升了上下文学习和长上下文检索任务的性能。学习记忆初始状态进一步改善了常识知识和推理任务的表现。

Conclusion: 稀疏寻址方案能有效突破线性注意力模型的状态容量瓶颈，在保持计算效率的同时实现长上下文性能提升，参数化初始记忆状态可额外增强推理能力。

Abstract: Linear attention models allow a fixed state size and a fixed amount of compute per token. However, due to their limited state size, linear attention models fall behind in long-context recall compared to softmax-attention-based transformer architectures. Increasing the state size of linear attention improves recall performance but at the cost of higher FLOPs. In this work, we introduce Sparse Delta Memory (SDM), an architecture that scales the hidden state of gated linear RNNs to orders of magnitude higher capacity using a sparse addressing scheme. SDM extends the Gated DeltaNet architecture by replacing the dense key-value outer product with sparse reads and writes to a large explicit memory. We show that, under an isoFLOP constraint and with an identical number of parameters, a higher state memory capacity significantly improves performance on in-context learning and long-context retrieval tasks. Moreover, by learning the initial state of the SDM memory and therefore using it as a parametric memory, we show that the model further improves on a wide range of common-knowledge and reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [212] [The Optimal Sample Complexity of Learning Autoregressive Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07423)
*Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在可实现PAC学习框架下，完整自回归思维链轨迹的精确跟踪学习样本复杂度由局部下一词类的标准多类学习率上界决定，该率由Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz维度控制。通过引入奇偶维度（一种基于偶伪立方体的滚动稳定DS维度精炼），证明了样本复杂度与轨迹长度无关，且依赖关系在最坏情况下最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在PAC可学习框架下，自回归思维链（Chain-of-Thought）轨迹的精确跟踪学习（exact-trace learning）的样本复杂度，特别是当轨迹长度可能很长时，样本复杂度是否会随长度增长而爆炸式增加。

Method: 1) 引入奇偶维度（parity dimension）作为DS维度的滚动稳定精炼版本；2) 通过低坐标支撑定理（low-coordinate spanning theorem）控制包含一密度；3) 证明DS维度在滚动操作下可能增大，因此需要奇偶维度这个绕路方案。

Result: 1) 精确跟踪学习的样本复杂度为O((DSdim(H)+log(1/δ))/ε)，与滚动长度无关；2) 该依赖关系在最坏情况下是最优的；3) 奇偶维度在自回归滚动下不会增大，而DS维度可能增大。

Conclusion: 自回归思维链的精确跟踪学习不会因轨迹长度增加而需要更多样本，其样本复杂度仅由局部下一词类本身的复杂度决定，为思维链推理的可学习性提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We prove that, in the realizable PAC setting, the sample complexity of exact-trace learning for full autoregressive Chain-of-Thought traces is upper bounded by the standard multiclass rate of the local next-token class, where this rate is governed by the Daniely--Shalev-Shwartz dimension. Under exact-trace loss, one wrong action makes the whole trace incorrect; nevertheless, for every stopping rule $\mathtt{halt}$ and every pointwise $\mathtt{halt}$-halting local class $\mathrm{H}$, $n_{\mathrm{PAC}}^{\varepsilon,δ}(\operatorname{Roll}_{\mathtt{halt}}(\mathrm{H}))=O((\operatorname{DSdim}(\mathrm{H})+\log(1/δ))/\varepsilon)$, with no dependence on rollout length. The dependence on $\operatorname{DSdim}(\mathrm{H})$ is worst-case optimal, since one-step stopping recovers ordinary multiclass learning of $\mathrm{H}$. The proof introduces parity dimension, a rollout-stable refinement of DS dimension based on even pseudo-cubes. It controls one-inclusion density via a low-coordinate spanning theorem on finite restrictions and, unlike DS dimension itself, does not increase under autoregressive rollout. We also show why this detour is necessary: DS dimension can increase under rollout.

</details>


### [213] [RLVP: Penalize the Path, Reward the Outcome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07435)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对智能体在现实世界中在线学习面临的路径约束和样本效率问题，提出"惩罚路径、奖励结果"的训练框架，相比纯结果导向的RLVR能显著降低违规率同时保持高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的智能体（如代打电话）必须在线学习，交互成本高且不可逆，但现有强化学习从可验证奖励（RLVR）仅优化最终结果，忽视路径约束（如不反复拨打无人接听的电话、遵守营业时间等），且浪费大量昂贵交互在无效探索上。

Method: 提出"惩罚路径、奖励结果"的联合训练框架：利用可验证的路径惩罚（verifiable penalty）替代难以验证的进度奖励，通过四个设计规则避免惩罚单独使用的"不作为陷阱"。核心理论是组相对优势等价于组内方差，密集信号仅在提供结果缺乏的方差时有效，而路径惩罚总能满足这一条件。

Result: 该方法在保持高任务成功率的同时实现近乎零的约束违规，而纯结果训练的RLVR几乎每轮都违反约束。

Conclusion: 对于现实世界中的智能体在线学习，可验证的路径惩罚比可验证的进度奖励更可靠地提供密集监督信号，联合优化路径和结果是更优的范式。

Abstract: Agents acting on our behalf in the real world (e.g. placing phone calls) must learn online from costly, often irreversible interactions rather than cheap simulator steps. Two things follow. First, deployability depends on the path, not only the outcome. An agent must respect outcome-neutral constraints such as not repeatedly calling an unresponsive user, respecting business hours, or completing required authentication constraints that outcome-based rewards cannot express, since violating them frequently improves apparent success. Second, because each interaction is expensive, the agent must learn efficiently from very few examples.
  Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) is blind to both challenges: it optimizes solely on the outcome and wastes expensive rollouts on all-fail groups where group-relative advantage collapses to zero. Attempts to densify supervision by rewarding progress target the hard-to-verify direction. In contrast, real agentic environments can cheaply detect bad moves. Since group-relative advantage is equivalent to within-group variance, a dense signal helps only when it supplies variance the outcome lacks. A verifiable penalty on the path meets this condition reliably, while a progress potential helps only where partial progress is reachable. The resulting recipe "penalize the path, reward the outcome" achieves high task success with near-zero violations, where outcome-only training violates constraints on nearly every episode. We provide four design rules for effective penalties, including avoidance of the inaction trap that arises when a penalty is used in isolation.

</details>


### [214] [Where to Intervene? Benchmarking Fairness-Aware Learning on Differentially Private Synthetic Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07471)
*Vinícius Gabriel Angelozzi,Héber H. Arcolezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了差分隐私合成数据上的公平性干预措施，发现隐私保护会加剧不公平性，但事后处理方法能在不同隐私预算下实现较好的公平-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在高风险领域的部署引发了隐私与公平的双重担忧。差分隐私（DP）是隐私保护的标准方法，公平性机制旨在减少对弱势群体的歧视，但这两个目标可能相互冲突——DP往往会放大不同人口群体间的差异，且现有公平性干预在DP约束下是否仍然有效尚不清楚。

Method: 以AIM（当前最先进的基于边际的DP合成器）为核心，在四个数据集上评估多种群体公平性指标，涵盖预处理、处理中、事后处理三类缓解策略，在多种隐私预算下比较四种管线配置：基线（原始数据）、仅DP（DP合成数据）、仅公平（原始数据+公平机制）、DP+公平（DP合成数据+公平机制）。

Result: DP单独使用会降低效用和公平性；应用公平性干预可部分恢复公平结果；事后处理方法在不同隐私预算和合成器间提供更稳定的公平-效用权衡，在保持竞争性效用的同时实现较强的公平性改善。

Conclusion: 公平性干预可以缓解DP带来的不公平性放大效应，其中事后处理是最鲁棒的策略。本文提供了首个系统性基准测试，并开源所有代码、数据和实验成果以支持可复现性和后续隐私-公平-效用权衡研究。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains, raising concerns about both privacy and fairness. Differential Privacy (DP) has become a gold standard for privacy-preserving data analysis, while fairness-aware mechanisms aim to mitigate discrimination against underrepresented groups. However, these objectives can conflict: DP often amplifies disparities across demographic groups, and little is known about whether established fairness interventions remain effective under DP constraints. In this work, we present, to our knowledge, the first systematic evaluation of fairness interventions on differentially private synthetic tabular data. Our benchmark centers on the Adaptive Iterative Mechanism (AIM), identified as the state-of-the-art marginal-based DP synthesizer (Cormode et al. 2025). We thus evaluate fairness interventions across four datasets, multiple group fairness metrics, and three categories of mitigation strategies (pre-processing, in-processing, and post-processing) under a wide range of privacy budgets. We compare four pipeline configurations: (Baseline) training on original data; (DP-only) training on DP synthetic data; (Fair-only) applying fairness mechanisms on original data; and (DP+Fair) combining fairness mechanisms with DP synthetic data. Our results demonstrate that while DP alone can degrade both utility and fairness, applying fairness interventions can partially restore equitable outcomes. Among them, post-processing methods tend to provide more stable fairness-utility trade-offs across privacy budgets and synthesizers, achieving strong fairness improvements while preserving competitive utility relative to other intervention stages. We release all code, data, and experimental artifacts in an open-source repository to ensure full reproducibility and to support future research on the privacy-fairness-utility trade-off.

</details>


### [215] [Reward-Adaptive Iterative Discovery: A Case Study on Automated Game Testing for NHL26](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07498)
*Florian Fuchs,Jessy Gosselin-Grant,Boris Skuin,Michele Petteni,Alessandro Sestini,Joakim Bergdahl,Amir Baghi,Linus Gisslén*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reward-Adaptive Iterative Discovery (RAID)方法，利用迭代强化学习自动发现EA SPORTS NHL 26游戏中门将AI的行为漏洞，在单次实验中找到了六种与人类测试员数小时手动测试相似的得分漏洞策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏行业测试成本高昂，人类玩家测试门将AI漏洞需要大量人力和时间，需要自动化方法来降低回归测试的负担。

Method: 提出RAID方法：采用迭代强化学习训练一群进球智能体，在现有RL算法基础上增加简单扩展以找到多个多样化高质量解决方案，避免RL算法对单一方案的过拟合。

Result: 单次实验发现六种冰球得分漏洞策略，与人类测试员数小时手动测试发现的漏洞在性质上相似。

Conclusion: RAID方法能有效自动发现游戏AI漏洞，减少人工测试工作量，且能发现多样化高质量解决方案。

Abstract: Testing is a major effort for the gaming industry, requiring a significant part of development budget and people power. We present a case study on a development version of the ice hockey game EA SPORTS NHL 26, for which human playtesters test the goalie AI for behavioral exploits. To reduce the effort of re-testing the goalie AI after every game or behavior modification in the development phase, we propose Reward-Adaptive Iterative Discovery (RAID), a novel approach to automatically find exploits using an iterative Reinforcement Learning (RL) approach that trains a population of goal scoring agents. While previous approaches can already successfully find exploits, RL algorithms tend to overfit to a single solution. We introduce a simple extension on top of existing RL algorithms, such that they find multiple diverse high-quality solutions. For our first deployment of this approach, within a single experiment we were able to find six hockey scoring exploit strategies that were qualitatively similar to those that playtesters had found in hours-long manual testing sessions.

</details>


### [216] [TimEE: End-to-end Time Series Classification via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07500)
*Jaris Küken,Shi Bin Hoo,Martin Mráz,Frank Hutter,Lennart Purucker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TimEE，一个仅4.5M参数的时间序列分类基础模型，通过元训练纯合成数据实现端到端上下文学习，无需针对特定数据集训练即可在UCR基准上达到SOTA性能（ROC AUC第一，准确率第三）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSC方法遵循两阶段范式（特征编码+分类器），存在三个问题：表示学习与分类目标解耦导致优化次优、需要针对每个数据集重新训练、推理时无法利用标签信息。作者希望设计一个能端到端完成分类、无需每数据集训练、且能利用上下文标签信息的模型。

Method: 采用Prior-data Fitted Networks (PFN)框架，在纯合成时间序列分类任务上进行元训练。每个合成任务包含来自不同生成过程（具有结构化分布偏移）的时间序列，模型通过上下文学习直接输出查询序列的类别分布。模型仅4.5M参数，单次前向传播完成分类。

Result: 在UCR基准上，TimEE在ROC AUC指标上排名第一，准确率排名第三。与现有基础模型和监督深度学习方法相比均取得竞争优势。这是首个仅凭合成预训练就在UCR上达到SOTA的模型。

Conclusion: 端到端上下文学习结合合成先验是TSC领域一个极具前景但尚未充分探索的方向。未来可沿扩展模型规模、改进先验设计、丰富生成机制等方向进一步提升。

Abstract: Time series classification (TSC) is dominated by a two-stage paradigm: train a feature encoder -- either from scratch on the target dataset or via pretraining on large corpora -- and then fit a task-specific classifier on top. While effective, this decoupling optimizes representation learning independently of the classification objective, requires per-dataset training, and prevents the model from exploiting label information during inference. We introduce TimEE, a 4.5M-parameter foundation model for end-to-end TSC via in-context learning. Given a labeled support set and a query time series, TimEE directly outputs a predicted class distribution in a single forward pass with no per-dataset training required. Following the prior-data fitted network (PFN) framework, TimEE is meta-trained exclusively on synthetic TSC tasks, where each task contains time series with distinct class identities arising from structured distributional shifts in the generative process. Despite seeing no real time series during pre-training, TimEE ranks first in ROC AUC (and third on accuracy) on the UCR benchmark among all compared methods, which include both foundation models and supervised deep learning baselines. To our knowledge, TimEE is the first purely synthetic-pretrained model to reach state-of-the-art performance on the UCR benchmark. These results establish end-to-end ICL with synthetic priors as a compelling, largely unexplored direction for TSC, with scaling, prior design, and richer generation mechanisms as natural avenues for improvement. Code is publicly available at http://github.com/automl/timee.

</details>


### [217] [Single-Rollout Asynchronous Optimization for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07508)
*Zhenyu Hou,Yujiang Li,Jie Tang,Yuxiao Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出单次 rollout 异步优化（SAO）方法，解决异步强化学习在 LLM 后训练中的稳定性和 off-policy 问题，通过单 rollout 采样和严格的双侧 token 级裁剪策略，在编码和推理基准上持续优于 GRPO 及其变体，并成功部署于 GLM-5.2 模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步 RL 在长程智能体任务中效率更高，但现有系统侧重吞吐量，忽略了训练稳定性和任务有效性。GRPO 的组级采样不适用于异步智能体训练，存在 off-policy 和稳定性挑战。

Method: 提出 SAO 框架：1）用单 rollout 采样替代组级采样，每个 prompt 只用一个 rollout，减少 off-policy 影响并提升泛化；2）引入严格的双侧 token 级裁剪策略提升优化稳定性；3）结合实用的价值模型训练设计。

Result: SAO 能够稳定训练一千步，在 SWE-Bench Verified、BeyondAIME、IMOAnswerBench 等编码和推理基准上持续优于 GRPO 及其变体。单 rollout RL 在模拟在线学习环境中特别有效。SAO 已成功部署于 GLM-5.2（750B-A40B）的智能体 RL 训练管线。

Conclusion: 单 rollout 异步优化结合严格裁剪策略能有效解决异步 RL 的稳定性和 off-policy 问题，为大规模 LLM 后训练提供更高效的方案，尤其在智能体和在线学习场景中表现出色。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is becoming increasingly important for post-training large language models (LLMs). Previous RL pipelines for LLMs were mostly synchronous and batch-interleaved, which is inefficient for long-horizon agentic tasks. Recently, asynchronous RL has emerged as a more efficient alternative by updating the model as rollouts arrive. However, existing asynchronous RL systems often emphasize throughput, while leaving training stability and task effectiveness largely underexplored. For example, a key challenge is that group-wise sampling in the widely-used GRPO framework does not naturally fit asynchronous agentic training. In this paper, we present Single-rollout Asynchronous Optimization (SAO) to address the stability and off-policy challenges in asynchronous RL. To reduce off-policy effects and improve generalization, we replace group-wise sampling with single-rollout sampling, that is, using one rollout per prompt. We further improve this single-rollout strategy with practical value-model training designs. To improve optimization stability, we introduce a strict double-side token-level clipping strategy. SAO is able to train stably for one thousand steps and consistently outperform GRPO and its variants on agentic coding and reasoning benchmarks, such as SWE-Bench Verified, BeyondAIME, and IMOAnswerBench. We also demonstrate that single-rollout RL is particularly effective in a simulated online learning setting, where the model must adapt to changing evolving environments. To this end, SAO is successfully deployed in the agentic RL pipeline for training the open GLM-5.2 model (750B-A40B).

</details>


### [218] [Fast Rates for Semi-Supervised Learning via Data-Augmentation Graph Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07513)
*Adam M. Oberman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为自监督学习的标签效率提供了理论解释：数据增强诱导的相似图将下游学习转化为图拉普拉斯正则化学习，证明了标签数量上的快速传递率 O(1/n_L)（远优于监督学习的 O(1/√n_L)），并显式地将增强质量与误差 bound 联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习在标注数据极少的情况下能达到与监督学习相当的精度，但这种标注样本效率缺乏理论解释。

Method: 将数据增强诱导的相似图上的下游学习建模为图拉普拉斯正则化学习，将 Johnson 和 Zhang 的留一法稳定性分析框架迁移到增强图上，并设计了一种精简损失函数（去除了投影头、负样本和正交性开销）。

Result: 证明了快速传递率 O(1/n_L) 而非监督学习的 O(1/√n_L)；误差上界为 C/n_L + R_DA(y)，其中 R_DA(y) 是跨越标签边界的数据增强的图割质量；精简损失在无限数据极限下仍能恢复前 K 个理想特征。

Conclusion: 该理论解释了观测到的精度-标签数量曲线，而不仅仅是界定泛化差距，并显式量化了数据增强质量对标签效率的影响。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning matches supervised accuracy from a fraction of the labels, but the labeled-sample efficiency behind this has lacked a theoretical explanation. We provide one. Data augmentation induces a similarity graph on the unlabeled data, so downstream learning on that graph is graph-Laplacian-regularized learning. We prove a fast transductive rate, $O(1/n_L)$ in the number of labels, in place of the supervised $O(1/\sqrt{n_L})$, by carrying the leave-one-out stability apparatus of Johnson and Zhang (JMLR 2007) over to the augmentation graph, and without the unrealistic assumptions of limit-based analyses (exact kernel, generalizing features). The bound makes augmentation quality explicit: the expected error is at most $C/n_L + R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$, where the data-augmentation alignment error $R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$ is the graph-cut mass of augmentations that cross a label boundary, so good augmentations let few labels suffice. The analysis uses a streamlined loss that drops the projector, negative-sample, and orthogonality overhead of standard objectives yet still recovers the top-$K$ ideal features in the infinite-data limit, the augmentation-kernel eigenspace studied by Zhai et al. The result explains the observed accuracy-versus-label-count curve rather than only bounding a generalization gap.

</details>


### [219] [Gradient-free Riemannian Langevin Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07519)
*Ricardo Baptista,Olivier Zahm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需梯度信息的黎曼 Langevin 采样器（GRiLS），通过引入黎曼度量重塑局部几何结构以增强多峰分布采样，实验表明其混合效率优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 MCMC 方法在多峰概率分布采样中常面临混合不良和模态捕获问题，而现有梯度方法在复杂/计算昂贵的目标中导数不可用或不实用。

Method: 提出 GRiLS 算法，通过引入黎曼度量重塑局部几何结构以促进模态间转移，无需目标密度的梯度评估；利用交互粒子系综估计目标分布的均值和协方差，以此构建提议分布。

Result: 在多峰基准测试中，GRiLS 在混合效率上优于现有的基于梯度和无梯度 MCMC 方法。

Conclusion: GRiLS 有效解决了多峰采样中模态捕获问题，尤其适用于梯度不可用或计算昂贵的复杂目标分布。

Abstract: We address the problem of efficiently sampling multimodal probability distributions, where standard Markov Chain Monte Carlo methods often suffer from poor mixing and mode trapping. To mitigate these issues, we propose Gradient-free Riemannian Langevin Sampler (GRiLS), a novel proposal that improves exploration without requiring gradient evaluations of the target density. Our approach introduces a Riemannian metric which reshapes the local geometry in order to facilitate transitions across modes. The resulting gradient-free MCMC algorithm is particularly suitable for complex, computationally expensive targets where derivatives are unavailable or impractical. The GRiLS proposal requires knowing the mean and covariance of the target density, which we estimate using an ensemble of interacting particles. Empirical results on multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that GRiLS achieves improved mixing compared to existing gradient-based and gradient-free MCMC approaches.

</details>


### [220] [Selective Timestep Weighting and Advantage-Based Replay for Sample-Efficient Diffusion RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07693)
*Eric Zhu,Abhinav Shrivastava,Soumik Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补策略（逐时间步加权和优先回放机制）以提升扩散模型RLHF的反馈效率，在相同超参数下实现了高达6倍的样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型RLHF方法需要大量人类或奖励模型评估，反馈效率低下，限制了其在真实场景中的实用性。

Method: 1) 逐时间步加权方案：根据去噪时间步的信息量重新加权，并从理论上将该加权与PPO的最优收敛性建立联系；2) 优先回放机制：优先利用信息量大的轨迹，避免重复查询新奖励。

Result: 在相同超参数设置下，与广泛使用的扩散RLHF基线相比，样本效率提升高达6倍。

Conclusion: 通过强调信息量大的时间步和轨迹，可以显著提高扩散RLHF的反馈效率，同时保持对未见提示的泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for aligning generative models with human preferences. However, applying RLHF to diffusion models remains highly feedback inefficient, as existing approaches typically require large amounts of human or reward model evaluations. This limitation reduces the practicality of diffusion RLHF in realworld settings where feedback is the primary bottleneck. In this paper, we propose two complementary strategies that substantially improve the feedback efficiency of diffusion RLHF while preserving generalization to unseen prompts. Our key observation is that reward information in diffusion trajectories is unevenly distributed: not all denoising timesteps or trajectories contribute equally to learning from a reward signal. By emphasizing informative timesteps and trajectories during optimization, we obtain more effective gradient updates. First, we introduce a per-timestep weighting scheme that reweights denoising steps during policy optimization. We theoretically connect this weighting to the optimal convergence properties of proximal policy optimization (PPO) and approximate the resulting weighting trend empirically. Second, we introduce a replay mechanism that prioritizes informative trajectories, enabling the model to reuse past samples instead of repeatedly querying new rewards. Together, these strategies significantly improve the feedback efficiency of diffusion RLHF. Under identical hyperparameter settings, our approach achieves up to a 6$\times$ improvement in sample efficiency compared to widely used diffusion RLHF baselines.

</details>


### [221] [Avoiding unsafe sets when training with Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07538)
*Adam M. Oberman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了强凸损失函数下噪声梯度下降（过阻尼Langevin动力学）的路径安全性问题，给出了轨迹落入失效区域的概率上界。主要结论包括：平衡态下失效概率随维度指数衰减；轨迹概率以全局谱间隙为速率松弛到静态值，但需要维度量级的预热时间；通过引入局部松弛率，对几何隔离区域可缩短预热时间并实现均匀概率上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在噪声梯度下降训练的神经网络中，轨迹可能意外进入失效区域（如导致错误预测的参数空间），需要量化这种风险的概率上界，以确保训练过程的安全性。

Method: 将带有噪声的梯度下降理想化为过阻尼Langevin动力学，利用谱理论、最大原理和Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程示例，从全局谱间隙和局部松弛率两个角度分析失效概率的动力学行为。

Result: 1）平衡态失效概率π(A_H)随维度d指数衰减，且在小噪声下具有能量势垒率；2）轨迹概率满足形状无关的上界，预热时间约O(d)；3）对几何隔离区域，局部松弛率可超过全局速率，显著缩短预热时间；4）OU示例表明平衡态壳层的角切片可产生指数级瞬态膨胀。

Conclusion: 强凸性决定了训练的松弛速度，但失效区域的几何形状决定了轨迹是否会中途"鼓包"穿过该区域。局部松弛率的引入为理解非平衡态下的安全风险提供了更精细的工具。

Abstract: Training a model with noisy gradient descent can be idealized as overdamped Langevin dynamics on the loss landscape, and a natural safety question is to bound the probability $ν_t(\mathcal{A}_H) = \mathbb{P}(Q_t \in \mathcal{A}_H)$ that the trajectory lies in a designated failure region $\mathcal{A}_H$. We study this for a smooth, strongly convex loss in $d$ dimensions and a failure region separated from the minimizer by an energy gap. Three bounds emerge. At the end of training, the equilibrium mass $π(\mathcal{A}_H)$ is exponentially small in $d$, with a complementary energy-barrier rate when the noise is small. Along the trajectory, a shape-free bound $ν_t(\mathcal{A}_H) \le π(\mathcal{A}_H)(1 + \sqrt{χ_0^2/π(\mathcal{A}_H)}\,e^{-mt})$ shows that the in-set probability relaxes to (twice) the static value after a burn-in time of order $d$, using only the global spectral gap $m$ of the loss. A worked Ornstein-Uhlenbeck example shows this burn-in is necessary: an angular slice of the equilibrium shell can transiently swell by a factor exponential in $d$, even though its equilibrium mass is tiny. To rule such swelling out we introduce a local relaxation rate attached to the failure region, defined through the spectral measure of its centered indicator rather than a Dirichlet-form Rayleigh quotient. For geometrically isolated regions this rate exceeds the global one, shrinking the burn-in proportionally, and combined with a maximum-principle ceiling it caps the trajectory probability uniformly in time. The picture is that strong convexity sets how fast training relaxes, but the shape of the unsafe set decides whether the trajectory bulges through it on the way home.

</details>


### [222] [Collaborative Synthetic Data Generation for Knowledge Transfer in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07565)
*Maximilian Andreas Hoefler,Karsten Mueller,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedKT-CSD是一个单轮联邦学习框架，利用预训练自编码器将客户端私有数据压缩为类条件潜在统计量，通过安全聚合和差分隐私噪声添加，在服务器端解码生成合成数据集以训练全局模型，在保证隐私的前提下实现低通信开销和对数据异质性的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单轮联邦学习方法缺乏正式隐私保证，无法同时实现低通信开销、对数据异质性鲁棒性和严格隐私保护，存在研究空白。

Method: 利用公共预训练自编码器作为共享潜空间，客户端单次前向编码私有数据并计算类条件潜统计量；服务器通过安全聚合统计量，添加校准的差分隐私噪声，再解码生成合成数据集，用于训练全局模型和下游任务。

Result: 在隐私约束下，FedKT-CSD在多种数据集和异质性设置下与甚至超越非隐私基线方法，且可扩展至大量客户端。

Conclusion: FedKT-CSD弥合了单轮联邦学习中低通信、异质性鲁棒性和严格隐私之间的差距，通过神经图像压缩启发式设计实现了形式化的差分隐私保证。

Abstract: One-shot federated learning (OSFL) addresses the communication overhead of federated learning by limiting training to a single round, but doing so without sacrificing model quality is non-trivial, particularly when client data distributions diverge. Recent work has addressed this challenge by aggregating client knowledge on the server through the construction of transferable synthetic datasets or distillates. However, most of these methods lack formal privacy guarantees, leaving a gap in jointly achieving low communication, robustness to heterogeneity, and rigorous privacy. We propose FedKT-CSD (Federated Knowledge Transfer via Collaborative Synthetic Data), a framework inspired by neural image compression that closes this gap by leveraging publicly pretrained autoencoders as a shared latent space. Each client encodes its private data in a single forward pass, computes class-conditional latent statistics, and transmits these to the server. The server aggregates these statistics via secure aggregation, adds calibrated differential privacy noise, and decodes a synthetic dataset for training a global model and further downstream tasks. This design provides formal $(\varepsilon,δ)$-differential privacy by construction, while keeping client-side computation and communication lightweight. Despite operating under privacy constraints, FedKT-CSD is competitive with and even outperforms non-private baselines across diverse datasets and heterogeneity settings, and scales to a large number of clients. Our code is available at: https://github.com/an7123/FedKT-CSD

</details>


### [223] [Multi-Class vs. Multi-Label BERT for CVE-to-CWE Mapping: How Taxonomy Structure Shapes the Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07573)
*Ana Schwengber Kelm,Christian Bockermann,Jörg Frochte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将CVE记录自动分类到CWE类别的任务，比较了多类与多标签两种建模策略，以及三种Transformer编码器在三个嵌套标签空间上的表现。结果表明多类训练在宏观F1上整体更优，但多标签在后处理优化后可缩小差距；错误结构主要受CWE层次设计影响而非编码器选择；CySecBERT在多数设置下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CWE分类是漏洞分析中的重要步骤，但目前主要依赖人工完成，效率低下。本文旨在探索利用文本分类方法自动将CVE记录分配到CWE类别，以减少人工工作量。

Method: 将任务形式化为文本分类问题，对比多类（单标签）和多标签两种建模方式。使用BERT Base、SecureBERT和CySecBERT三种Transformer编码器，在83、47、25三类标签空间上评估。多标签端采用后验阈值优化，并进行层次松弛评估。

Result: 多类训练在所有设置下宏观F1更高，但随标签空间缩小，与多标签的差距从21个百分点降至2个百分点。多标签的阈值优化在25类设置下消除了这一差距。混淆分析显示三种编码器的误分类模式高度相关（Pearson r > 0.92）。层次松弛评估后宏观F1从~81%提升至~90%。CySecBERT在多标签设置下表现最优。

Conclusion: 错误结构主要由CWE层次设计驱动而非编码器选择；严格的评价指标低估了分支级别的分类质量；CySecBERT在漏洞分析领域表现突出。

Abstract: Assigning Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) categories to Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) records remains an important but largely manual step in vulnerability analysis. We study this task as a text classification problem and compare two modelling choices: a \emph{multi-class} formulation that predicts a single CWE per CVE and a \emph{multi-label} formulation that allows multiple assignments. Three transformer encoders (BERT Base, SecureBERT, and CySecBERT) are evaluated on three nested label spaces (83, 47, and 25 classes). Multi-class training achieves higher macro-F1 across all settings, although the gap to multi-label narrows from 21 to 2 percentage points as the label space shrinks. Post-hoc threshold optimisation on the multi-label side closes this gap on the 25-class setting. Confusion analysis shows that the dominant misclassification patterns follow the CWE hierarchy and are shared across all three encoders (Pearson $r > 0.92$), which suggests that the error structure is driven more by taxonomy design than by encoder choice. A hierarchy-relaxed evaluation that forgives within-family confusions raises macro-F1 from ${\sim}$81\% to ${\sim}$90\%, indicating that strict metrics understate branch-level classifier quality. CySecBERT achieves the strongest results overall, with statistically significant gains concentrated in the multi-label setting.

</details>


### [224] [Asymmetric Focal Loss Improves Graph Neural Network Prediction of Drug-Drug Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07611)
*Faranak Hatami,Mousa Moradi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在TWOSIDES数据集上，将非对称聚焦损失（ClinicalFocal loss）集成到关系感知图卷积网络中，在相同实验条件下相比标准二元交叉熵，准确率从0.699提升至0.892（+19.3个百分点），F1从0.700提升至0.894，AUROC从0.766提升至0.914，假阴性率从29.8%降至9.1%，分类误差相对降低64.1%，表明损失函数设计是提升图神经网络药物相互作用预测性能的直接可调杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准二元交叉熵训练对已正确分类样本和困难样本分配相同的模型容量，可能导致遗漏临床显著的药物相互作用，因此需要一种能够强调困难正例的损失函数来改进多关系药物相互作用预测。

Method: 将ClinicalFocal损失（非对称聚焦目标函数）集成到关系感知图卷积网络中，使用分子指纹、理化描述符和学习嵌入作为特征，在TWOSIDES数据集上采用五折交叉验证，在架构、特征、数据划分、超参数和随机种子完全相同的条件下与二元交叉熵基线进行比较。

Result: ClinicalFocal损失相比基线：准确率从0.699提升至0.892（+19.3%），F1从0.700提升至0.894（+19.4%），AUROC从0.766提升至0.914，AUCPR从0.714提升至0.860，假阴性率从29.8%降至9.1%，特异度从69.6%提升至87.5%，分类误差从30.1%降至10.8%（相对降低64.1%），所有五折结果一致改善。

Conclusion: 非对称聚焦优化在不修改底层架构的情况下提升了分类和排序性能，对观测到的相互作用三元组实现了90.9%的召回率；损失函数设计是改进基于图的药物相互作用预测的直接、可调杠杆。

Abstract: Background: Graph neural networks improve computational prediction of polypharmacy side effects, but standard binary cross-entropy training allocates equal capacity to well-classified and difficult examples, potentially missing clinically significant interactions. We evaluated whether an asymmetric focal objective could improve multi-relational drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction by emphasizing difficult positive interactions. Methods: ClinicalFocal loss was integrated into a relation-aware graph convolutional network using molecular fingerprints, physicochemical descriptors, and learned embeddings. The model was evaluated on TWOSIDES using five-fold cross-validation with identical experimental conditions (architecture, features, data partitions, hyperparameters, and random seeds) for ClinicalFocal loss and binary cross-entropy baseline. Results: ClinicalFocal loss increased accuracy from 0.699 to 0.892 (+19.3 percentage points) and F1 score from 0.700 to 0.894 (+19.4 percentage points). AUROC increased from 0.766 to 0.914, and AUCPR increased from 0.714 to 0.860. The false-negative rate decreased from 29.8% to 9.1%, while specificity increased from 69.6% to 87.5%. Overall classification error decreased from 30.1% to 10.8%, corresponding to a 64.1% relative reduction. Improvements were consistent across all five folds. Conclusions: Asymmetric focal optimization improved classification and ranking performance while achieving 90.9% recall for observed interaction triples, without modifying the underlying architecture. Loss-function design is a direct, tunable lever for improving graph-based DDI prediction.

</details>


### [225] [Higher-Order Geometric Updates for Levenberg-Marquardt Method via Riemann Normal Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07623)
*Jianing Liu,Dong H. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出黎曼正规坐标莱文贝格-马夸尔特方法(RNC-LM)，通过构建高阶测地线修正来消除参数效应曲率，使有限步优化与线性模型预测更一致，在经典基准、PINN失效模式和势能面拟合任务上显著提升收敛性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LM方法在参数坐标中应用切空间步长，受参数效应曲率主导非线性影响；测地线加速度仅在无穷小步长下精确消除该曲率，有限步长时一致性不足。

Method: 重新表述测地线方程，将测地线加速度推广至任意阶修正；构建具有渐近高重参数化一致性的有限步更新；沿RNC曲线进行线搜索控制步长，保持计算成本接近标准LM；在移动切框架中逐阶消除残差加速度的切向分量。

Result: 在经典非线性最小二乘基准中改善了弯曲山谷和秩亏问题的收敛性与鲁棒性；在反应扩散PINN失效模式基准上将相对L2误差降至1e-3量级并恢复物理意义解；在大规模机器学习势能面拟合任务中实现较标准LM 34倍加速。

Conclusion: RNC-LM通过高阶几何修正有效克服参数效应曲率限制，在多种非线性优化场景中显著优于标准LM，兼具理论优雅性与实际高效性。

Abstract: Nonlinear least-squares optimization is central to regression, physics-informed neural networks, and other machine-learning tasks. Such problems have a natural geometric interpretation, model predictions form a manifold in data space, while the chosen parameterization can introduce parameter-effects curvature that becomes a dominant source of nonlinearity. This exposes a limitation of the Levenberg-Marquardt (LM) method, its tangent-space step is applied as a straight update in parameter coordinates. Geodesic acceleration gives a second-order correction, but its removal of parameter-effect curvature is exact only in the infinitesimal-step limit. We propose a Riemann-normal-coordinate Levenberg-Marquardt method (RNC-LM) to improve this consistency for finite optimization steps. By reformulating the geodesic equation, RNC-LM extends geodesic acceleration to arbitrary-order corrections and constructs finite-step updates with progressively higher reparameterization consistency. A line search along the resulting RNC curve controls the traveled distance while keeping the cost close to standard LM. The method eliminates the tangential component of residual acceleration order by order in a moving tangent frame, making the actual objective reduction more consistent with the linear model prediction of LM. On classical nonlinear least-squares benchmarks, RNC-LM improves convergence and robustness in curved valleys and rank-deficient problems. On a reaction-diffusion PINN failure-mode benchmark, it reduces the relative L2 error to the order of 1e-3 and recovers a physically meaningful solution. On a large-scale machine-learning potential-energy-surface fitting task, it achieves a 34-fold speedup over standard LM.

</details>


### [226] [An optimal control approach for neural network architecture adaptation with a posteriori error estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07637)
*C G Krishnanunni,Thomas Scott,Tan Bui-Thanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于后验误差估计的神经网络深度自适应方法，通过将网络训练建模为连续时间最优控制问题，推导了层间近似误差的显式上界，并利用对偶加权残差方法实现可计算的误差分解，从而指导在误差最大处插入新层。在Navier-Stokes方程等科学数据集上，该方法在泛化性能上优于现有架构自适应方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络的深度设计往往依赖经验或启发式方法，缺乏严格的理论指导。本文旨在通过数学上严格的误差估计，为网络架构的深度自适应提供理论基础，使网络能够在最需要的地方增加容量，从而高效捕获复杂非线性变化。

Method: 将神经网络训练形式化为连续时间最优控制问题；基于对偶加权残差（DWR）方法推导层间近似误差的可计算上界；将权重和偏置建模为随层变化的逐段线性函数；根据误差分解结果，在估计误差最大的位置插入新层，实现深度自适应增长。

Result: 该方法在科学数据集上（包括Navier-Stokes方程的可观测-参数映射学习）进行了验证，数值结果表明其泛化性能一致优于现有架构自适应方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了将连续最优控制理论与有限元分析中的后验误差估计技术相结合，能够为神经网络深度自适应提供严格且有效的方法论，显著提升模型在科学计算任务中的泛化能力。

Abstract: This work presents a novel approach for adapting neural network architecture along the depth based on a posteriori error estimation. By formulating neural network training as a continuous-time optimal control problem, we derive rigorous error estimates that quantify how approximation error distributes across network layers. This error decomposition enables a principled depth adaptation strategy: new layers are inserted at locations of maximum estimated error, allowing the network to efficiently capture complex, nonlinear variations in the underlying problem. Our framework introduces a novel network architecture that treats weights and biases as piecewise linear functions varying across layers, with the error estimator bounding the discrepancy between this discrete representation and the true continuous optimal control solution. The approach leverages dual weighted residual methodology from finite element analysis to derive computable upper bounds on the functional error. A key theoretical contribution is the derivation of explicit error bounds that decompose the total approximation error into interval-wise contributions, providing a rigorous basis for targeted architecture refinement. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on scientific datasets, including learning the observable-to-parameter map for the Navier-Stokes equation. Numerical results reveal that our approach consistently outperforms existing architecture adaptation methods in terms of generalization performance.

</details>


### [227] [ALER-TI: Aligned Latent Embedding Retrieval for Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07640)
*Xuan-Thong Truong,Trung-Kien Le,Tung Kieu,Thi-Thu Nguyen,Nhat-Hai Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ALER-TI是一种检索增强的时间序列插补框架，通过对齐历史潜在嵌入来补充缺失的局部上下文信息，从而在非平稳、弱时序相关等挑战场景下实现更可靠的缺失值重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列插补方法主要依赖损坏输入序列中的局部时序上下文，这在非平稳动态、弱时序相关和低频模式等现实场景中效果有限，难以仅从邻近观测值可靠地重建缺失值。

Method: 提出ALER-TI检索增强框架，核心是潜在嵌入对齐（LEA）模块：通过在潜在空间施加后验掩码，对齐损坏查询与完整历史候选的表示分布，同时允许历史嵌入预计算和缓存以实现高效检索；该框架是模型无关的，可通过轻量适配模块集成到各种插补基座模型。

Result: 在六个真实世界数据集、不同缺失率下的实验表明，ALER-TI能够持续提升强基线模型的表现，并在多种插补设置下增强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 检索增强范式通过显式利用历史模式，能够有效弥补局部退化上下文的不足，为时间序列插补提供了一种可靠且通用的改进途径。

Abstract: Deep learning has significantly advanced time series imputation, yet most existing architectures primarily rely on localized temporal context within the corrupted input sequence. This reliance can be limiting in real-world scenarios, where time series often exhibit non-stationary dynamics, weak temporal correlations, and infrequent patterns that are difficult to reconstruct from nearby observations alone. In this paper, we propose ALER-TI, Aligned Latent Embedding Retrieval for Time Series Imputation, a retrieval-augmented framework that explicitly leverages historical patterns to supplement degraded local context for more reliable missing-value reconstruction. The core of ALER-TI is Latent Embedding Alignment (LEA), which mitigates the representation mismatch between corrupted queries and complete historical candidates. By applying post-hoc masking in the latent space, LEA aligns candidates with the query's missingness pattern while allowing historical embeddings to be pre-computed and cached for efficient retrieval. ALER-TI is model-agnostic and can be integrated with various imputation backbones through a lightweight adaptation module. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets under different missing rates demonstrate that ALER-TI consistently improves strong baseline models and enhances robustness across diverse imputation settings.

</details>


### [228] [Guidance Breaks the Fitted Operator: A Terminal-Fitted Repair for Classifier-Free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07665)
*Shiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过渐近保持数值分析视角，揭示了无分类器引导（CFG）在扩散模型中的过饱和和不稳定问题源于DDIM步长在引导下失去拟合特性，并提出了一个零额外NFE的修复方案：将CFG的w(r-1)替换为r^(1+w)-r，该修复能消除残差发散、稳定高引导采样，并在CIFAR-10和Stable Diffusion 1.5上取得9/9的FID优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFG在大引导强度下会出现过饱和和不稳定现象，现有方法通过增加步数或限制时间区间来缓解，但缺乏理论理解和根本性解决方案。

Method: 采用渐近保持数值分析框架，证明DDIM在无引导时是终端层的唯一拟合算子，而引导会以指数1+w使判别子空间重新刚化，导致DDIM不再拟合且引导残差随sigma_min趋于零而发散。基于此推导出引导时钟屏障的三个有序步长阈值，并提出修复公式r^(1+w)-r。

Result: 修复方法在CIFAR-10和Stable Diffusion 1.5上实现了9/9的点位FID胜率，有效削减残差放大和过饱和，在硬性细胞块中保持分类器代理目标精度。但并非普适性图像质量提升，也不是对稠密vanilla-CFG参考的均匀更优积分器。

Conclusion: CFG的过饱和是求解器伪影而非连续引导律本身的问题，所提修复可作为高引导稳定性增强器而非通用质量调节旋钮。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is the standard way to strengthen class-conditioning in diffusion and flow-matching samplers, yet at large guidance it oversaturates and destabilizes, symptoms practitioners suppress with more steps or limited-interval schedules. We analyze CFG through an asymptotic-preserving, numerical-analysis lens. Building on a recent result that the deterministic DDIM step is the unique fitted operator for the unguided terminal layer, exact on the final small-sigma stretch of sampling, we show that guidance re-stiffens exactly the discriminative subspace to an anomalous exponent 1+w. DDIM is therefore no longer fitted there, and on coarse meshes its guided residual diverges as sigma_min goes to zero. We prove a guided clock barrier with three ordered step-size thresholds, and read one-step oversaturation as its endpoint: a solver artifact on the calibration model rather than the continuous guided law. The same analysis yields a one-coefficient, zero-extra-NFE repair: replace CFG's w(r-1) by r^(1+w)-r on the guidance direction. On the calibration model's discriminative crossover, this removes CFG's sigma_min-divergent blow-up and is first-order accurate against the exact guided flow as sigma_min goes to zero. On learned CIFAR-10 checkpoints, and as a cross-domain smoke test on Stable Diffusion 1.5 DDIM, it acts as a high-guidance stabilizer at no extra cost rather than a universal quality knob: it cuts residual amplification and saturation, gives 9/9 point-FID wins over CFG on the tested grid, and preserves classifier-proxy target accuracy in the hard-cell blocks. We report the limits alongside: it is not a universal image-quality win, and against a dense vanilla-CFG reference it is not a uniformly better integrator of that field.

</details>


### [229] [PeTeR: Post-Training Robustification of Probabilistic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07671)
*Adrian Ciotinga,Yeming Dai,YooJung Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PeTeR框架，一种无需数据即可对预训练概率电路进行后训练鲁棒化的方法，能有效抵御分布偏移，无需从头重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准似然概率电路学习在面对数据噪声、小样本或分布偏移时容易过拟合和泛化脆弱。现有分布鲁棒优化方法需要从头训练，效率低下。

Method: 提出PeTeR（Post-training Robustification），一种无数据的后训练框架，通过在Wasserstein球最坏情况分布下对预训练PC进行鲁棒化，无需重新训练。

Result: 在多个密度估计基准上，PeTeR有效提升了基线模型对随机和对抗扰动的鲁棒性，性能达到或优于需要数据的鲁棒学习基线方法。

Conclusion: PeTeR是一种高效的无数据后训练方法，可在不重新训练的情况下增强预训练概率电路对分布偏移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Probabilistic circuits (PCs) can model complex joint distributions while supporting exact and efficient computation of many inference queries. However, standard likelihood-based PC learning is vulnerable to overfitting and fragile generalization when confronted with data noise, small sample sizes, or distribution shifts. This can be mitigated using distributionally-robust optimization which consider worst-case distributions within a Wasserstein ball of the empirical distribution, but current methods are limited to training a model from scratch in this framework. Instead, we propose PeTeR: a novel, data-free post-training framework designed to robustify pre-trained PCs against distribution shifts without retraining from scratch. Empirical evaluations across multiple density estimation benchmarks demonstrate that PeTeR effectively robustifies baseline models against both random and adversarial perturbations, achieving competitive or superior performance to data-dependent robust learning baselines.

</details>


### [230] [How Data Shapes RoPE Frequency Usage: From Positional Scale Matching to Length Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07678)
*Xinyi Wu,Siyuan Liu,Ali Jadbabaie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE)中的位置频率并非均匀使用，而是由训练数据的相对距离结构决定。通过"场-分辨率权衡"理论，提出最优频率与数据依赖宽度W成反比（频率匹配原则），并解释了语言模型中低频带出现的成因。进一步将频率选择与位置插值的长上下文泛化联系起来，证明自然语言具有跨位置尺度的近似自相似性，因此测试时缩放频率可支持长上下文泛化。最终指出长上下文泛化依赖于两种尺度匹配：学习频率与训练依赖的匹配，以及频率缩放与依赖扩展方式的匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究RoPE中位置频率非均匀使用的内在机制，以及该机制对长上下文泛化的影响。

Method: 通过理论分析提出"场-分辨率权衡"和频率匹配原则，在合成数据和文本数据上进行受控实验验证，并分析自然语言的自相似性特征。

Result: 1）RoPE频率选择由训练数据的依赖宽度W决定，最优频率与1/W成正比；2）语言模型中的中低频带源于自然语言的多尺度依赖结构；3）自然语言具有近似自相似性，使得测试时频率缩放有效。

Conclusion: 长上下文泛化依赖于两种尺度匹配：学习频率与训练依赖的匹配，以及频率缩放与依赖扩展方式的匹配。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) provide transformers with a fixed grid of positional frequencies, yet trained models use these frequencies highly non-uniformly. We study what determines this frequency usage and propose a data-centered explanation: RoPE frequencies are selected to match the relative-distance structure of the training data. Viewing each frequency as a positional lens, we formalize a field-resolution tradeoff and show that, for a data-induced dependency profile of width $W$, the optimal frequency scales as $1/W$. This frequency-matching principle explains controlled observations on synthetic and text-based data, and suggests that the mid-low frequency bands observed in language models arise from the multi-scale dependency structure of natural language. We further connect frequency selection to position-interpolation-based length generalization: scaling frequencies down expands the effective field while reducing resolution. This helps when longer-context dependencies are approximate dilations of those seen during training, but can fail when relevant dependencies do not scale with context length. Empirically, we show that natural language exhibits approximate self-similarity across positional scales, explaining why test-time frequency scaling can support long-context generalization. Overall, our results identify a data-driven mechanism behind emergent RoPE frequency usage and show that long-context generalization depends on two forms of scale matching: between learned frequencies and training-time dependencies, and between frequency scaling and how those dependencies extend to longer contexts.

</details>


### [231] [Neural Operator-enabled Topology-informed Evolutionary Strategy for PDE-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07682)
*Xiangming Huang,Guannan Zhang,Lu Lu,Raphaël Pestourie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NOTES框架，将神经算子(DeepONet)与进化策略(CMA-ES)结合，在紧凑潜在空间中进行全局优化。在纳米光子束偏转器逆设计中，将设计维度从256降至25，效率超95%；在结构优化中实现246的柔度，优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏微分方程控制的物理系统逆设计面临高维和非凸设计空间的挑战。生成模型缺乏鲁棒性和可迁移性，进化策略虽鲁棒但在高维空间中表现不佳，亟需一种兼具效率、鲁棒性和可迁移性的逆设计方法。

Method: NOTES将DeepONet神经算子与CMA-ES进化策略耦合，在编码拓扑感知先验的紧凑潜在空间中执行全局优化，通过降维、表示学习和进化优化实现高效且可迁移的逆设计。

Result: 在纳米光子束偏转器逆设计中，设计维度从256降至25，效率超95%，优于CMA-ES、拓扑优化等基线；在结构优化中，设计柔度达246。

Conclusion: 通过将DeepONet的拓扑学习与PDE求解器中的物理规律解耦，NOTES为物理系统逆设计提供了灵活且可迁移的框架，实现了高效、鲁棒的全局优化。

Abstract: The inverse design of physical systems governed by partial differential equations is computationally demanding due to the high dimensionality and non-convexity of design spaces. Generative models for inverse design often lack robustness and transferability, whereas evolutionary strategies are robust but struggle in high-dimensional spaces. This paper introduces a Neural Operator-enabled Topology-informed Evolutionary Strategy (NOTES) that integrates dimensionality reduction, representation learning, and evolutionary optimization for efficient and transferable inverse design. NOTES couples a DeepONet-based neural operator with the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES) to perform global optimization in a compact latent space that encodes topology-aware priors while discovering high-performance designs for unseen operating conditions. Applied to nanophotonic beam-deflector inverse design governed by Maxwell's equations, NOTES reduces the design dimensionality from 256 to 25 and consistently achieves over 95 percent efficiency, outperforming CMA-ES, topology optimization, and other baselines. Applied to structural optimization, NOTES discovers designs that achieve compliance down to 246. By decoupling topology learning of a DeepONet from the governing physics in a PDE solver, NOTES provides a flexible and transferable framework for the inverse design of physical systems.

</details>


### [232] [ECGLight: Compute-Light Framework For Paper ECG Digitization and Myocardial Infarction Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07683)
*Shreyasvi Natraj,Cyrus Achtari,Felice Gragnano,Andrea Milzi,Marco Valgimigli,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级端到端流水线，可将纸质心电图照片/扫描件转化为校准的12导联信号并检测心肌梗死，在仅CPU条件下30秒内完成，准确率达95.51%，无需高速网络或高端计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏远诊所因缺乏连接和计算能力仍使用纸质心电图，导致无法被基于AI的决策支持系统访问，造成急性冠状动脉闭塞等病症被漏诊、再灌注治疗延迟。现有工作将数字化和诊断分离处理，缺乏轻量级、设备端的一体化框架。

Method: 构建端到端轻量级设备端流水线：将纸质ECG照片/扫描件转换为校准的12导联信号，利用SHAP进行可解释性分析，筛查心肌梗死病理。模型在CPU上运行，单次处理<30秒。

Result: 基于PTB-XL数据集（21,799份ECG）训练评估，并在医院获取的ECG-Matrix数据集上进一步验证。MI检测准确率95.51%（F1=0.9519），OMI检测准确率88.89%（F1=0.8862）。

Conclusion: 表明在缺乏数字ECG导出、网络连接或高端计算资源的地区，可通过该框架可靠地利用传统纸质记录，提供可扩展的决策支持。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) is one of the most widely used tests for diagnosing cardiovascular disease. Yet several remote clinics still utilize paper ECG printouts for their analysis due to limited connectivity and computational capacity. As a result, vast numbers of physical ECGs obtained in remote areas still remain incapable of being accessed by contemporary artificial-intelligence (AI)-based decision support as they require high computational resources or strong high-speed internet connectivity. This causes several cases where conditions like acute coronary occlusion (ACS) is overlooked and reperfusion therapy delayed. Although prior work has tackled digitization and diagnosis separately, and utilized advanced AI models for them, there still remains a lack of a compute-light, on-device framework that reconstructs paper ECGs at high fidelity, while accurately supporting multiple clinically relevant endpoints. We address this need with an end-to-end lightweight on-device digitization-to-diagnosis pipeline that converts a smartphone photo or scan of a paper ECG into a calibrated 12-lead signal and screens for Myocardial Infarction (MI) pathologies, with SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) to support interpretability. Trained and evaluated on 21,799 ECGs from the PTB-XL dataset and further validated on hospital-acquired ECG-Matrix dataset, the complete system runs in <30 s per ECG on CPU-only resources, achieving 95.51% accuracy (F1 = 0.9519) for MI detection on PTB-XL and 88.89% accuracy (F1 = 0.8862) for OMI detection on ECG-Matrix. This work showcases that legacy paper records can be reliably democratized in any part of the world, providing a scalable decision support when digital ECG export, connectivity, or high-end compute are unavailable

</details>


### [233] [The Key to Going Linear: Analysis-Driven Transformer Linearization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07706)
*Anna Kuzina,Paul N. Whatmough,Babak Ehteshami Bejnordi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文研究了如何通过后验线性化方法解决因果自注意力的二次成本瓶颈，在冻结骨干网络条件下分离了状态更新设计的影响，揭示了softmax依赖于关键驱动的秩1正交投影，并引入结构干预（sink tokens、短卷积和固定预算缓存路由）来减少近似误差，在LLaMA和Qwen模型上扩展到32B参数，在MMLU上超越先前基线，并在长上下文检索上匹配复杂自适应缓存框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果自注意力的二次计算成本严重制约了长上下文Transformer推理，现有后验线性化方法众多但难以判断哪些组件能保持模型质量，因此需要在严格的冻结骨干网络条件下系统分析状态更新设计的影响。

Method: 在冻结骨干网络的严格条件下，隔离状态更新设计的影响；分析softmax依赖关键驱动的秩1正交投影机制；引入结构干预措施：sink tokens、短卷积和固定预算缓存路由来减少近似误差。

Result: 在LLaMA和Qwen模型上扩展到32B参数，在MMLU基准上超越先前后验基线方法，在长上下文检索任务上匹配复杂自适应缓存框架的性能。

Conclusion: 揭示了delta风格网络优于纯门控累积的原因（softmax的关键驱动秩1正交投影机制），识别了近似误差的潜在来源，并通过结构干预有效缩小了残差，证明了后验线性化方法在大规模模型上的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: The quadratic cost of causal self-attention severely bottlenecks long-context transformer inference. While numerous post hoc linearization pipelines exist, it is difficult to identify which components preserve model quality. This work isolates the effect of state update design in a strict frozen-backbone regime. We show that softmax relies on key-dependent, rank-1 orthogonal projections, elucidating why delta-style networks outperform purely gated accumulation. We identify a potential source of approximation errors and introduce structural interventions, specifically sink tokens, short convolutions, and fixed-budget cache routing, which reduces the remaining gap. We scale this linearization approach across LLaMA and Qwen models up to 32B parameters, outperforming prior post hoc baselines on MMLU and matching the long-context retrieval of complex adaptive-caching frameworks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [AgentLens: Production-Assessed Trajectory Reviews for Coding Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06624)
*Andrey Podivilov,Vadim Lomshakov,Sergey Savin,Matvei Startsev,Roman Pozharskiy,Maksim Parshin,Sergey Nikolenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentLens 是一个面向交互式代码智能体的生产级基准测试，通过结合形式化验证和 LLM 生成的轨迹评估，全面衡量智能体在指令遵循、工具使用、自我验证、错误恢复和用户交互等多个维度的表现，并已用于实际产品开发中的模型诊断、版本对比和回归检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体基准测试仅以二元结果（通过/未通过）评估任务完成情况，忽略了智能体在执行过程中展现的关键能力（如指令遵循、工具使用、自我验证、错误恢复和用户交互），无法真实反映用户实际使用体验。

Method: AgentLens 采用双轨评估策略：(1) 形式化验证，对存在客观检查标准的任务进行自动判定；(2) LLM 生成的轨迹评审和并排比较，对每次运行生成可读性强的评分解释。该方法兼顾客观性和可解释性。

Result: AgentLens 不仅能够对模型进行排名，还能有效用于诊断模型行为、对比同一智能体的不同版本，并在夜间自动化评估管线中捕获产品回归问题。

Conclusion: AgentLens 是一个开源的、面向生产环境的交互式代码智能体评估基准，通过结合形式化验证与 LLM 轨迹分析，实现了超越简单二元判定的全面评估能力，且已在实际产品开发流程中得到验证。

Abstract: We present AgentLens, a production-assessed benchmark for interactive code agents. Most code-agent benchmarks reduce a run to a single bit -- did the task pass? -- but the people who actually use these agents experience the entire trajectory: how the agent follows instructions, uses its tools, verifies its own work, recovers from mistakes, and talks to them along the way. AgentLens evaluates that whole trajectory. It pairs formal verification, where an objective check exists, with LLM-written trajectory reviews and side-by-side comparisons, so that each run yields a readable explanation of why the score is what it is. This makes AgentLens useful for more than ranking models: we use it to diagnose model behavior, compare successive versions of our own agent, and catch product regressions in a nightly evaluation pipeline. We release the benchmark as open source at https://github.com/agent-lens/agent-lens-bench.

</details>


### [235] [When Does In-Context Search Help? A Sampling-Complexity Theory of Reflection-Driven Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06720)
*Yotam Wolf,Noam Wies,Amnon Shashua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析了LLM的上下文搜索（in-context search）机制，将其建模为对推理轨迹的近似后验推断。研究表明，当反思能够可靠定位早期错误时，上下文搜索可带来指数级改进，用多项式次数的顺序尝试解决零样本通过率指数级小的问题；否则对渐进性能无益。此外，这些增益是鲁棒且可学习的。研究还发现，在可验证奖励的强化学习阶段抽象下，最优策略扩展实现了相同的后验重加权规则，并在实际推理模型上验证了关键理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM通过扩展推理实现了上下文搜索（迭代生成、批判、修正），但其理论基础尚不明确。本文旨在建立上下文搜索的理论分析框架，解释其有效性、限制条件以及与传统采样策略的关系。

Method: 将上下文搜索建模为近似贝叶斯推断：基础模型定义先验，自我反思提供反馈用于后验更新。分析推理时采样复杂度（达到高成功概率所需的顺序尝试次数），并研究了强化学习中可验证奖励的stagewise抽象下的最优策略扩展。

Result: (1) 当反思能可靠定位早期错误时，上下文搜索可指数级改进基础模型，用多项式次数顺序尝试解决零样本通过率指数级小的问题。(2) 否则，基于过去尝试的条件采样与并行采样相比无渐进优势。(3) 近似后验更新足以实现这些增益，交叉熵训练可用多项式样本复杂度恢复所需行为。(4) 在RL设置下，最优策略扩展等价于后验重加权规则。在实际推理模型上验证了关键理论预测。

Conclusion: 上下文搜索的理论有效性依赖于反思定位早期错误的能力；当该条件满足时，顺序搜索可带来指数级加速；这些理论结果与真实LLM的行为一致，为理解扩展推理机制提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) with extended reasoning has enabled in-context search, in which models iteratively generate, critique, and revise solution attempts. We provide a theoretical analysis of in-context search by modeling it as approximate inference over reasoning traces, where the base model defines a prior and self-reflection provides feedback for posterior updates, and study the resulting inference-time sampling complexity - the number of sequential attempts needed to achieve high success probability. We show that when reflections reliably localize early mistakes, in-context search can yield exponential improvements over the base model, solving problems with exponentially small zero-shot pass rates using only a polynomial number of sequential attempts, whereas when this property fails, conditioning on past attempts offers no asymptotic benefit over parallel sampling. We further show that these gains are robust and learnable: approximate posterior updates suffice, and cross-entropy training on search rollouts recovers the required behavior with polynomial sample complexity. Finally, we show that under a stagewise abstraction of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, the optimal policy extension implements the same posterior reweighting rule. We validate key qualitative predictions of the theory on real large reasoning models.

</details>


### [236] [LLM-powered reasoning in agent-based modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06757)
*Sifat Afroj Moon,Dakotah Maguire,Adam Spannaus,Joe Tuccillo,Maksudul Alam,Sudip K. Seal,John Gounley,Heidi Hanson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HALE框架，将大语言模型与基于智能体的建模相结合，用于疫情模拟，使模型能根据实时人类决策动态调整，克服了传统ABM依赖静态先验的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于智能体的建模依赖静态先验知识，无法适应实时变化，存在信息缺口；需要一种能动态捕捉人类决策行为的方法来提高ABM在政策制定中的实用性。

Method: 提出HALE（Hybrid Agent-based and Language-driven Epidemic）框架，将大语言模型集成到ABM模拟中，利用LLM预测智能体的人类决策行为，实现可扩展的混合建模。

Result: 以犹他州盐湖县的新冠疫情模拟作为概念验证，展示了HALE框架在真实场景中的应用可行性。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的ABM能够突破传统静态先验的限制，为动态政策制定提供更灵活、适应性更强的建模工具。

Abstract: Agent-based modeling (ABM) has the capability to model millions of individuals and their interactions, which is useful for policy making. However, ABMs have traditionally relied on static prior, which prevents the models from adapting to real-time changes. Our research provides a novel approach to addressing this information gap. Large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to predict human decision-making. Here, we introduce a scalable Hybrid Agent-based and Language-driven Epidemic (HALE) modeling framework that leverages LLMs to predict human decision-making in an ABM simulation. As a proof-of-concept, we use HALE to simulate COVID-19 and its effects in Salt Lake County, UT.

</details>


### [237] [QANTIS: Hardware-Calibrated Sequential POMDP Belief Updates on IBM Heron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06760)
*Bayram Yuksel Eker,Suayb S. Arslan,Ozgur Nazli,Mustafa Serhat Demirgil,Furkan Deligoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在IBM Heron量子硬件上验证了QANTIS框架——将量子处理器作为信念更新服务，用于部分可观测环境下的序贯决策。通过对比无放大、Guarded Grover放大和全步固定点放大（FPAA）三种策略，证明FPAA能够在8步和12步Tiger POMDP轨迹中保持后验一致性，所有决策检查中硬件后验与精确贝叶斯后验选择的行动一致。边界感知的BIQAE稳定了近零和近一的振幅估计，稀有事件扫描映射了百万分之一证据的逻辑样本复杂度包络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证量子处理器能否作为校准的信念更新服务（belief-update primitive）被重复用于序贯POMDP决策，且不破坏面向规划器的后验概率质量，为未来量子辅助自主系统奠定硬件校准基础。

Method: 在IBM Heron硬件上，以Tiger POMDP为基准，分别执行无放大、Guarded Grover放大和全步固定点放大（FPAA）三种量子振幅估计策略；使用边界感知BIQAE（Boundary-aware BIQAE）稳定极端概率（近0或近1）的估计；通过稀有事件扫描映射逻辑样本复杂度包络。

Result: 全步FPAA在8步和12步主运行中保持Tiger后验一致性；20步和32步对照组仍在相同工作带内；所有决策检查中硬件后验与精确贝叶斯后验选择的即时行动完全一致。

Conclusion: 该研究界定了一个硬件校准的信念更新原语的操作包络（operating envelope），而非声称独立的硬件优势；量子处理器可以作为可靠的信念更新服务嵌入经典规划闭环。

Abstract: Autonomous systems under partial observability act on beliefs, not raw sensor events. QANTIS treats the quantum processor as a calibrated belief-update service in that loop: it receives a prior and an observation model, estimates the rare-event evidence term, and returns an ordinary posterior to a classical planner. This paper asks whether that service can be reused across a sequential Tiger POMDP horizon on present IBM Heron hardware without corrupting the planner-facing posterior. We answer with a controlled hardware case study rather than an end-to-end autonomy or wall-clock speedup claim. The study compares no amplification, guarded Grover amplification, and all-step fixed-point amplification on the same trajectory, then checks whether the returned posterior would change the downstream action. All-step FPAA preserves the Tiger posterior across the reported 8-step and 12-step primary runs, and the 20-step and 32-step controls remain inside the same operating band. In every reported decision check, the hardware posterior and the exact Bayes posterior select the same immediate action. Boundary-aware BIQAE stabilizes amplitude estimation near zero and near one, while a rare-event sweep maps the logical sample-complexity envelope for one-in-a-million evidence. The result is an operating envelope for a hardware-calibrated belief-update primitive, not a standalone hardware-advantage claim.

</details>


### [238] [Cost-Effective Agent Harnesses for Abstract Reasoning and Generalization on ARC-AGI-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06764)
*Kabir Moghe,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了第三种范式：在不使用基准专用微调或大量测试时计算的情况下，通过纯架构设计（非思考模式的DeepSeek V3.2）提升ARC-AGI-1性能。提出Explorer-Definer Pipeline（57.50% pass@2，$0.25/任务）和Reflective Orchestrator（67.25% pass@2，$0.62/任务），将单次基线提升约52个百分点。分析表明模型受限于生成能力而非排序能力，并验证了自适应重探索的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ARC-AGI-1解决方案主要依赖两种范式：前端模型的大量测试时计算（进化搜索、穷举采样、扩展思维链），或针对基准进行微调的小模型（通常使用任务特化架构）。本文探索第三种范式：使用开放权重模型（非思考模式）在严格预算下，不进行任何ARC专用微调，仅通过架构设计提升性能。

Method: 提出两阶段代理流水线：1) Explorer-Definer Pipeline，将模式发现与可执行变换合成明确分离；2) Reflective Orchestrator，在训练对上的先前假设失败时自主探索新变换。使用DeepSeek V3.2的非思考模式，无ARC专用微调。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-1公共400任务评估集上，Pipeline达到57.50% pass@2（$0.25/任务），Orchestrator达到67.25% pass@2（$0.62/任务），将15.50%的单次基线提升约52个百分点。消融实验表明"think tool"是关键组件，移除后pass@2降低5.75个百分点。

Conclusion: 模型是生成受限（generation-bound）而非选择受限（selection-bound），训练对准确率可捕获约95%的候选上限，改进方向应侧重于更广泛的生成而非更好的排序。Orchestrator通过自适应重探索验证了这一预测（无偏pass@1提升+9.81pp，与选择介导的pass@2提升匹配）。

Abstract: Recent progress on ARC-AGI-1 from disclosed architectures has come broadly from two regimes: heavy test-time compute over frontier models (evolutionary search, exhaustive sampling, extended chain-of-thought), or benchmark-specific training in which small models are fine-tuned on ARC data, often with task-specialized architectures. We study a third regime: an open-weight model in non-thinking mode (DeepSeek V3.2) under a strict budget, with no ARC-specific fine-tuning. We study what is recoverable through architecture alone, building agentic harnesses that decompose pattern-discovery and program-synthesis stages explicitly. First, we introduce an Explorer-Definer Pipeline that separates pattern discovery from executable transformation synthesis, implemented as a two-stage agent pipeline. Next, we present the Reflective Orchestrator, which augments the pipeline with autonomous exploration of new transformations when previous hypotheses fail on training pairs. On the ARC-AGI-1 public 400-task evaluation set, the pipeline reaches 57.50% pass@2 at \$0.25 per task, and the orchestrator reaches 67.25% pass@2 at \$0.62 per task. Together these architectures lift a 15.50% one-shot baseline by ~52 points without benchmark-specific training or heavy test-time compute. Furthermore, the orchestrator-driven lift tests a falsifiable diagnostic the pipeline produces; unbiased pass@k analysis suggests the pipeline is generation-bound, not selection-bound (selection via training-pair accuracy captures ~95% of the candidate ceiling) and predicts that significant improvement requires broader generation, not better ranking. The orchestrator implements this prediction via adaptive re-exploration and confirms it (unbiased pass@1 lift +9.81 pp, matching selection-mediated pass@2 lift). An additional pipeline ablation identifies its think tool as a significant component, with removal reducing pass@2 by 5.75 pp.

</details>


### [239] [Evaluating SageMath-Augmented LLM Agents for Computational and Experimental Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06820)
*Pavel Snopov,German Magai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合LLM推理与SageMath符号计算系统的ReAct风格智能体框架，通过Context7提供最新文档支持，在RealMath基准测试中评估了多个前沿模型。实验表明，接入SageMath使模型平均性能提升9.7个百分点（最高27.8pp），其中GPT-5.5达到75.2%的最高解决率和最低token消耗，Qwen 3.7-Max受益最大。研究还改进了RealMath基准的多步后处理和多阶段验证流程，证明CAS增强的智能体是辅助数学家计算探索的有前景方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI for Mathematics研究主要集中于自动形式化和定理证明，计算机代数系统（CAS）在智能体LLM工作流中的作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出ReAct风格智能体框架，将LLM推理与SageMath的符号计算反馈相结合，并使用Context7获取最新文档；对RealMath基准进行改进，引入多步后处理和多阶段验证流程。

Result: SageMath接入使所有评估模型平均性能提升9.7个百分点（1.5~27.8pp），缩小了开源模型与闭源模型之间的差距；Qwen 3.7-Max从SageMath获益最大，GPT-5.5达到75.2%最高解决率和最低token消耗。

Conclusion: CAS增强的智能体是辅助数学家计算探索的有前景方向，该工作是迈向自动化猜想发现的一步。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI for Mathematics have focused largely on autoformalization and theorem proving, leaving the role of Computer Algebra Systems (CAS) in agentic LLM workflows underexplored. We propose a ReAct-style agentic setup that combines LLM reasoning with verifiable feedback from SageMath, together with Context7 for the up-to-date documentation. We evaluate this agentic setup across frontier models for solving research-level mathematical problems from the RealMath benchmark in a setting that emulates a computational-mathematics research loop. We also propose a refinement to the RealMath benchmark by introducing a multi-step post-processing procedure and a multi-stage validation pipeline, both of which improve the quality and reliability of the extracted problem set. Our experiments reveal substantial performance gains from SageMath access across all evaluated models on +9.7~pp on average, the gains range from 1.5~pp to 27.8~pp and narrow the gap between open-weight and closed models. Qwen~3.7-Max benefits from SageMath the most, while GPT-5.5 achieves the highest solve rate of $75.2\%$ and the lowest token usage among tool-enabled configurations. Our findings suggest that CAS-augmented agents represent a promising direction for assisting mathematicians in computational exploration, and we believe that this work is a step towards automated conjecture discovery. The project repository is available online.

</details>


### [240] [The Harness Effect: How Orchestration Design Sets the Token Economics of Enterprise Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06906)
*Muayad Sayed Ali,Aliaksandra Novik,Anji Boddupally,Artem Yavorskyi,Chris Nickerson,Daniel Rica,Emily DuGranrut,Felix Leung,Garrett Prince,Grace Barnett,Heath Robinson,Hosain Al Ahmad,Jesse Resnick,Juan Carlos Farah,Jyothi Swaroop Meruga,Leonid Kuznetsov,Luke Gorham,Marie Schmoll,Michael Paciullo,Saumya Das,Sharath Sheripally,Tommy Griscom,Mykyta Osadchyi,Neha Mantri,Nick Westrum,Olivia Benowitz,Parikshith Kulkarni,Radik Chernyshov,Rakshith Vasudev,Rohith Nadimpally,Vikas Gangadevi,Waseem AlShikh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过控制变量实验证明，AI Agent的编排层（Harness）比底层模型更决定成本效率。在22个任务、6个模型上，仅更换编排层即可使每任务成本降低41%、时间缩短44%、令牌数减少38%，同时质量持平或略有提升。编排层效率提升与模型无关（所有模型成本下降33-61%），而质量提升与模型能力高度相关（r=0.99）。每美元质量提升82%，每百万令牌完成任务数从54.9增至92.0。编排层对每任务成本的影响超过了整个模型菜单的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agentic AI开发存在"令牌最大化"问题：通过消耗更多令牌（更长推理链、更多轮次、更大工具负载、更大上下文）来换取能力提升，导致每任务令牌消耗增速超过任务价值增长。虽然单令牌价格在下降，但总成本却在上升。亟需找到根本性杠杆来打破这一模式。

Method: 采用受控交换实验设计：22个锁定评估任务，6个基础模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.1, Gemini Flash 3.5, Qwen 3.6, GLM 5.1, Palmyra X6），仅改变编排层——冻结的传统生产循环 vs Writer Agent Harness。测量每任务成本、中位耗时、每任务令牌数、任务完成质量等指标。

Result: 编排层使混合成本每任务降低41%（$0.21→$0.12），中位耗时降低44%（48s→27s），每任务令牌减少38%（14.2k→8.8k），任务完成质量持平（0.78→0.81）。所有模型成本下降33-61%，质量提升与模型基线能力近乎完美相关（r=0.99）。每美元质量提升82%，每百万令牌完成任务数从54.9升至92.0。编排层对成本的影响超过模型菜单全范围差异。

Conclusion: 编排层是AI Agent系统中效率倍增的关键组件，其效率提升跨所有模型（当前和未来）有效。提出了"编排杠杆"（harness leverage）现象：模型越强，从编排优化中获得的收益越大。将令牌经济学形式化到编排层（含提示缓存的等效输入价格），详述了六类机制（从缓存形状约束到失败支出治理），并对比了六种主流Agent系统。

Abstract: Agentic AI development today runs on token maxing: buying capability with tokens -- longer reasoning traces, more turns, wider tool payloads, bigger replayed contexts -- so tokens per task grow faster than task value. Falling per-token prices mask the pattern; total spend rises anyway. We argue the decisive lever against token maxing is the harness: the orchestration layer that assembles context, exposes tools, sequences turns, delegates work, and carries enterprise observability and governance. We isolate it with a controlled swap: 22 locked evaluation tasks, six foundation models (Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.1, Gemini Flash 3.5, Qwen 3.6, GLM 5.1, Palmyra X6), changing only the orchestration layer -- a frozen conventional production loop versus the Writer Agent Harness. Holding models constant, the harness cuts blended cost per task 41% ($0.21->$0.12), median wall-clock 44% (48s->27s), and tokens per task 38% (14.2k->8.8k), with task-completion quality at parity (0.78->0.81, directional at this sample size). Efficiency is model-invariant -- every model gets cheaper (33-61%) -- while quality gains are capability-dependent: a model's gain correlates almost perfectly with its baseline strength (r=0.99, n=6), a phenomenon we term harness leverage. Quality per dollar rises 82%; task-completions per million tokens rise from 54.9 to 92.0. On this workload the orchestration layer moved cost per task more than the full spread of the model menu did. We formalize token economics at the orchestration layer (including effective input price under prompt caching), detail the six mechanism families behind the effect -- cache-shape discipline to failure-spend governance -- compare six widely used agent systems on the same axes, and argue the harness is the one component whose efficiency multiplies across every model an organization runs -- present and future.

</details>


### [241] [Grounding Spatial Relations in a Compact World Model: Instruction Leakage and a Goal-Free Dynamics Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06925)
*Yufeng Wang,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了目标条件世界模型中的一个严重混淆问题：当指令直接命名了待预测的答案时，模型实现的所谓"关系理解"实际上是"指令转录"而非真正的感知。通过一系列实验（准确率从0.90降至0.27、反事实指令94.5%跟随假指令），作者证明了这一泄漏现象，并提出了检测协议和修复方法：将目标排除在动力学之外，监督"读取"路径，从而恢复真正的、与指令无关的 grounding（准确率0.88，有/无目标时完全一致）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别并证明目标条件世界模型中存在"指令泄漏"这一严重混淆问题——模型并非真正理解语言与感知的关系，而只是从指令中转录答案。

Method: 通过三种实验设置（指令去除、反事实指令、跨设置消融）系统地检测泄漏现象，并提出了将目标从动力学中移除、监督读取路径的修复方法。

Result: 原始模型达到0.90高精度但实为指令转录；去除目标后降至0.27（随机）；反事实指令下94.5%跟随假指令；修复后模型达到0.88准确率，且有无目标表现完全一致。

Conclusion: 指令泄漏的核心条件是"被评分的量可从指令中转写"（即指令命名了答案），与输入的非指令部分预测能力无关。修复方案是保持目标不出现在动力学中（属于规划器的代价函数），监督读取路径。

Abstract: Compact world models that condition on a language goal promise to ground relations such as ``put the red block left of the blue block'' using a sparse set of explicit \emph{reference anchors}. We ask when such references actually ground a relation, and identify a trap: a goal-conditioned predictor reaches a striking $0.90$ relation-readout accuracy, yet this is \emph{instruction transcription}, not perception. Withholding the goal collapses it to chance ($0.90\!\to\!0.27$, three seeds) and a counterfactual instruction makes the predicted anchors follow the \emph{false} instruction $94.5\%$ of the time (true scene $2.3\%$; $N{=}256$). Tested across three settings and a within-task ablation, our central claim characterizes the confound: \textbf{instruction leakage occurs when the scored quantity is transcribable from the instruction (when the instruction names the answer) and is essentially independent of how predictive the non-instruction inputs are.} Our tabletop and the external BabyAI benchmark leak, whereas a Language-Table forward-dynamics world model whose instruction names \emph{referents} does not, until the instruction is augmented to name the direction; and degrading the action never increases leakage, the opposite of what predictor-competition predicts. The diagnosis prescribes the fix: keep the goal out of the dynamics (it belongs to the planner's cost) and supervise the \emph{read} path, recovering genuine, instruction-independent grounding ($0.88$, identical with and without the goal). The detection protocol and remedy apply to any goal-conditioned world model whose instruction names the scored quantity.

</details>


### [242] [Large Behavior Model: A Promptable Digital Twin of the Retail Customer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06993)
*Wachiravit Modecrua,Krittin Pachtrachai,Touchapon Kraisingkorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出大行为模型(LBM)，通过统一的人物-环境框架从大规模零售交易中学习客户决策，在多项任务上优于通用语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么优化预测准确性但缺乏可解释性，要么模拟用户但缺乏真实行为数据支撑，需要一种既能准确预测又能基于真实行为数据解释决策的客户行为建模方法。

Method: 采用人物-环境统一框架，用行为画像表示客户状态，通过检索增强生成融入产品上下文；训练分为三步：行为数据语言化后的持续预训练、决策生成的监督微调、基于可验证奖励的强化学习校准。

Result: 在购买预测、难负样本区分、购物篮补全、促销响应和跨域优惠券兑换等任务上一致优于通用语言模型，并展现出强大的零样本和微调迁移能力。消融实验表明持续预训练是行为泛化的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 交易历史中编码的行为知识可被语言模型有效学习，为构建客户数字孪生和行为模拟提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Customer behavior modeling underpins recommendation, marketing, and decision support, yet existing approaches either optimize predictive accuracy without explaining decisions or simulate users without grounding them in real behavioral data. We present the Large Behavioral Model (LBM) that learns customer decision making directly from large-scale retail transactions through a unified Person-Environment formulation. Customer state is represented by a behavioral profile derived from historical purchases, while product context is incorporated through retrieval-augmented generation. The model is trained using continued pre-training on verbalized behavioral data, supervised fine-tuning for decision generation, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for evidence-based calibration. We evaluate the proposed framework on purchase prediction, hard-negative discrimination, basket completion, promotion response, and cross-domain voucher redemption. The model consistently outperforms frontier general-purpose language models on in-domain retail tasks while demonstrating strong zero-shot and fine-tuned transfer across retailers and decision domains. Ablation studies show that continued pre-training is the primary driver of behavioral generalization, retrieval is most effective when applied during both training and inference, and reinforcement learning improves reliance on explicit behavioral evidence over generic language-model priors. These results demonstrate that behavioral knowledge encoded in transaction histories can be effectively learned by language models, providing a scalable foundation for customer digital twins and behavior simulation.

</details>


### [243] [Learning social norms enhances compatibility in dynamic human-AI coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07021)
*Yi Yang,Siyuan Liu,Xin Gao,Huamu Sun,Chao Liu,Qing Zhou,Bingbing Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过将隐性社会规范形式化为可量化的原则（结果可预测性、价值对齐、优势意识），显著提升了AI Agent（特别是LLM）在行人-车辆动态交互中的协调能力，使LLM在闭环交互任务中总分提升近四倍，并超越人类-人类交互表现43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI Agent在与人类的动态交互中常常无法实现有效、体贴且自然的协调，原因是现有方法仅将模型行为与人类演示对齐，而未显式量化生成这些行为背后的隐性社会规范。

Method: 选取行人-车辆交互作为代表性动态交互场景，开发简化实验平台收集3,456次动态人类交互数据，从中归纳出三项社会规范原则（结果可预测性、价值对齐、优势意识），并将这些原则注入LLM驱动的AI Agent中。

Result: 融入社会规范的LLM在人类闭环交互任务中总分比基线策略提升近四倍，并超越人类-人类交互表现43%。

Conclusion: 将隐性社会规范形式化为显式、可量化的原则，能使AI Agent在动态交互中实现互利协调，从而更自然地融入人类社会。

Abstract: Humans continuously coordinate with others in dynamic interactions, often through implicit, hard-to-quantify social norms that act as shared tacit expectations among interacting agents. As AI agents, including large language models (LLMs), become embedded in daily life, they increasingly participate in such interactions and reshape social interaction structures. Yet they often fail to coordinate with humans in an effective, considerate, and natural manner. We hypothesize that this gap arises because existing approaches align model behavior with human demonstrations without explicitly quantifying the underlying norms that generate such behavior. We selected pedestrian-vehicle interaction as a representative dynamic interaction and developed a simplified experimental platform that captures its key interactive features. From 3,456 dynamic human interactions collected via this platform, we identified three principles underlying human social norms: outcome predictability, value alignment, and advantage awareness. Incorporating these principles into AI agents significantly improves human-AI coordination. In the closed-loop interaction task with humans, the social-norm-informed LLM achieved a nearly fourfold higher total score than the baseline strategy and outperformed human-human interactions by 43%. These findings indicate that formalizing tacit social norms into explicit, quantifiable principles can enable AI agents to achieve mutually beneficial coordination in dynamic interactions, supporting their more natural integration into human society.

</details>


### [244] [Measuring Intelligence Beyond Human Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07040)
*Jerry Han,Rafael Moschopoulos,Ella Colby,Vishrut Goyal,Andrew Tu,Kia Ghods,Mark Braverman,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于相对测量的对抗心理测量评级系统，通过让模型生成公开挑战来分离其他系统，从而突破人类基准饱和问题，实现超越人类能力的智能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类编写的基准测试容易饱和，且当模型能力超越人类时，评估者无法确定哪些任务既困难又可验证。这种困难是绝对尺度评估固有的问题。

Method: 提出基于相对测量的新范式：模型生成公开挑战来区分其他系统。该方法包括：减少私人信息攻击激励的实用协议、支持无评判者裁决、自然随被测系统能力扩展的对抗心理测量评级系统。

Result: 该框架在可验证领域和开放性、非可验证领域均得到实例化，展示了模型生成的评估如何继续衡量超越人类前沿的系统。

Conclusion: 相对测量范式能够解决绝对尺度评估在超人类能力场景下的固有困难，为持续衡量超越人类能力的智能系统提供了可行的评估路径。

Abstract: How can we measure intelligence beyond human capability?
  Human-authored benchmarks saturate, and above human capability, examiners may not know which tasks are both hard and verifiable. We argue that this difficulty is inherent to absolute-scale evaluation and propose a new paradigm based on relative measurement in which models generate public challenges that separate other systems. Aggregating these outcomes yields an adversarial psychometric rating system that can scale with the systems being measured. We describe practical protocols that reduce incentives for private-information attacks, support judge-free adjudication, and naturally scale with agent capabilities. We instantiate the framework across verifiable and open-ended, non-verifiable domains, illustrating how model-generated evaluation can continue to measure systems beyond the human frontier.

</details>


### [245] [Operational Reframing and Approval-Framed Delegation in Multi-Agent LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07097)
*Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu,Xiaochong Jiang,Su Wang,Pin Qian,Yihang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 传统多智能体LLM安全评估将"pipeline效应"归因于单一架构属性，但这混淆了三种机制。本文提出五条件对照实验设计，发现聚合安全性并非稳定的架构属性：操作重构是最可转移的风险信号；规划器行为主要通过拒绝来抵消风险；委托框架受提示设计、模型配对和场景来源影响。原始模型排名无法预测实际部署行为，建议分别报告各机制因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估将直接提示与规划器-执行器管线的差异聚合为单一的"pipeline效应"，这种聚合难以解释，因为它混淆了三种不同机制：恶意意图被重构为合理操作、规划器拒绝或转换请求、以及委托提示下执行器的行为变化。需要系统性地分离这些因素。

Method: 提出五条件受控对照实验设计，在30个合成有害场景和四个智能体安全基准的外部验证集上进行评估，使用LLM评判的合规性指标。条件设计系统性地隔离了操作重构、规划器行为、委托框架和模型配对各因素。

Result: 1) 聚合管线安全性不是稳定的架构属性；2) 操作重构是最可转移的风险信号，GPT、Gemini和DeepSeek均受影响，Claude相对抵抗；3) 规划器主要通过拒绝来抵消风险，但产生可执行步骤时执行器合规性可能超过直接操作基线；4) 批准框架下的委托对提示设计、模型配对和场景来源敏感，怀疑导向的执行器提示可大幅降低合规性；5) 原始直接模型排名无法预测部署行为，如Gemini在原始提示中最安全，但与Claude规划器配对时合规性从8.9%飙升至38.9%。

Conclusion: 多智能体安全评估应分别报告操作重构、规划器行为、委托框架和模型配对等各因素，而非将其归因于架构本身。聚合pipeline效应具有误导性，需要在不同粒度上分解行为才能理解安全风险的真实来源。

Abstract: Safety evaluations of multi-agent LLM systems often compare a direct prompt with a planner-executor pipeline and report the difference as a single "pipeline effect." We argue that this aggregate is difficult to interpret because it conflates three mechanisms: harmful intent may be reframed as plausible operational work, the planner may refuse or transform the request, and the executor may act under delegation prompts implying prior approval. To separate these factors, we introduce a five-condition controlled contrast design, evaluated on 30 synthetic harmful scenarios and an exploratory external validation set from four agent-safety benchmarks using LLM-judged compliance.
  Our results show that aggregate pipeline safety is not a stable architectural property. Operational reframing is the most portable risk signal, increasing compliance for GPT, Gemini, and DeepSeek across both scenario sets, while Claude is comparatively resistant. Planner behavior can offset this risk mainly through refusal; however, when the planner produces executable steps, the executor may become more compliant than under the direct operational baseline. Approval-framed delegation is sensitive to prompt design, model pairing, and scenario source, and a skeptical executor prompt sharply reduces compliance.
  Raw-direct model rankings can also mispredict deployed planner-executor behavior. Gemini is safest under raw direct prompts in the primary set yet shows the largest amplification with a Claude planner, rising from 8.9 percent to 38.9 percent compliance. GPTs near-zero aggregate pipeline effect instead hides a reframing increase canceled by planner refusal. These findings suggest that multi-agent safety evaluations should report reframing, planner behavior, delegation framing, and model pairing separately before attributing failures to architecture itself.

</details>


### [246] [Does AI Understand Imaging? A Systematic Benchmark of Agentic AI for Computational Imaging Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07189)
*Ethan Chung,Chuanjun Zheng,Jasper Tan,Jingxi Li,Haopeng Zhang,Huaijin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ImagingBench基准测试，包含20个计算成像任务，评估视觉语言模型和智能体AI在物理成像方面的能力。结果表明，尽管这些模型在语义视觉任务上表现优异，但在计算成像问题上远弱于专业方法，揭示了语义视觉能力与物理成像性能之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM和智能体AI在语义视觉任务上表现强劲，但能否处理计算成像中的物理和逆问题尚不明确，因此需要系统性基准来评估这一差距。
