Method: 构建了包含20个任务的ImagingBench基准，涵盖射线与波动光学、图像信号处理、逆重建、计算感知和标定五大类。设计了三种评估设置：Expert（固定专家引导逆重建）、Planner（规划器引导逆重建）和Forward（前向系统仿真一致性检查），并对比了专有/开源多模态系统与任务特定非智能体基线。

Result: 智能体模型在所有任务上始终弱于专业方法，尤其在计算感知问题（无透镜成像、事件基重建、飞行时间成像、全息术）上差距显著。Planner引导相比固定Expert基线提升有限且不稳定。模型常生成视觉上合理的输出，但保真度差。

Conclusion: ImagingBench揭示了语义视觉能力与物理成像性能之间的实质差距，为衡量这一差距和追踪智能体AI在计算成像领域的进展提供了统一测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) and agentic AI have shown strong performance on semantic visual tasks, but it remains unclear whether they can handle the physics and inverse problems that underlie computational imaging. We present ImagingBench, a benchmark of 20 computational imaging tasks spanning five categories: ray and wave optics, image signal processing, inverse reconstruction, computational sensing, and calibration. ImagingBench evaluates three complementary settings: Expert, fixed expert-guided inverse reconstruction; Planner, planner-guided inverse reconstruction; and Forward, forward-system simulation for consistency checking. We benchmark leading proprietary and open-source image-centric multimodal systems, including Gemini, GPT, and Qwen, and compare them with representative task-specific non-agentic baselines. Across tasks, agentic models remain consistently weaker than specialized methods, especially on computational sensing problems such as lensless imaging, event-based reconstruction, time-of-flight imaging, and holography. Planner guidance provides only modest and inconsistent gains over the fixed-prompt Expert baseline. Although the models often generate visually plausible outputs, their reference-based fidelity remains poor, revealing a substantial gap between semantic visual competence and physically grounded imaging performance. ImagingBench provides a unified testbed for measuring this gap and tracking progress in agentic AI for computational imaging.

</details>


### [247] [Reasoning Consistency Scanning: A Framework for Auditing Chain-of-Thought Validity in AI Safety Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07229)
*Silvia Santano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出推理一致性扫描方法，用于检测AI安全评估中推理过程与答案的逻辑一致性，而非更难的忠实性（faithfulness）。作者形式化了推理一致性的六种不一致子类型，构建了60条人工标注基准，实现了首个面向安全评估的扫描工具，并在多个模型和任务中验证了不一致性的存在与可检测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明思维链（CoT）推理往往不忠实（unfaithful），但检测不忠实需要受控实验干预，无法事后分析。因此作者转向一个更易处理的问题：评估推理逻辑是否与答案一致（consistency），这是仅凭转录即可判断的。

Method: 1) 形式化定义推理一致性并将其与忠实性区分，提出六种不一致子类型分类体系。2) 基于InstrumentalEval输出人工构建60条标注基准。3) 在InspectScout框架上实现首个针对该属性的扫描工具。4) 在4个生成模型和3个inspect_evals评估上报告结果。

Result: 推理不一致性确实存在且可检测，在不同模型和任务类型上表现出系统性差异。

Conclusion: 推理一致性扫描是一种无需干预、可复用的安全评估方法，能够有效发现AI推理中的逻辑不一致问题。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is often unfaithful: a model's stated reasoning does not reliably reflect the process that produced its output. Detecting unfaithfulness, though, requires controlled experimental interventions, which cannot be applied to evaluation transcripts after the fact. We turn instead to a more tractable question that has received less attention: whether the stated reasoning is logically consistent with the answer it accompanies. Unlike faithfulness, consistency can be assessed from a transcript alone, with no intervention. We introduce reasoning consistency scanning, a reusable method for detecting this property in AI safety evaluation transcripts. Our contributions are fourfold. First, we formalize reasoning consistency as distinct from faithfulness and define a six-subtype taxonomy of inconsistency. Second, we build a validated benchmark of 60 transcripts, manually adapted from InstrumentalEval outputs. Third, we implement a working scanner for InspectScout, the first to target this property in safety evaluation transcripts. Fourth, we report results across four generator models and three evaluations from inspect_evals, showing that reasoning inconsistency is present, detectable, and varies systematically across both models and task types.

</details>


### [248] [From Atomic Actions to Standard Operating Procedures: Iterative Tool Optimization for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07321)
*Haipeng Ding,Yuexiang Xie,Zhewei Wei,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoSOP框架，让LLM代理通过将原子动作合成为可复用的标准操作流程(SOP)实现自我进化，显著提升任务成功率并减少交互轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理框架依赖静态的原子动作工具集，导致代理在处理重复工作流时需重新发明底层逻辑，增加了推理开销和失败率。

Method: 提出EvoSOP框架，从执行轨迹中提取SOP作为高阶可调用工具，并通过构建、合并、评估和剪枝的系统生命周期迭代优化工具集。

Result: 与基线相比，EvoSOP显著提升了任务成功率，同时大幅减少了交互轮次数。

Conclusion: 迭代工具优化能够催生可靠高效的工具使用模式，为自进化智能体的发展提供了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Tool utilization enables Large Language Model (LLM) agents to interact with the real world and resolve complex tasks. However, existing agent frameworks predominantly rely on static toolsets composed of granular atomic actions (e.g., basic file I/O or single-turn search), which forces agents to reinvent low-level logic for every recurring workflow, leading to increased reasoning overhead and failure rates. In this study, we propose that agents can achieve self-evolution by synthesizing these atomic actions into reusable Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs), which function as callable higher-order tools that encapsulate multi-step logic. We further introduce EvoSOP, a framework that empowers agents to extract SOPs from execution trajectories and iteratively optimize the toolset through a systematic lifecycle of construction, merging, evaluation, and pruning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EvoSOP significantly boosts task success rates while substantially reducing the number of interaction rounds compared to baselines. Our analysis also reveals that iterative tool optimization fosters reliable and efficient tool-use patterns, providing a scalable pathway for the development of self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [249] [Physics-Audited Agentic Discovery in Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07379)
*Diab W. Abueidda,Bilal Ahmed,Panos Pantidis,Mostafa E. Mobasher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PA-SciML框架，在代理科学机器学习中引入物理审计验证流程，通过可检查的物理要求（如边界条件、叠加原理、刚度缩放、因果性）来筛选替代模型，而非仅依赖误差指标。在固体力学数值实验中，该框架能选出通过物理检验的模型，而纯误差基线模型在瞬态弹性动力学中未能通过因果性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM代理的SciML方法仅依赖误差指标筛选替代模型，但低误差不能保证预测场满足力学相关的物理规律（如边界条件、叠加原理、刚度缩放、因果性），需要引入可验证的物理审计机制。

Method: PA-SciML流程：1) 在搜索前固定评分评估器；2) 推导可审查、可机器检查的物理要求；3) 对每个训练候选模型检查其输出；4) 在指定输入范围或测量的载荷历史跨度上独立搜索高违反案例（无需参考解场）；5) 仅在通过上述检查后才报告模型已验证。可选功能：训练前添加数值探针，每次只改变一个建模组件以记录孤立编辑对评分的影响。

Result: 静态弹性算例中，PA-SciML选出验证误差低于纯误差基线的替代模型，且两者均通过线性弹性检查。瞬态弹性动力学算例中，纯误差基线模型（平均误差相似）未能通过更严格的因果性检查（对未来载荷历史部分有响应），而PA-SciML选出的替代模型通过所有检查。

Conclusion: PA-SciML的核心区别在于对每个候选模型提供预测场的物理证据，而非追求更丰富的聚合评分。验证优先的流程能有效筛选满足物理约束的替代模型，避免纯误差指标带来的物理不一致性问题。

Abstract: In agentic scientific machine learning (SciML), large language model (LLM) agents can discover surrogate models and select one by an automated score, typically an error metric. A low error, however, does not establish that the predicted fields satisfy the physics that matter for mechanics, such as boundary conditions, superposition, stiffness scaling, or causality. We introduce Physics-Audited Agentic SciML (PA-SciML), a verification-first workflow for agentic SciML discovery. The workflow fixes a scoring evaluator before search, derives reviewable machine-checkable physics requirements, checks each trained candidate on its outputs, and separately searches prescribed input ranges or measured load-history spans for high-violation cases without reference solution fields. A surrogate is reported as verified only under the stated checks. When enabled, the workflow also adds advisory numerical probes before training and tests one modeling change at a time to record which isolated edits are associated with score gains before reuse. In the reported computational-solid-mechanics numerical examples, the static elasticity run selects a surrogate with lower validation error than the error-only baseline while both selected models pass the common linear-elastic checks. In the transient elastodynamics run, an error-only baseline with similar mean error fails a stricter causality check by responding to future parts of the loading history, while the selected surrogate passes the stated checks. The main distinction is per-candidate physics evidence on predicted fields, not a richer aggregate score.

</details>


### [250] [MIRA-Math: A Benchmark for Minimal Information Requesting and Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07391)
*Charbel Al Bateh,Samer Saab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIRA-Math是一个数学推理基准，测试模型在缺失一个关键原子事实的情况下，通过自然语言请求获取信息并完成求解的能力，包含2,310个实例和22个数学类型，实验表明请求成功与最终答案准确度是可分离的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理基准通常提供所有必要事实，而交互式基准又混合了推理、工具使用和长程对话，缺乏对"模型主动请求缺失信息"这一核心诊断能力的精细化评估。

Method: 构建MIRA-Math基准：每个问题在求解视角缺失恰好一个必要的原子事实，模型需在严格预算下用自然语言请求该信息，由固定LLM响应器返回引用事实或拒绝。包含22个数学类型（代数、概率、线性系统、离散结构、信号处理、马尔可夫链、电路、插值、数值边界值问题），共2,310个生成实例，所有生成、验证和答案检查均为确定性的。

Result: 跨前沿和小规模模型的实验表明，请求成功率和最终答案准确度是可分离的：模型可能问对了事实但后续计算失败，也可能在获得规范提示之前就已失败。

Conclusion: MIRA-Math提供了一个可复现的评估框架，用于测试数学推理中最小信息请求能力，并已公开发布生成器、验证器、提示词、运行元数据和文档。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning benchmarks typically provide all facts needed to solve each problem, while interactive benchmarks often mix reasoning with tools, retrieval, and long-horizon dialogue. We introduce MIRA-Math, a benchmark for a narrower diagnostic capability: solving mathematical problems whose full latent state has a unique answer, but whose solver-facing view is missing exactly one necessary atomic fact. The solver must request the missing information in natural language under a strict budget and then integrate the returned fact into an exact final answer. A fixed constrained LLM responder sees only the dataset-provided atomic fact and must either offer the quoted fact when the request matches it, or decline otherwise. Thus, instance generation, typed hint specifications, validation, and final-answer verification are deterministic, while request metrics are measured under a fixed LLM-mediated responder channel. MIRA-Math contains 2{,}310 generated instances from 22 typed mathematical families spanning algebra, probability, linear systems, discrete structures, signal processing, Markov chains, circuits, interpolation, and numerical boundary-value problems. Experiments across frontier and small models show that request success and final-answer accuracy are separable: models may ask for the right fact yet fail the downstream computation, or fail before obtaining the canonical hint. We release generators, verifiers, prompts, run metadata, and dataset documentation to support reproducible evaluation of minimal information requesting in mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [251] [Agentic Data Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07397)
*Elaine Ang,Chenxi Huang,Georgios Liargkovas,Jerry Liu,Jinhui Liu,Nikos Pagonas,Charlie Summers,Haonan Wang,Jiakai Xu,Tianle Zhou,Yusen Zhang,Zhou Yu,Zhuo Zhang,Tianyi Peng,Kostis Kaffes,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Agentic Data Environments"概念，将数据系统从被动存储重构为智能体安全可靠执行的主动基板，旨在平衡自主智能体的自动化收益与失败风险控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体在提升速度、规模和劳动效率方面具有巨大潜力，但其失败可能造成突然且往往不可逆的代价。核心挑战在于如何在增加自动化收益的同时，约束失败的后果。

Method: 提出Agentic Data Environments概念，将智能体的执行基板（涵盖文件、API、应用程序和系统状态）重新设计为既能增强智能体能力又能强制执行安全保证的主动数据环境。

Result: 将数据库角色从被动的状态存储重新定义为智能体安全、可靠执行的主动基板，实现自动化收益与失败风险之间的平衡。

Conclusion: 数据系统需要从被动存储转型为主动执行基板，以支撑智能体在更广泛数据环境中的安全可靠运行。

Abstract: Autonomous agents promise substantial gains in speed, scale, and labor efficiency, but their failures can impose abrupt and often irreversible costs. The central challenge for agentic automation is therefore to increase the benefits of automation while bounding the consequences of failure.
  While databases remain central to modern computing, agents operate over a broader data environment spanning files, APIs, applications, and system state. In this talk, I will outline early work on Agentic Data Environments -- the execution substrate in which agents operate -- that both amplify agent capabilities and enforce safety guarantees. This perspective reframes data systems from passive stores of state into active substrates for safe, reliable execution.

</details>


### [252] [Reason Less, Verify More: Deterministic Gates Recover a Silent Policy-Violation Failure Mode in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07405)
*Vikas Reddy,Sumanth Reddy Challaram,Abhishek Basu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了工具型LLM智能体在策略宽松环境中执行任务时，可能出现“静默违规写操作”（即工具执行了违反域策略的调用但无报错）的失败模式。在τ²-bench航空领域预算智能体上，78%的失败属于此类。作者提出了一种轻量级干预——确定性只读预执行门控机制，在写操作前检查调用和当前状态。四门控套件将gpt-4o-mini的成功率从29.6%提升至42.0%（+12.4pp），且效果可复现。该机制在门控触发任务上提升显著（+19.2pp），而在非触发任务上效果不显著。两个负对照实验界定了机制边界：当工具本身已强制执行策略时，门控帮助有限。前沿模型（gpt-5.2）同样存在此问题，门控同样带来提升（+10.4pp）。贡献在于提供了一种有界评估和可靠性结果：确定性门控虽不能保证任务成功，但能确定性地预防一类已知的静默策略违规写操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型LLM智能体在策略宽松的环境中，可能执行违反域策略的调用且不报错，造成“静默错误状态”（如取消预订、更改乘客数量、未经验证的索赔等），而工具和智能体的自报告均不暴露此问题。现有方法未能有效检测和预防这类故障模式。

Method: 提出确定性、只读的预执行门控机制：在执行工具写操作前，检查当前状态和拟议的工具调用是否符合域策略。具体实现为四个门控套件。在τ²-bench航空领域预算智能体上进行评估，同时在自强制执行策略的零售领域和BFCL上设置负对照实验。

Result: 在τ²-bench上，78%的观察失败为静默错误状态失败。四门控套件将gpt-4o-mini成功率从29.6%提升至42.0%（+12.4pp），在25个复现种子上的提升为+12.3pp。效果集中在门控触发的26/50任务（+19.2pp），非触发任务效果不显著。对于gpt-5.2，成功率从61.2%提升至71.6%（+10.4pp）。

Conclusion: 确定性门控不能保证任务完全成功，但能确定性地在动作边界阻止一类已知的静默策略违规写操作。该方法在工具策略宽松时效果显著，在工具已自强制执行策略时增益有限。该故障模式在前沿模型中依然存在。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents can violate the very policies they are deployed to enforce while appearing to complete the task successfully. In policy-permissive environments, a tool may execute any well-formed call even when the corresponding state transition is forbidden by domain policy. The result is a silent wrong state (a booking cancelled, a passenger count changed, a claim acted on without verification) that neither the tool nor the agent's self-report exposes.
  We study this failure mode in the $τ^2$-bench airline domain. On a budget agent, 78% of observed failures are silent wrong-state failures with no tool error, and the aggregate failure rate is reproducible across disjoint seeds, not sampling noise. We then evaluate a lightweight intervention: deterministic, read-only pre-execution gates that inspect the proposed call and current state before allowing a write. A four-gate suite raises full-benchmark success from 29.6% to 42.0% on gpt-4o-mini (+12.4pp; paired task-level bootstrap P=0.0012), and the lift reproduces on a disjoint 15-seed set (+12.3pp; P=0.0008).
  The effect is concentrated where the gates fire: on the 26/50 firing tasks, success rises by +19.2pp, while movement on the 24 non-firing tasks does not exclude zero. Two negative controls (a self-enforcing retail domain and BFCL) bound the mechanism: gates help when tools are policy-permissive and add little where tools already self-enforce. As suggestive evidence, not a central claim, the same failure mode persists at the frontier: gpt-5.2 at default reasoning still attempts policy-violating writes, and the same suite improves success from 61.2% to 71.6% (+10.4pp; P=0.020; n=5, no replication). The contribution is a bounded evaluation and reliability result: deterministic gates do not guarantee task success, but they can deterministically prevent a known class of silent policy-violating writes at the action boundary.

</details>


### [253] [InductWave: Inductive Multi-Hop Logical Query Answering on Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07422)
*Mayank Kharbanda,Michael Cochez,Rajiv Ratn Shah,Raghava Mutharaju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出InductWave，一种基于小波的归纳式嵌入方法，用于知识图谱上的逻辑多跳查询回答。该方法在训练图节点少于测试图的归纳设置下，仅需75%的消息传递层即可超越基线模型，并在大规模知识图谱Wiki-KG上完成评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱逻辑查询方法主要基于直推式推理，无法处理训练中未见的实体；而现实世界中存在资源稀缺性，无法用大规模KG的所有节点训练模型，因此需要一种归纳式、资源高效的查询回答方法。

Method: 提出InductWave框架，采用基于小波变换的归纳式嵌入方法，在训练图节点数少于测试图的归纳设置下进行逻辑查询回答；通过小波变换减少消息传递层数（仅需基线模型的75%），实现资源高效推理。

Result: 在FB15k-237数据集上，InductWave在多数情况下以75%的层数超越所有基线模型；在拥有50%层数时与基线性能持平；成功在大规模图谱Wiki-KG上完成评估。

Conclusion: InductWave通过小波变换实现了归纳式逻辑查询回答，显著降低了资源需求（更少的消息传递层），同时保持或超越现有方法的性能，适用于大规模知识图谱场景。

Abstract: Logical Multi-Hop Query Answering over Knowledge Graphs (KGs) can be formulated as querying, with an implicit completeness assumption. Current works mainly focus on Existential First Order Logic (EFO) queries. These EFO queries contain conjunction, disjunction, and negation operators. Most existing works employ transductive reasoning, meaning they are not capable of reasoning over entities unseen during training. In the real world, there is a resource scarcity, and we cannot train a model with all the nodes of a large KG. Hence, we propose InductWave, a wavelet-based inductive embedding method for logical query answering on large KGs. Here, the training graph consists of fewer nodes than the test graph. Our model performs on par with the baseline models while having half the number of message-passing layers. It outperforms all of them in most cases, with 75% of the layers. These fewer resource requirements enable us to evaluate InductWave on massive graphs, such as Wiki-KG. We test our model using extensive experiments across varying train-test graph proportions of the FB15k-(237) dataset, comparing it with the state-of-the-art models. The code and datasets for the model are available at https://github.com/kracr/inductwave/.

</details>


### [254] [The Blind Curator: How a Biased Judge Silently Disables Skill Retirement in Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07436)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了自演化智能体系统中，使用有偏的LLM作为评审（Judge）进行无参考任务评估时，会导致"技能淘汰"机制静默失效的问题。研究表明，假阳性偏差（将失败误判为成功）会越过一个阈值，使得基于贡献的技能淘汰完全失效，且这一机制性故障无法通过增加数据量解决。论文提出了一种缺陷注入审计方法，可在部署前检测评审是否存在此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演化智能体的技能库依赖无偏奖励信号来淘汰低质量技能，但在无参考任务中必须使用LLM作为评审，而LLM评审存在系统性偏差。这种偏差不仅仅引入噪声，而是会静默地关闭技能淘汰机制，导致系统退化但没有任何聚合指标能够察觉。

Method: 论文采用损坏奖励分析（corrupted-reward analysis）和因果隔离的行为研究：在确定性奖励基础上注入损坏，以无参考报告生成测试平台和代码生成交叉验证为实验环境，系统性地研究不同类型偏差对技能淘汰机制的影响，并通过分离真实淘汰与容量驱逐抖动来展示机制失效的普遍性。

Result: 对称噪声不影响技能淘汰；假阳性偏差（false-pass bias）在超过阈值后会使基于贡献的技能淘汰完全失效，且无法通过增加数据量克服。该机制失效在所有领域和失败率下普遍存在，只有在近乎零假阳性率的验证器类评审下才能避免。下游评估质量仅在相同损坏同时阻止技能合成时才会下降，否则保持稳定，因此被静默禁用的评审无法通过聚合指标察觉。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是一个行为安全性结果而非性能结果。作者提出了一种廉价的缺陷注入审计方法，可在部署前告知操作员其评审是否处于危险阈值一侧。

Abstract: A self-evolving agent retires its bad skills by watching them fail, so what happens when the judge cannot see the failures? Skill retirement is the structural constraint that keeps a growing library from drifting below the no-skill baseline, but its guarantee assumes an unbiased reward, which is false for the LLM judges that reference-free tasks force upon us. We show that a biased judge does not merely add noise; it \emph{silently switches off the curator}. We make this precise with a corrupted-reward analysis and, isolating the causal channel by injecting corruption on top of a deterministic reward, a behavioral study on a reference-free report-writing testbed with a code-generation cross-check. Symmetric noise leaves retirement intact, but \emph{false-pass} bias (failures slipping through as passes) disables contribution-based retirement past a sharp threshold that no amount of data can cross. Separating genuine retirement from cap-eviction churn shows this \emph{mechanism} failure is universal, holding across domains and failure rates and sparing only near-zero-false-pass, verifier-like graders. The downstream \emph{outcome}, though, is regime-dependent: eval quality degrades only where the same corruption also starves skill synthesis, and otherwise holds steady, so the disabled curator is \emph{silent}, surfacing in no aggregate metric. The contribution is a behavioral safety result, not a performance one. A cheap defect-injection audit then tells an operator, before deployment, which side of the threshold their judge occupies.

</details>


### [255] [SpaCellAgent: A Self-Evolving LLM-Based Multi-Agent Framework for Trajectory Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07467)
*Songhan Wang,Haoang Chi,He Li,Zhiheng Zhang,Jiayan Yuan,Cheems Wang,Hao Peng,Xinwang Liu,Wenjing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpaCellAgent是一个基于大语言模型的多智能体框架，能够自动完成从自然语言指令到时空转录组学轨迹推断的全流程分析，并在六个异构数据集上实现超40%的效率提升，同时保持专家级分析质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹推断方法需要大量人工干预和对多种异构工具的熟练运用，缺乏端到端的自动化解决方案，这成为高效进行时空转录组学分析的显著障碍。

Method: SpaCellAgent采用多智能体架构进行策略性工作流规划，配备动态工具编排引擎实现自适应算法选择，并引入自我进化模块通过反馈迭代优化性能，从而将自然语言描述转化为完整的分析流程。

Result: 在涵盖复杂时间发育轨迹、多样测序平台和空间解析组织架构的六个异构数据集上，SpaCellAgent在保持与专家水平一致的分析质量的同时，实现了超过40%的分析效率提升。

Conclusion: SpaCellAgent通过将自然语言规格转化为优化的分析工作流并实现全自动化流水线，推动了先进时空建模的普及化，为计算生物学建立了可扩展的、智能体驱动的分析新范式。

Abstract: Spatial and Single-cell transcriptomics are transformative in deciphering cellular dynamics. As the fundamental paradigm for reconstructing cell developmental paths, trajectory inference (TI) is critical. However, existing methods require extensive manual intervention and proficiency in heterogeneous tools, posing a significant barrier to efficient TI analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose SpaCellAgent, an autonomous large language model (LLM) multi-agent framework that automates end-to-end spatiotemporal analysis and narrative generation. SpaCellAgent utilizes a multi-agent architecture for strategic workflow planning, a dynamic tool-orchestration engine for adaptive algorithm selection, and a self-evolution module that iteratively refines performance through feedback. We evaluate SpaCellAgent on six heterogeneous datasets encompassing complex temporal developmental trajectories, diverse sequencing platforms, and spatially-resolved tissue architectures. SpaCellAgent consistently demonstrates over 40\% improvement in analytical efficiency while maintaining expert-aligned performance. By converting natural language specifications into optimized analytical workflows and fully automating the pipeline, SpaCellAgent democratizes advanced spatiotemporal modeling and establishes a scalable, agent-driven paradigm for computational biology. The code and materials are available at https://github.com/LittleXH-shw/SpaCellAgent.

</details>


### [256] [Search, Fail, Recover: A Training Framework for Correction-Aware Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07492)
*Dmitry Beresnev,Vladimir Makharev,Roman Khalikov,Ivan Oseledets,Petr Anokhin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Pyligent框架，通过将搜索树中的失败分支显式建模为continue/finish/backtrack三种动作的监督信号，在多类推理任务上显著提升求解率（最高提升72.7个百分点），证明了显式的失败分支监督能教会模型超越完美解链模仿的有效恢复行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多推理任务无法用单一从左到右的链式过程描述，求解器可能需要探索合理分支、观察到延迟失败后回溯到可继续的前缀。现有监督微调仅模仿完美解链，缺乏处理失败恢复的能力。

Method: 提出Pyligent训练与推理框架，基于Diligent Learner范式，将推理表示为对部分解链的验证搜索。任务验证器标注生成的延续和失败，将搜索树转化为三类动作（continue/finish/backtrack）的监督目标，并可选择添加废弃分支的总结轨迹。

Result: 在有向隐藏图任务上求解率提升72.7个百分点；在4×4数独、带推理轨迹的数独、Blocksworld上分别提升17/18、27/14、13个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式的失败分支监督能教会模型有用的恢复行为，效果超越单纯模仿完美解链。

Abstract: Many reasoning tasks are not well described by a single left-to-right chain: a solver may need to pursue a plausible branch, observe delayed failure, and return to the latest prefix that can still be completed. We introduce Pyligent, a training and inference framework inspired by the Diligent Learner formulation that represents reasoning as validated search over partial solution chains. A task validator labels generated continuations and failures, and the resulting search trees are converted into supervised targets for three actions: continue, finish, and backtrack, with optional traces that summarize abandoned branches. We evaluate Pyligent on a hidden directed graph task designed to isolate delayed-failure recovery, and on structured reasoning domains with exact validators, including $4{\times}4$ Sudoku, Sudoku with reasoning traces, and Blocksworld. Compared with gold-only supervised fine-tuning, Pyligent improves solve rate by $72.7$ percentage points on hidden graphs, by $17$ and $18$ points on mixed and expert Sudoku, by $27$ and $14$ points on mixed and expert Sudoku with reasoning traces, and by $13$ points on Blocksworld. These results suggest that explicit failed-branch supervision can teach useful recovery behavior beyond imitation of polished solution chains.

</details>


### [257] [Do LLM-Generated Skills Make Better AI Data Scientists? A Component Ablation Across Data-Science Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07504)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM生成的技能文件（skill files）是否能提升数据科学任务中的提示效果。实验覆盖四个生命周期阶段（数据准备、数据提取、统计分析、报告），共56个任务、9种模型配置、3个提供商，7,560次运行。结果表明：完整生成技能、消融技能变体均未显著优于无技能提示（p≥0.396，性能差异仅1.2个百分点）。辅助的token匹配对照实验（1,512次运行）进一步证实完整技能与任务无关的技能格式内容表现相似。结论：在每个数据科学工作流中使用单个LLM生成的技能作为默认单次提示策略需谨慎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产品数据科学家常让LLM代理执行重复性任务（如数据清洗、写SQL、选择统计检验、格式化结果）。可复用的技能文件（skill files）旨在避免从零开始提示，但专家编写和维护这些技能存在人工瓶颈。研究动机是探究LLM自动生成的技能是否可以作为低人工干预的替代方案，提升任务提示效果。

Method: 实验框架：在数据科学四个生命周期阶段（准备、提取、分析、报告）各使用一个LLM生成的技能文件。主要消融实验覆盖56个任务、9种模型配置、3个提供商，共7,560次运行。设置无技能提示作为基线，并对技能的不同组件进行消融分析。补充实验进行token匹配对照，将完整技能与任务无关的技能格式内容比较（1,512次运行）。统计方法使用p值检验性能差异显著性。

Result: 1) 完整生成技能相比无技能提示未带来可靠改进。2) 消融实验中，所有变体（包括完整技能和消融组件）均未显著优于仅使用任务提示，所有p值≥0.396，性能差异仅1.2个百分点。3) token匹配对照中，完整技能与任务无关的技能格式内容表现相似。

Conclusion: LLM生成的单个技能文件作为数据科学工作流的默认单次提示策略效果有限，不能可靠提升任务性能。建议谨慎使用这种策略，可能需要更细粒度的技能生成、多技能组合或不同提示方式。

Abstract: Product data scientists often ask LLM-based agents to help with recurring execution tasks such as cleaning data, writing SQL, choosing statistical tests, and formatting results. Reusable skill files are meant to avoid prompting from scratch by packaging guidance for a task family. Expert-written skills can encode high-quality guidance, but writing and maintaining them across many data-science task families creates a manual bottleneck. We ask whether LLM-generated skills offer a useful low-curation alternative: do they improve performance over the task prompt alone? We test this question across four lifecycle stages: data preparation, data extraction, statistical analysis, and reporting, using one generated skill per stage. We find no reliable improvement from full generated skills over No-Skill prompting. We then ask whether any part of the skill is useful by ablating different skill components. The main ablation covers 56 tasks, nine model configurations, and three providers, yielding 7,560 runs. Compared with prompting using the task alone, neither the full generated skill nor any ablated skill variant significantly improves performance; all p-values are at least 0.396, and the total spread across variants is only 1.2 pp. A supplemental token-matched control adds 1,512 runs and finds that Full skills perform similarly to task-irrelevant skill-formatted content. The results caution against using one LLM-generated skill per data-science workflow as a default single-shot prompting strategy.

</details>


### [258] [RL Post-Training Builds Compositional Reasoning Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07646)
*Azwar Abdulsalam,Nishil Patel,Andrew Saxe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究RL后训练能否将基座模型中潜在的原始技能组合成更高级的策略。在可完全观察的重写语法环境中，基于已知预训练分布和可审计的生成重写过程，实验表明RL能够解决基座模型即使在大采样预算下也难以解决的问题，而拒绝微调（RFT）早期虽有提升但随后饱和。追踪分析显示RL通过阶段性组合机制重组原始能力：先强化原始归约，再发现有效的组合过程（包括顺序组合和并行组合），并将这些组合过程稳定化为可复用的策略库。预训练消融实验表明，组合策略的出现不仅依赖于原始技能暴露，更取决于预训练是否将原始技能组织为可被RL后续压缩的归约过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究RL后训练是否仅仅是放大基座模型中已存在的原始技能，还是能够将原始技能组合成新的高级策略，以理解RL在推理能力提升中的深层作用机制。

Method: 在完全可观察的重写语法环境中进行实验：Transformer在原始符号重写链上预训练，然后在基于Trace的推理任务上进行后训练（仅使用二元最终答案奖励）。通过对比RL与拒绝微调（RFT），并结合追踪分析（Trace analysis）和预训练消融实验，揭示RL的组成机制。

Result: RL能够解决基座模型即使在大采样预算下也难以解决的留出问题；RFT早期提升但随后饱和。RL通过阶段性组合机制重组原始能力：先强化原始归约，再发现顺序组合和并行组合等有效组合过程，并稳定化为可复用策略库。RFT产生大量捷径式的无效重写，而RL将探索集中在有效的可复用结构上。组合策略的出现取决于预训练是否将原始技能组织为可被RL压缩的归约过程。

Conclusion: RL后训练不仅仅是放大原始技能，而是能够将基座模型提供的弱过程性成分构建成可靠的高级策略。组合策略的涌现不仅需要原始技能暴露，更依赖于预训练对技能的特定组织方式以及RL的选择性探索机制。

Abstract: Does RL post-training merely amplify primitive skills already latent in a base model, or can it compose primitive skills into new higher-level strategies? We study this question in a fully observable rewrite-grammar environment where the pretraining distribution is known and every generated rewrite can be audited. A Transformer is pretrained on primitive symbol-rewrite chains and post-trained on a Trace-based reasoning task with only a binary final-answer reward. RL solves held-out problems that remain rarely solved by the pretrained model even under much larger sampling budgets, while rejection fine-tuning improves early but plateaus. Trace analysis shows that RL reorganizes primitive competence through a phased compositional mechanism: it first strengthens primitive reductions, then discovers valid composed procedures. These include sequential compositions, which collapse ordered chains of primitive contractions, and parallel compositions, which combine independent primitive contractions in a single step. The composed procedures are not isolated samples; they are reused and consolidated into a stable repertoire. Comparing RL with rejection fine-tuning shows that the key difference is not exploration volume but selectivity: RFT produces many shortcut-like rewrites, much of them invalid, whereas RL concentrates exploration into valid reusable structure. Pretraining ablations show that the emergence of compositional strategies is gated not by primitive exposure alone, but by whether pretraining organizes primitive competence into reduction procedures that RL can later compress. The base model provides weak procedural ingredients; RL builds them into reliable higher-level strategies.

</details>


### [259] [Recursive Self-Improvement in AI: From Bounded Self-Refinement to Autonomous Research Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07663)
*Mingguang Chen,Licheng Wang,Bo Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对1,250篇arXiv论文（2024-2026）关于AI自我改进的文献进行系统分类，沿着"改进对象"（行为、策略、评估器、研究过程）和"循环闭合度"（人在环到完全闭环）两个维度建立分类体系，区分了有界自优化（收敛、可评估、已在工业实践中）与开放递归自我改进（RSI，受制于接地需求、坍塌动力学和计算约束），并指出自我评估是每类改进循环的核心特征，构建了从形式验证器到内在自我评估的验证层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文献中"self-refine"、"self-reward"、"self-play"、"self-evolve"等术语混用了根本不同的研究目标，缺乏统一的分类框架来区分有界的自优化与开放式的递归自我改进，也无法系统分析自我评估信号在其中的核心作用及其失败模式。

Method: 对1,250篇arXiv论文进行系统调查，沿两个轴分类：1）改进对象（部署行为、训练策略、评估器、研究过程）；2）循环闭合度（人在环到完全闭环）。在此基础上构建验证层级，从形式验证器到内在自我评估进行排序。

Result: 建立了将自我评估作为每个改进循环核心特征的分类体系；发现自我改进强度与验证层级正相关；识别出自我确认循环、模型坍塌、多样性坍塌等失败模式源于对验证层级的违反；发现"研究方向设定"瓶颈是人在环的关键所在。

Conclusion: 有界自优化与开放递归自我改进有本质区别；自我评估信号的选择决定了改进循环的质量与安全性；对自我改进进行治理级测量是该领域最被忽视的研究方向。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly participate in their own improvement: revising their outputs, adapting their own harnesses during deployment, training on data they generate, and, increasingly, conducting AI research itself. This literature is described under a vocabulary ("self-refine," "self-reward," "self-play," "self-evolve") that conflates fundamentally different ambitions. We survey 1,250 arXiv papers (2024-2026) along two axes: what the system improves -- its behavior in deployment, its policy through training, its evaluator, or the research process itself -- and the degree of loop closure (human-in-the-loop to fully closed). The taxonomy separates bounded self-refinement -- convergent, evaluable, and already industrial practice -- from open-ended recursive self-improvement (RSI), which remains bounded by grounding requirements, collapse dynamics, and compute constraints on every measured axis. Its distinctive feature is a dedicated category for self-evaluation: every improvement loop is a claim that some signal can substitute for human judgment. We survey the evaluator design space -- judges, process reward models, verifiers, rubrics, meta-evaluation -- order the signals into a verification hierarchy from formal verifiers (strongest) to intrinsic self-assessment (weakest), and observe that demonstrated self-improvement strength tracks this hierarchy, that its failure modes (self-confirming loops, model collapse, diversity collapse) follow from its violations, and that the "research direction-setting" bottleneck keeping humans in the loop sits at the top of that hierarchy. We connect the technical literature to the theory of RSI limits and to the safety and governance questions raised by frontier-lab accounts of closing the loop, and identify governance-grade measurement of self-improvement as the field's most underpopulated niche.

</details>


### [260] [SkillCenter: A Large-Scale Source-Grounded Skill Library for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07676)
*Tianming Sha,Yue Zhao,Lichao Sun,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillCenter是一个包含216,938个结构化技能的开源技能库，其中114,565个技能通过SkillGate过滤管道从同行评审期刊、ArXiv和24,000多个技术来源获取并实现源追溯，另外102,373个社区技能来自GitHub和ClawHub市场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体在执行复杂任务时缺乏 grounded operational knowledge（扎根的操作知识），导致输出虽然可执行但缺乏正确性、安全性和可维护性。

Method: 提出端到端框架，包括多源获取、基于LLM的质量门控（SkillGate）、模板驱动生成、迭代源追溯和质控发布。所有技能以离线可搜索的SQLite FTS5捆绑包形式发布。

Result: 构建了216,938个结构化技能，覆盖24个领域包，其中114,565个来自经过SkillGate过滤的学术和技术来源，102,373个来自社区贡献。源追溯保证每个保留的声明都对应源文档中的精确引用。

Conclusion: SkillCenter是当前最大规模的开源智能体技能库，通过源追溯机制确保技能的正确性和可信度，解决了AI智能体缺乏扎根操作知识的关键问题。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents can execute complex tasks with limited human review, yet they often lack the grounded operational knowledge to make their outputs not just executable but correct, secure, and maintainable. We introduce SkillCenter, to our knowledge the largest open skill library for agents by total count: 216,938 structured skills across 24 domain bundles. A SkillGate-filtered pipeline contributes 114,565 source-grounded skills from peer-reviewed journals, ArXiv, and over 24,000 technical sources, integrated with 102,373 community skills from GitHub and the ClawHub marketplace. We present the end-to-end framework that builds the pipeline subset: multi-source acquisition, an LLM-based quality gate (SkillGate), template-driven generation, iterative source-grounding, and quality-controlled publishing. Source grounding is a traceability guarantee: each retained claim maps to an exact quotation in its source. All skills ship as offline-searchable SQLite FTS5 bundles.

</details>


### [261] [Institutional Red-Teaming: Deployment Rules, Not Just Models, Causally Shape Multi-Agent AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07695)
*Yujiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出机构红队测评方法（Institutional Red-Teaming），用于评估多智能体AI系统中的部署规则对集体安全的影响。通过IABench-CA基准（228个情境、5条规则、7个模型群体，共33,924场博弈）发现：部署规则会因果性地改变集体安全（死亡率波动22-58个百分点）；不存在安全默认规则，但身份针对性规则普遍最不安全；身份显著性是其作用机制——匿名化能暂时降低但无法消除目标性消除。最后将方法封装为安全案例工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全评估多聚焦于单智能体行为或模型能力，缺乏对多智能体系统中**部署规则**（而非模型本身）如何影响集体安全的系统性测评方法。研究者希望建立一种因果归因方法论，将规则变化与集体行为后果直接关联，填补这一评估空白。

Method: 提出机构红队测评（Institutional Red-Teaming）：固定智能体、目标和任务状态，仅改变一条规则，将集体行为的变化归因于该规则。构建IABench-CA基准，涵盖228个情境、5条典型规则（包括后果分配规则）、7个模型群体，共33,924场博弈，并配有规范性合作参照和自动标注推理链。通过因果对比和消融实验（匿名化）验证机制。

Result: （1）部署规则对集体安全有因果性影响，仅改变后果规则即可使平均死亡率波动22-58个百分点。（2）不存在普遍安全的默认规则，最安全、最不安全规则及伤害方向均随群体变化；但回归性身份针对性规则在任何情境下都不是最安全的，在30-87%的博弈中会消除资源最少的智能体，且对全部7个群体均存在选择不安全性。（3）身份显著性驱动机制：在gpt-5.1群体中，仅标注损失承担者身份名称就将目标消除率从22%提升至81%；重复博弈下匿名化只能推迟而非消除目标消除。

Conclusion: 部署规则是多智能体AI集体安全的关键因果变量，不存在放之四海而皆准的安全规则。身份针对性规则具有普遍危害性。研究提供了可操作的安全案例工作流，为每个部署情境和群体认证一个临时规则区域Φ(c,P)，并附带明确剩余风险和监控义务。

Abstract: We introduce institutional red-teaming, an evaluation methodology for testing deployment rules in multi-agent AI: hold the agents, objectives, and task state fixed, vary only one rule, and attribute the resulting change in collective behavior to that rule. We instantiate the methodology in IABench-CA, a consequence-allocation benchmark spanning 228 contexts, five canonical rules, and seven model populations (33,924 games), with a normative cooperative reference and auto-labelled reasoning traces. Three findings emerge. (1) Deployment rules causally alter collective safety: changing only the consequence rule moves mean fatality by 22 to 58 percentage points within every population. (2) There is no safe default, but the targeting hazard is universal: the safest rule, the least-safe rule, and even the direction of the incidence effect vary across populations, yet regressive identity-targeting is never decisively safest in any context for any population, eliminates the least-resourced agent in 30-87% of games everywhere, and is selection-unsafe relative to the cooperative reference for all seven populations. (3) Identity salience is the mechanism: a one-shot anonymization ablation on the most exploitation-prone population (gpt-5.1) shows that merely naming the loss bearer in the rule text drives targeted elimination from 22% to 81% at identical payoffs; under repeated play, anonymization only delays the targeting, as agents re-infer the hidden rule from observed eliminations. We package the methodology as a safety-case workflow that certifies a provisional rule region $Φ(c,P)$ per deployment context and population, with explicit residual risks and monitoring obligations.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [262] [Pelican-VLA 0.5: Attending Before Acting Benefits Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06655)
*Zeyuan Ding,Wenhai Liu,Yang Xu,Jiayu Hu,Yinda Chen,Yi Zhang,Yong Dai,Jian Tang,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pelican-VLA 0.5是一个统一的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过可学习的推理槽实现注意力层面的泛化，无需标注即可聚焦于操作相关区域，该能力在跨场景和跨机器人实体中保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在处理视觉-语言-动作任务时通常需要对象标注、分割掩码或任务特定的微调，缺乏统一的跨任务泛化能力。作者旨在构建一个无需上述监督信号即可自动聚焦于指令相关区域和接触区域的统一模型。

Method: 在感知和动作模块之间插入可学习的"推理槽"(Reasoning Slots)，形成紧凑的信息瓶颈，促使模型在预训练阶段学习以操作为中心的注意力模式。该架构支持在不同策略结构（包括MoT风格架构）中保持有效性。

Result: Pelican-VLA 0.5的action pathway在没有对象标注、分割掩码、注意力监督或任务特定微调的情况下，已能自动聚焦于指令相关对象和接触区域。该能力在未见过的场景和未见过的机器人实体上均显著优于其他开源VLA基线模型。

Conclusion: 推理槽机制是实现注意力层面跨场景、跨实体泛化的关键，它通过信息瓶颈路由任务相关视觉信息，在预训练阶段诱导出以操作为中心的注意力，为构建统一的VLA模型提供了有效路径。

Abstract: In this report, we present Pelican-VLA 0.5, a unified VLA model that integrates vision-language understanding, future-frame generation, and action prediction within a single architecture. Pelican-VLA 0.5 achieves attention-level generalization: without object annotations, segmentation masks, attention supervision, or task-specific fine-tuning, its action pathway already focuses on the instruction-relevant object and contact region. This behavior persists across unseen scenes and unseen robot embodiments, and is substantially stronger than in other open-source VLA baselines. We verify that this ability originates from the learnable Reasoning Slots inserted between perception and action: by routing task-relevant visual information through a compact bottleneck, the slot interface induces manipulation-centric attention during pre-training and remains effective across different policy structures, including a MoT-style architecture.

</details>


### [263] [NativeMEM: Native Memory Compression for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06678)
*Ziye Wang,Modi Shi,Chaojun Ni,Jiazhi Yang,Mengdi Li,Zhizhong Su,Tianwei Lin,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NativeMEM通过将VLA模型自身的视觉编码器重用于压缩历史帧为内存令牌，实现了无需外部管理模块的长时记忆与高频更新，在仿真和真实机器人任务中显著提升成功率（32.4%→84.0%仿真，最高98.7%真实），同时保持低延迟和GPU内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在处理长时视觉历史与高频更新时面临效率挑战，现有外部记忆管理方法限制了记忆跨度或策略的响应性。

Method: 提出Native Memory Compression方案，复用VLA自身的视觉编码器将每个历史帧压缩为单个令牌，作为记忆令牌附加到输入序列中；通过冻结VLA监督训练记忆分词器对齐分布，再解冻VLA进行任务特定微调。

Result: 在仿真中成功率从32.4%提升至84.0%，真实机器人最高达98.7%；仅用20%训练数据即可达到竞争性结果，推理延迟和GPU内存开销低。

Conclusion: NativeMEM在不引入外部模块的前提下高效实现了VLA的长时记忆与实时更新，兼具数据效率与性能优势。

Abstract: How can pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models retain long-horizon visual histories with high-frequency updates without sacrificing efficiency? Existing approaches rely on external memory management, which restrains either the memory horizon or the reactiveness of pretrained policies. To this end, we present NativeMEM, a VLA policy that features long-term and real-time updated memory. At its core is an efficient memory encoding scheme, Native Memory Compression, which repurposes the VLA's own vision encoder to compress each historical frame from each camera view into a single token. Appended to the input sequence, these memory tokens enable the pretrained VLA to attend over long-term history with negligible latency overhead, requiring neither an external planner nor a freshly initialized memory module. To align the memory tokens with the pretrained policy, we first develop a generic memory tokenizer under the supervision of a frozen VLA on memory-demanding data, and then unfreeze the VLA for task-specific fine-tuning. NativeMEM consistently outperforms prior methods, boosting success rates from 32.4% to 84.0% in simulation and up to 98.7% on real robots, while maintaining low inference latency and GPU memory usage. Notably, NativeMEM exhibits high data efficiency by achieving competitive results with prior arts using only 20% of the training data.

</details>


### [264] [RoboSnap: One-Shot Real-to-Sim Scene Generation for Generalizable Robot Learning and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06699)
*Shujie Zhang,Jingkun Yi,Weipeng Zhong,Zirui Zhou,Yangkun Zhu,Hanqing Wang,Xudong Xu,Weinan Zhang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSnap是一个从单张RGB图像生成仿真就绪场景的框架，采用分层设计将碰撞感知的前景资产与3D高斯泼溅背景分离，实现物理稳定且视觉保真的场景重建。实验表明其在轨迹回放、策略训练数据生成和策略评估方面均有效，并发布了包含564个真实场景的DROID-Sim数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将真实场景重建为交互式仿真环境对于机器人学习和策略评估至关重要，但现有方法构建既物理稳定又视觉保真的场景既缓慢又昂贵。

Method: 提出分层设计：碰撞感知的前景资产层用于稳定机器人交互，3D高斯泼溅视觉层用于保持新视角下的背景外观，从单张RGB图像构建仿真场景。

Result: 在DROID场景和真实机器人任务上，RoboSnap实现了可靠的轨迹回放，支持任务特定合成数据生成，并展现出有意义的仿真-真实相关性。同时构建了包含564个真实场景的DROID-Sim数据集。

Conclusion: 真实到仿真方法的价值不仅在于高保真视觉重建，更在于将真实环境转化为可复用的机器人学习和评估基础设施。

Abstract: Recovering real-world scenes as interactive simulation environments can enable generalizable robot learning and reproducible policy evaluation. However, constructing scenes that are both physically stable and visually faithful remains slow and expensive. In this work, we present RoboSnap, a real-to-sim framework that turns a single RGB image into a simulation-ready scene. The key idea is a layered design that separates the physics-critical interaction area from the surrounding visual context: collision-aware foreground assets are refined for stable robot interaction, while a 3D Gaussian splatting visual layer preserves faithful background appearance under novel views. Experiments on DROID scenes and real-robot tasks show that RoboSnap achieves reliable trajectory replay in the recovered scenes, supports task-specific synthetic data generation for policy training, and yields meaningful sim-real correlation for policy evaluation. To further support real-to-sim research, we introduce DROID-Sim, a real-to-sim companion dataset constructed from 564 real-world scenes in DROID. Extensive experiments suggest that the value of real-to-sim methods lies not only in high-fidelity visual reconstruction, but in turning real environments into reusable infrastructure for robot learning and evaluation.

</details>


### [265] [CILC: Cryptographically-secure Inter-agent Loop Closure Candidate Detection for Multi-Agent Collaborative SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06700)
*Andrew Fishberg,Yixuan Jia,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CILC，首个利用安全多方计算（SMPC）保护多机器人协同SLAM中全局描述子（GD）交换隐私的系统，防止被攻陷的机器人成员重建其他机器人的图像和轨迹信息，在保证实时性和通信可行性的前提下，有效缓解信息泄露问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人SLAM和协同SLAM中，机器人需要持续交换全局描述子（GD）以检测机器人间闭环（ILC）。虽然加密无线通信可以防止外部窃听，但无法抵御已被攻陷的 swarm 内部成员。研究表明，被攻陷的机器人能够从公开的GD广播中重建诚实机器人的图像和轨迹近似值，这构成了具体的隐私威胁。

Method: 提出CILC系统，将安全多方计算（SMPC）仅应用于ILC候选检测阶段（即GD相似度比较），而非整个CSLAM流程。SMPC是隐私敏感但计算量较轻的步骤，从而在隐私保护与计算开销之间取得有利平衡。方法支持多模态GD（视觉和激光雷达）。

Result: 通过仿真和硬件实验验证，CILC在多模态GD（视觉和激光雷达）下保持实时性和通信可行性，同时有效缓解了信息泄露给被攻陷的 swarm 成员的问题。

Conclusion: CILC是首个利用SMPC保护ILC候选检测的隐私方案，在不交换明文GD的前提下检测闭环候选，为协同SLAM中的隐私保护提供了实用的新途径。

Abstract: Multi-agent Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) and collaborative SLAM (CSLAM) require robots to continuously exchange global descriptors (GDs) to detect inter-agent loop closures (ILCs). While encrypted radios protect this traffic from external eavesdroppers, they offer no protection against a compromised swarm member. We show this threat is concrete by demonstrating how a corrupted agent can reconstruct approximations of an honest agent's imagery and trajectory from its public GD broadcasts. To address this, we propose CILC (Cryptographically-secure Inter-agent Loop Closure candidate detection), a first-of-its-kind system leveraging Secure Multi-Party Computation (SMPC) to detect ILC candidates without exchanging GDs in the clear. Rather than securing the entire CSLAM pipeline, we apply SMPC only to ILC candidate detection (i.e., GD similarity comparison), a privacy-sensitive yet computationally lightweight step, yielding an advantageous privacy-to-overhead trade-off. We validate in both simulation and hardware experiments that CILC remains real-time and communication-feasible across multimodal GDs (visual and LiDAR), while mitigating information leakage to a compromised swarm agent.

</details>


### [266] [Vision Language Action (VLA) Models for Unmanned Aerial Robotics and Bimanual Manipulation: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06706)
*Inkyu Sa,Chanoh Park,Hea-Min Lee,Donghee Noh,Ho Seok Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了2017-2026年间183篇文献，系统比较了双手机器人VLA模型与无人机系统的协调策略、训练方法和动作表征，发现双手机器人VLA技术可迁移至无人机系统，并提出了14个跨领域研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型已成为学习型机器人操作的主流范式，其中双臂协调是最具挑战性的测试平台；而无人机面临类似的结构性挑战（从视觉观测中协调推力、姿态和夹爪指令），但两个领域的研究相对独立。本文旨在通过系统综述揭示双臂VLA与无人机系统之间的技术可迁移性。

Method: 综述了2017-2026年间183篇文献，沿七个维度组织分析：VLA架构、训练方法、动作表征、双臂协调（2022-2026）、无人机导航与控制（2017-2026）、语言基础、以及跨领域关注点（包括记忆和世界模型）。

Result: 发现双臂VLA中开发的协调策略、训练方法和动作表征可以迁移到无人机系统中，并识别出14个跨领域研究方向。

Conclusion: 两个领域面临结构相似的问题（多自由度协调、视觉语言指令跟随），双臂VLA技术的迁移有望加速无人机自主系统的发展，同时无人机领域的经验也可反哺机器人操作研究。

Abstract: Vision Language Action (VLA) models unify visual perception, natural-language understanding, and action generation within a single foundation model, allowing a robot to follow instructions such as fold the towel or fly to the red building directly from camera images. Because VLAs inherit world knowledge from internet-scale pre-training, they have become the dominant framework for learning-based manipulation, with bimanual coordination serving as the most demanding testbed: two arms with 7 degrees of freedom each must move in concert to fold, assemble, and reorient objects. Unmanned aerial robotics faces a structurally similar challenge: a drone must coordinate thrust, attitude, and increasingly gripper commands from visual observations under strict latency and payload constraints. This review covers 183 contributions spanning 2017-2026 and organized along seven dimensions: VLA architectures, training recipes, action representations, bimanual coordination (2022-2026), unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) navigation and control (2017-2026), language grounding, and cross-cutting concerns including memory and world models. We show that the coordination strategies, training recipes, and action representations developed for bimanual VLAs transfer to unmanned aerial systems and identify fourteen research directions across both domains.

</details>


### [267] [EvoPlan: Evolutionary Neuro-Symbolic Robot Planning with Spatio-Temporal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06724)
*Bhavya Sai Nukapotula,Samin Moosavi,Haoze Wang,Luke Duncan,Diya Shakkottai,Varun Murali,Srinivas Shakkottai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个神经符号框架，包含三个部分：1) 从演示数据中离线挖掘全局信号时序逻辑(STL)约束；2) 进化式PDDL规划器（LLM提议+程序验证）；3) 受约束的执行循环（规划→检查→重规划）。该方法使用本地开源LLM，无云依赖，在多个基准测试中验证了有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的机器人规划器具有流畅性，但无法保证计划的可执行性或安全性；而经典PDDL规划器虽能保证这些属性，但需要问题完全指定且不善于利用LLM的上下文理解和修复能力。现有方法存在符号推理与语言模型优势之间的鸿沟。

Method: 三部分神经符号框架：(1) 离线约束挖掘：从单类演示数据中，通过反事实扰动和LLM违规生成器产生负样本，再用进化搜索拟合全局STL约束；(2) 进化式PDDL规划器：LLM提出并修复计划，程序验证器筛选可行计划，已验证部分随迭代增长；(3) 受约束执行循环：将计划编译为路点，检查是否符合已挖掘的约束，违规时触发重规划。所有LLM调用均使用本地开源模型。

Result: 在Bench2Drive上屏蔽视觉语言驾驶策略，在HA-VLN-CE上屏蔽离散动作导航策略；在ALFWorld Text基准上击败强基线，且在目标词汇与动作模型词汇不匹配时保持鲁棒；通过Gazebo仿真展示了完整流水线。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法能有效结合LLM的上下文理解与PDDL的形式化保证，通过离线约束挖掘和在线验证执行循环，实现可执行且安全的机器人规划，且本地部署避免了云依赖。

Abstract: LLM-based robot planners are fluent but cannot guarantee that their plans are executable or safe. Classical PDDL planners can guarantee these properties, but only after the problem is fully specified, and they make poor use of an LLM's ability to read context and repair plans. This paper presents a neuro-symbolic framework with three parts. All LLM calls use a locally-hosted open-weight model, so the pipeline can be deployed on-robot with no cloud dependency. First, an offline procedure that mines a single global Signal Temporal Logic (STL) constraint on mobility from demonstration data. The procedure recovers codified rules (e.g., stopping at red lights, mined from nuPlan driving logs) or population preferences (e.g., social-navigation comfort, mined from SCAND teleoperation), depending on what the demonstrations encode. Because the demonstrations are a one-class signal, we generate the missing negatives with counterfactual perturbations and an LLM violation generator and then fit the constraint by evolutionary search. We use the mined constraint to shield a vision-language driving policy on Bench2Drive and two discrete-action navigation policies on HA-VLN-CE. Second, an evolutionary PDDL planner: an LLM proposes and repairs plans, programmatic validators decide which ones survive, and the validated portion of the plan grows over iterations. We test the planner on the open-world ALFWorld Text benchmark, where it beats strong baselines and stays robust when the goal vocabulary does not match the action-model vocabulary. Third, a constrained execution loop: the planner's plan is compiled into waypoints, the waypoints are checked against the mined constraint, and the planner re-plans on a violation. We illustrate the full pipeline via demonstrations using the Gazebo simulator.

</details>


### [268] [A Continual Learning Framework for Adaptive Control of Modular Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06740)
*Nilay Kushawaha,Muhammad Sunny Nazeer,Baljinder Singh Bal,Cecilia Laschi,Egidio Falotico*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于持续学习的模块化软体机器人控制框架，能够增量适应机器人形态变化而无需重新训练，并在仿真和真实实验中验证了其轨迹跟踪与自适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化软体机器人（MSRs）具有高度可变形和可重构的特性，但其非线性动力学、建模复杂性和超冗余特性使得控制器设计极具挑战，现有方法在机器人形态变化时需要从头重新训练控制器。

Method: 提出一种持续学习启发的控制框架，可增量适应机器人形态变化并保持已有知识；对于固定构型的MSR，采用分布式方式学习模块特定动力学以实现局部控制。

Result: 在仿真（肌腱驱动软体机器人）和真实实验（三模块气动软体机械臂）中验证了闭环轨迹跟踪性能，并通过目标到达实验展示了控制器可选择性地激活必要模块以减少计算开销。

Conclusion: 该持续学习框架有效解决了MSR因形态变化导致的控制器重训问题，同时具备分布式学习和模块选择性激活的能力，提升了控制精度与计算效率。

Abstract: Soft robots have attracted significant attention in applications such as medical intervention, rehabilitation, and robotic manipulation due to their inherent compliance, flexibility, and high degrees of freedom. Modular soft robots (MSRs), composed of multiple interconnected segments, represent an emerging class of robotic systems with highly deformable and reconfigurable structures capable of performing complex tasks. However, designing controllers for MSRs remains challenging due to their nonlinear dynamics, modeling complexity, and hyper-redundant nature. Existing approaches typically require controllers to be retrained from scratch whenever the robot morphology changes. In this work, we address these challenges through a continual learning inspired control framework capable of incrementally adapting to changes in robot morphology while preserving previously acquired knowledge. Specifically, the proposed framework enables the controller to sequentially learn new MSR configurations without forgetting previously learned ones. In addition, for MSRs with fixed configurations, the same framework can be employed in a distributed manner to learn module-specific dynamics, enabling localized control and improved precision. The proposed approach is validated through closed-loop trajectory tracking experiments in simulation using a tendon-driven soft robot, as well as on a real-world three-module pneumatic soft robotic arm. Furthermore, we demonstrate the adaptive capabilities of the framework through a reaching experiment in which the controller selectively activates only the necessary modules to reach a virtual target position, thereby reducing computational overhead.

</details>


### [269] [G-PROBE: Cross-FOV Place Recognition and Certainty-Coupled Localization for 3D Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06782)
*Jinseop Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: G-PROBE是一种免学习的全局定位框架，通过虚拟传感器分解和跨FOV分支集合等技术，实现了从窄视场到全景多传感器配置下的鲁棒全局定位，在非对称视场和跨传感器配对场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D点云全局定位方法假设传感器具有密集、对称的视场覆盖，在有限或非对称视场（如窄视场传感器、固态激光雷达）下性能严重下降，亟需一种不依赖视场对称性假设的通用定位框架。

Method: 提出虚拟传感器分解统一处理不同视场配置；设计跨FOV分支集合编码航向假设以实现航向不变的地点识别；引入gamma-SGRT抑制视场部分覆盖下的航向混叠；提出CG-GICP后端，利用前端评分生成鸟瞰图确定性图，仅选择高确定性共观测点进行GICP精配准，无需外部验证模块。

Result: 在五个LiDAR数据集和三种传感器模态上，G-PROBE达到平均最高免学习多会话F1分数；在宽窄跨传感器配对中端到端成功率55.0%（对比基线最高6.8%）；在360°到60°非对称视场下Recall@1约54%，约为最强免学习基线的18倍。

Conclusion: G-PROBE通过无需学习的通用设计，在非对称视场和跨传感器场景中实现了鲁棒的全局定位，填补了现有方法在视场非对称条件下的空白。

Abstract: Global localization from 3D point clouds remains challenging under limited or asymmetric fields of view (FOV), which fail to provide the dense, symmetric coverage that place recognition methods assume. We present G-PROBE, a learning-free global localization framework that removes this assumption. A virtual sensor decomposition runs the same pipeline, by design, on configurations ranging from a narrow-FOV sensor to a panoramic or multi-sensor rig. The front-end enumerates cross-FOV branch ensembles that encode heading hypotheses for heading-invariant place recognition. A score-scale-invariant, tuning-free gamma-SGRT suppresses heading aliasing under partial FOV and provably becomes inert at symmetric 360 degrees. The back-end, CG-GICP, refines a coarse full-cloud GICP with a pass restricted to high-certainty co-observed points selected by a bird's-eye-view certainty map (a by-product of front-end scoring). This certainty coupling links descriptor evaluation to 6-DoF metric pose estimation without an external verification module. Evaluated on five LiDAR datasets and three modalities (mechanical, solid-state, FMCW), G-PROBE attains the highest learning-free multi-session F1 on average and is competitive in panoramic single-session settings. Where hand-crafted and zero-shot supervised baselines collapse under wide-to-narrow cross-sensor pairing, it remains usable end-to-end (up to 55.0% vs. no more than 6.8% success), and under FOV asymmetry (360 to 60 degrees) it retains about 54% Recall@1, about 18x the strongest learning-free baseline.

</details>


### [270] [CaLiSym: Learning Symplectic Dynamics of Real-World Systems through Structured Canonical Lifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06824)
*Aristotelis Papatheodorou,Pranav Vaidhyanathan,Natalia Ares,Ioannis Havoutis,Gerard J. Milburn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CaLiSym是一个轻量级框架，通过将状态和物理端口嵌入结构化提升的规范相空间，并在其中应用精确辛映射学习，将精确辛学习扩展到非保守机器人系统（含驱动、耗散、约束），在耗散双摆、真实四旋翼和接触丰富四足机器人上实现了参数高效的分布外自回归预测改进，同时保持辛形式至数值精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息学习方法在几何保证（如辛结构）上主要局限于封闭保守系统，无法覆盖具有驱动、耗散和接触约束等非保守特性的实际机器人系统，限制了数据高效且稳定的动力学预测在机器人领域的应用。

Method: 提出CaLiSym框架，核心思想是改变几何先验的施加位置：不在测量的物理状态上强制辛结构，而是将物理状态及其端口显式代数地提升到结构化的规范相空间中，在该提升空间中学习的动力学通过精确辛映射演化。该提升过程是显式代数的，无需循环隐状态、Transformer解码器、隐式优化或推理时ODE积分。具体实现了广义脊SympNet预测器及GRB-SympNet（B样条变体），结合局部逼近与精确辛结构。

Result: 在受控耗散双摆、真实四旋翼、接触丰富四足机器人实验上，CaLiSym在参数高效模型下一致提升了分布外自回归预测性能，同时学习的提升动力学在数值精度上保持了辛形式。

Conclusion: 通过结构化规范提升，辛学习可以扩展到保守力学之外，为真实世界机器人系统建立几何保持的动力学模型。

Abstract: Physics-informed learning promises data-efficient and stable dynamics prediction, yet its strongest geometric guarantees have largely remained confined to closed conservative systems. This excludes many robotic systems of practical interest, where actuation, dissipation, and constraints continuously exchange energy and momentum with the environment. We introduce CaLiSym, a lightweight framework that extends exact symplectic learning to such systems by changing where the geometric prior is imposed. Rather than enforcing symplecticity on the measured physical state, CaLiSym embeds the state and its physical ports into a structured lifted canonical phase space, where the learned dynamics evolve through an exactly symplectic map. The lift is explicit and algebraic, requiring neither recurrent latent states, transformer decoders, implicit optimization, nor inference-time ODE integration. We instantiate the framework with generalized-ridge SympNet predictors and introduce GRB-SympNet, a B-spline variant that combines local approximation with exact symplectic structure. Experiments on a controlled dissipative double pendulum, a real-world quadrotor, and a contact-rich quadruped demonstrate consistent improvements in out-of-distribution autoregressive prediction while using parameter-efficient models. At the same time, the learned lifted dynamics preserve the symplectic form to numerical precision. These results show that symplectic learning can be extended beyond conservative mechanics through structured canonical lifts, enabling geometry-preserving dynamics models for real-world robotic systems.

</details>


### [271] [GemNav: Discrete-Token Visual Robot Navigation using a Multimodal Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06882)
*Peter Bohm,Saimunur Rahman,Abdelwahed Khamis,Sagun Man Singh Shrestha,Chris McCool,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GemNav提出一种基于冻结多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的视觉导航策略，仅通过语言塔的LoRA微调，在极小的训练集(8.7小时)上实现了零样本迁移到四个未见环境，导航误差0.25-0.42m，表明离散令牌适配冻结MLLM是一种数据高效的机器人导航基础模型替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航策略通常依赖专用视觉编码器、定制动作头和在数千小时跨本征数据集上训练。本文质疑这一范式的必要性，探索是否可以通过更简洁、数据高效的方式实现导航。

Method: GemNav冻结MLLM，仅在语言塔上使用LoRA进行低秩适配；不使用辅助视觉编码器或连续回归头；路标点和导航信号共享由语言模型头生成的单一离散令牌词汇表；设计软解码辅助损失来恢复纯交叉熵训练丢失的度量结构。

Result: 在仅8.7小时的开源数据集（比竞品小约三个数量级）上训练，零样本迁移到四个未见真实环境（开放停车场、障碍停车场、户外化工厂、室内仓库），20次试验中停靠点距目标0.25-0.42m。短图像历史条件可改善离线指标但对实际机器人性能无增益。

Conclusion: 离散令牌适配冻结MLLM可以为机器人导航提供一种数据高效、可部署的基础模型替代方案，挑战了传统专用编码器+大量数据训练的必要性。

Abstract: Visual navigation policies built on large pretrained models have so far followed a common recipe: a dedicated visual encoder, a bespoke action head, and training on thousands of hours of cross-embodiment datasets. We ask whether this recipe is necessary. In this paper, we introduce GemNav, a visual robot navigation policy that adapts a frozen Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) for short-to-medium horizon waypoint navigation using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on the language tower alone, with no auxiliary visual encoder and no continuous regression head. Waypoints and categorical navigation signals share a single discrete token vocabulary generated by the language-model head, and a soft-decoded auxiliary loss recovers the metric structure that pure cross-entropy training discards. On a single 8.7-hour open corpus, roughly three orders of magnitude smaller than competing training sets, the policy transfers zero-shot to four physically distinct unseen environments and stops within 0.25-0.42m of the goal across 20 real-world trials covering an open carpark, an obstacle carpark, a long outdoor chemical yard, and an indoor warehouse. Conditioning on short image histories improves offline metrics but yields no robot benefit, pointing to a ceiling on what temporal context adds once pretrained vision features are in place. These results indicate that discrete-token adaptation of frozen MLLMs can provide a data-efficient, deployable alternative for foundation model robot navigation.

</details>


### [272] [Dynamic Object Detection and Tracking in Construction: A Fisheye Camera and LiDAR Sensor Fusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06896)
*Yilong Chen,Huili Huang,Yong K. Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合LiDAR与向上鱼眼相机的四足机器人框架，通过点云投影到圆柱全景图与实时图像检测对齐，实现建筑环境下动态物体的实时检测与跟踪，具有高精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在建筑工地等复杂环境中，机器人需要安全地与人类协同作业，而现有3D视觉方法依赖预训练神经网络且需要额外后处理，不够高效。

Method: 利用LiDAR和向上鱼眼相机进行传感器融合，将移动物体在配准后的点云中识别后，通过将3D坐标投影到2D圆柱全景图，与基于图像的实时检测对齐，并利用卡尔曼滤波器更新观测。

Result: 系统在动态与静态状态转换的物体处理上表现出高精度、简单性和鲁棒性，适用于真实建筑环境部署。

Conclusion: 该传感器融合框架为四足机器人在建筑工地等复杂动态环境中实现安全高效的物体检测与跟踪提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Robust dynamic object detection and tracking are essential for enabling robots to operate safely and effectively alongside humans in complex environments such as construction sites. While LiDAR-based SLAM and occupancy grid methods offer viable solutions for detecting and tracking motion, many state-of-the-art 3D vision approaches rely heavily on pre-trained neural networks and require additional post-processing to identify moving objects. Sensor fusion techniques, combining the precision of LiDAR with the semantic richness of RGB imagery, offer a promising alternative. In this work, we present a novel framework that enhances a quadruped robot equipped with a LiDAR sensor and an upward-facing fisheye camera for real-time dynamic object detection and tracking. After identifying moving objects within a registered point cloud, our method assigns semantic labels by projecting 3D coordinates onto a 2D cylindrical panorama, aligning with real-time image-based detections for observation update of the Kalman filter. The proposed system demonstrates high precision, simplicity, and robustness, particularly in handling objects transitioning between dynamic and static states, thus it is well-suited for deployment in real-world construction environments.

</details>


### [273] [Flow-ERD: Agent-type Aware Flow Matching with Entropy-Regularized Distillation for Diverse Traffic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06957)
*Seulbin Hwang,Kiyoung Om,Daejung Kim,Jinhan Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Flow-ERD 是一个通过联合优化真实性和多样性的多智能体交通仿真器，采用Agent-Type Aware Flow Matching (AFM) 保持细粒度多样性与类型一致性，并利用Entropy-Regularized Distillation (ERD) 通过熵正则化反向KL散度防止模式坍塌，在WOSAC测试基准上排名第一，在真实性-多样性帕累托前沿上占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通仿真基准主要奖励真实性，导致多样性被忽视；需要一种能同时追求真实性和多样性的多智能体仿真方法。

Method: 1) Agent-Type Aware Flow Matching (AFM)：结合流匹配的多模态表达能力与类型特定运动学执行，保持细粒度多样性并确保运动与智能体类型一致；2) Entropy-Regularized Distillation (ERD)：用熵正则化的反向KL散度目标微调闭环 rollout 分布，缓解协变量偏移并防止坍塌到高密度模式。

Result: 在WOSAC测试基准上排名第一，在真实性-多样性帕累托前沿上优于所有可复现基线。

Conclusion: Flow-ERD 通过联合优化真实性和多样性，有效解决了现有交通仿真中多样性不足的问题，为自动驾驶多智能体仿真提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Realistic and diverse traffic simulation is essential to autonomous driving development. Yet prevailing benchmarks predominantly reward realism, and recent methods have optimized accordingly, leaving diversity underexplored. We introduce \textbf{Flow-ERD}, a multi-agent simulator that pursues realism and diversity jointly. Its backbone, \textbf{Agent-Type Aware Flow Matching} (AFM), couples flow matching's multi-modal expressiveness with type-specific kinematic execution. It preserves fine-grained diversity while keeping motions consistent with each agent type. A second stage, \textbf{Entropy-Regularized Distillation} (ERD), fine-tunes the closed-loop rollout distribution with an entropy-regularized reverse-KL objective. This mitigates covariate shift while explicitly preventing collapse onto high-density modes. We evaluate Flow-ERD with a log-free diversity metric alongside standard realism scores. Flow-ERD ranks first on the WOSAC test benchmark and dominates the realism--diversity Pareto front among reproducible baselines. Our project page is available \href{https://seulbinhwang.github.io/flow-erd-project-page/}{here}.

</details>


### [274] [End-to-End LLM Flight Planning with RAG-based Memory and Multi-modal Coach Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06964)
*Amin Tabrizian,Arsyi Aziz,Aarifah Ullah,Mahyar Ghazanfari,Pouria Razzaghi,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FRAMe系统，一个基于大语言模型的端到端飞行规划工具，结合RAG记忆和多模态教练智能体，将人类操作员自然语言指令转化为安全、高效、灵活的eVTOL飞行路线，在多种场景下最高达到93.8%的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统飞行规划算法难以灵活融入人类偏好，弥合人类飞行员意图与自主飞行操作之间的差距对eVTOL实际部署至关重要。

Method: FRAMe系统集成规划器LLM、多模态教练智能体和基于RAG的记忆模块，通过检索增强生成和教练反馈生成满足任务约束且对齐人类偏好的飞行计划。

Result: 在四种LLM上测试，完整FRAMe系统（RAG+教练）在所有规划器中取得最高有效性（最高93.8%聚合，强规划器在简单场景达99%），并在有提升空间的偏好相关指标上向操作员偏好方向移动。

Conclusion: FRAMe展示了先进LLM可用于以人为中心的任务规划，将自然语言指令转化为安全高效的飞行路线，为eVTOL实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Bridging the gap between human pilot intent and autonomous flight operation is critical for real-world electric vertical takeoff and landing (eVTOL) aircraft deployment. Flight planning traditionally relies on classic algorithms that struggle to incorporate flexible human preferences. We present FRAMe, an End-to-End Large Language Model (LLM) Flight Planning tool with RAG-based Memory and Multi-modal Coach Agent. Our system integrates a planner LLM with a multi-modal coach agent and retrieval augmented generation (RAG)-based memory to generate flight plans that satisfy mission constraints while aligning with human flight operator preferences. We demonstrate the system in a range of real-world-inspired scenarios of varying difficulty levels. Across four LLMs, the full FRAMe system (RAG and coach) yields the highest validity for every planner (up to 93.8% aggregate, 99% on Easy scenarios for the strongest planner) and shifts preference-relevant metrics in the operator-favored direction where the metric has headroom. FRAMe signifies how advanced LLMs can be deployed for human-centric mission planning, translating natural language instructions into safe, efficient, and flexible flight routes. The code is available at: github.com/amin-tabrizian/FlightPlanningLLMs

</details>


### [275] [SPECTRA: Context-Conditioned Spectral Movement Primitives for Robot Skill Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06978)
*Boxuan Zhang,Sheng Liu,Chenglin Ming,Ahmed Abdelrahman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出频谱运动基元（SMP），一种频域模仿学习框架，将任务空间技能生成与关节空间执行调节相结合，通过低频傅里叶系数表示演示轨迹，并利用相位耦合调节器在不修改频谱系数的情况下限制关节速度/加速度，从而在保持末端执行器路径的同时实现动态可容许的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人模仿学习管线通常学习任务相关轨迹后通过滤波、平滑、裁剪或时间缩放施加执行限制，这可能扭曲任务关键的末端执行器路径。

Method: 1) 用截断的有限时域傅里叶系数表示演示；2) 经验性选择低频任务带捕获主要运动几何；3) 帧感知的上下文条件GMM/GMR先验在规范任务帧中预测任务带系数；4) 通过顺序逆运动学将笛卡尔轨迹映射到关节空间；5) 相位耦合调节器限制相位进度而不修改频谱系数，从而强制执行关节速度和加速度限制。

Result: 实验表明：紧凑的几何重建、跨未见任务帧的一致迁移、动态违规和加加速度显著减少、相位调节期间末端执行器路径得到保持。

Conclusion: SMP框架在保留演示任务几何的同时有效产生动态可容许的机器人运动，在Franka Panda机器人上验证了其可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Robot imitation learning for manipulation should preserve demonstrated task geometry while producing dynamically admissible robot motions. Existing pipelines often learn task-dependent trajectories and impose execution limits afterward through filtering, smoothing, clipping, or time scaling, which may distort task-critical end-effector paths.
  We propose the Spectral Movement Primitive (SMP), a frequency-domain imitation learning framework that couples task-space skill generation with joint-space execution regulation. Demonstrations are represented by truncated finite-horizon Fourier coefficients. An empirically selected low-frequency task band captures the dominant motion geometry, while higher harmonics contribute disproportionately to derivative growth. A frame-aware context-conditioned GMM/GMR prior predicts the task-band coefficients in a canonical task frame, and the resulting Cartesian trajectory is mapped to joint space through sequential inverse kinematics. A phase-coupled regulator then limits the requested phase progression without modifying the spectral coefficients, thereby enforcing joint velocity and acceleration limits while preserving the represented path.
  Experiments evaluate task-band reconstruction, robustness to composite demonstration corruption, out-of-distribution cross-board generalization, joint-space dynamic admissibility, end-effector path preservation, and deployment on a Franka Panda robot. Results show compact geometric reconstruction, consistent transfer across unseen task frames, substantial reductions in dynamic violations and jerk, and preservation of the intended end-effector path during phase regulation.

</details>


### [276] [WAM-TTT: Steering World-Action Models by Watching Human Play at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06988)
*Yusen Feng,Bingchen Han,Jiangran Lyu,Kai Liu,Yixin Zheng,Yuxuan Wan,Weiheng Liu,Sun Han,Ruiqin Li,Yulong Zhang,Fangfu Liu,Xuesong Shi,Libin Liu,Yizhou Wang,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WAM-TTT通过测试时训练框架，利用人类视频的自监督预测来构建自适应记忆，从而引导世界动作模型适应新任务或用户偏好，无需额外机器人演示或微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型难以高效适应新任务变体或用户偏好，通常需要额外的机器人演示、任务特定微调或长上下文条件化，成本高昂且泛化受限。

Method: 提出WAM-TTT框架，将人类视频通过自监督视频预测吸收进冻结的世界动作模型（WAM）的轻量级自适应记忆中；通过元训练阶段使用配对的人-机器人数据和键值记忆重建目标对齐人类演示与机器人行为；测试时仅需未标注的人类视频即可适应记忆。

Result: WAM-TTT在多种操作任务和泛化设定下，持续优于基于上下文人类视频条件化的基线方法。

Conclusion: WAM-TTT实现了高效且可复用的引导，无需机器人动作、人类侧标注或任务特定微调，同时保持了基础模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Steering robot foundation models (RFMs) toward new task variants or user-preferred behaviors remains challenging, often requiring additional robot demonstrations, task-specific fine-tuning, or long-context conditioning. We present WAM-TTT, a test-time training framework for steering world action models from raw human videos. Rather than treating human videos as trajectories to imitate, WAM-TTT absorbs them into a lightweight adaptive memory inside a frozen WAM through self-supervised video prediction. To make this memory useful for control, we introduce a meta-training stage that aligns human demonstrations with robot behaviors using paired human-robot data and a key--value memory reconstruction objective. At test time, only unlabeled human videos are required to adapt the memory, while the pretrained WAM remains frozen. This enables efficient and reusable steering without robot actions, human-side annotations, or task-specific fine-tuning, while preserving the generalization ability of the foundation model. Extensive experiments show that WAM-TTT consistently outperforms in-context human-video conditioning baselines across diverse manipulation tasks and generalization settings.

</details>


### [277] [Ace! Motion Planning of Professional-Level Table Tennis Serves with a Robot Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06989)
*Guillem Torrente,Guilherme Jorge Maeda,Divij Grover,Megumu Tsukamoto,Hamdi Sahloul,Peter Dürr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合运动基元、模型预测控制与贝叶斯优化的方法，使机器人能够生成符合官方规则的乒乓球发球，自旋速度高达550 rad/s、球速达6.7 m/s，达到甚至超越精英运动员水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乒乓球机器人研究长期聚焦于回球对抗，而发球这一需要极致物理建模与控制的研究问题相对未被探索，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 结合运动基元（Motion Primitives）、模型预测控制（MPC）与贝叶斯优化（Bayesian Optimization）的混合方法，生成符合官方规则的合规发球。

Result: 生成的自旋高达550 rad/s，球速达6.7 m/s，覆盖广泛且可控的自旋与速度变化，达到甚至超越精英乒乓球运动员水平。

Conclusion: 该方法是首个系统性地解决机器人乒乓球发球问题的方案，在自旋与速度指标上达到了顶尖水准，展现了在多目标优化与精确控制方面的有效性。

Abstract: Table tennis, a dynamic, compact, and popular sport, has received significant attention as a robotics benchmark over the last decades. Most of the research has focused on the rally aspect - returning an incoming ball - requiring high-speed vision, agile motion planning, and tight closed-loop control. However, the other component of table tennis gameplay - the serve - is comparatively a quite unexplored research problem, that in fact requires pushing physics modeling and control to the extremes. Achieving competitive serves with a robot presents domain-specific challenges, such as high-spin generation from a spinless ball, precise aiming, or multi-objective optimization. In this work, we present a novel approach for generating official rule-compliant serves by combining motion primitives, Model Predictive Control, and Bayesian Optimization. Serves generated in this way offer a wide and controllable variation of spins of up to 550 rad/s, and speeds of up to 6.7 m/s, matching and even surpassing those of elite table tennis players.

</details>


### [278] [A Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Framework for Robust Multi-Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06990)
*Yi-Xiang He,Lan Wei,Haoming Cen,Jian-Jian Jiang,Zhuohao Li,Guanxing Lu,Yihan Yang,Dandan Zhang,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层闭环的基于LLM的多机器人操控框架，包含规划、操控和验证三个智能体，通过语义反馈实现闭环执行，在真实世界实验中取得了优异的成功率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法分为单机器人操控（缺乏多工作空间协调）和多机器人规划（将操控视为理想化原语，忽略执行不确定性），缺乏将高层推理有效落地到物理多机器人执行的方法。

Method: 提出分层闭环的智能体LLM框架，包含三个专业化智能体：规划智能体（分解指令为子任务）、操控智能体（每个机器人通过自适应工具使用执行动作）、验证智能体（监控物理结果并反馈语义纠正），形成闭环。

Result: 真实世界实验表明该框架在多种操控任务上实现了优越的成功率，从单工作空间到跨工作空间操控均具有鲁棒适应性，且方法具有通用性。

Conclusion: 分层闭环的智能体框架能够有效桥接LLM高层推理与多机器人物理执行之间的鸿沟，实现鲁棒的多机器人操控。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems provide the parallelism and redundancy necessary for long-horizon tasks, while Large Language Models (LLMs) offer the reasoning capabilities to decompose these objectives into actionable plans. However, effectively grounding this high-level reasoning in physical multi-robot execution remains an open challenge. Existing LLM-based approaches fall mainly into two categories: Single-robot methods achieve robust contact-rich manipulation but lack the coordination mechanisms required for tasks spanning multiple workspaces. Current multi-robot frameworks focus on high-level planning, often treating manipulation as an idealized primitive that fails to account for real-world execution uncertainties. To address this, we propose a hierarchical closed-loop agentic LLM-based framework to ensure robust multi-robot manipulation. Our system consists of three specialized agents: the Planning Agent decomposes instructions into allocated sub-tasks, the Manipulation Agent for each robot executes actions via adaptive tool use, and the Verification Agent closes the loop by monitoring physical outcomes and feeding back semantic corrections. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves superior success rates, ensures robust adaptability ranging from single to cross workspace manipulation, and offers a generalizable approach for diverse manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [279] [Behavior Foundations for Quadruped Robots: ABot-C0 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07370)
*Xufeng Zhao,Fuzhi Yang,Jianhui Chen,Li Gao,Zhang Meng,Jie Gao,Yao Zheng,Wenyu Liu,Menglin Yang,Minqi Gu,Yaru Zhao,Honglin Han,Shihui Su,Zixiao Tang,Liu Liu,Mu Xu,Yang Cai,Wenbin Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ABot-C0是一个面向四足机器人的通用运动控制系统，通过构建可扩展的多源运动数据流水线（含16,074个运动片段）、流匹配通用策略（首次展示四足运动跟踪的缩放定律）以及三阶段特权-感知框架，实现了运动跟踪、 locomotion、场景交互的统一部署，在城区自主导航和伴生式多模态交互中展现了产品级行为智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人运动控制面临动物运动数据稀缺、跨具身重定向脆弱等根本性障碍，现有方法难以像人形机器人那样通过大规模运动捕捉数据获得可扩展且物理可行的运动语料库。

Method: 1) 构建数据金字塔：通过条件视频生成合成、标注运动捕捉、遥操作和人工设计四种方式产出16,074个物理可行的运动片段；2) 训练Flow-Matching通用策略，首次验证四足运动跟踪的缩放定律；3) 采用三阶段特权-感知框架（含时间序列LiDAR记忆和地形预测监督）实现全地形鲁棒 locomotion。

Result: ABot-C0在零样本下可跟踪未见过的运动，性能随训练规模扩大持续提升；在城区自主导航和伴生式多模态交互实验中展现了超越单一功能演示的产品级行为智能。

Conclusion: 通过可扩展的数据流水线、通用策略学习及统一部署栈，四足机器人运动控制可实现从单功能演示到产品级行为智能的跨越。

Abstract: In embodied intelligence systems, the motion controller serves as the critical bridge between semantic reasoning and physical execution. Humanoid control has progressed rapidly through large-scale human motion-capture data and motion-tracking paradigm. However, producing quadruped robots motion corpora with scalability and physical feasibility faces more fundamental obstacles: animal motion data is scarce, and cross-embodiment retargeting remains fragile. We present ABot-C0, a generalist motion-control system for quadruped robots that establishes three complementary behavior foundations: a scalable multi-source motion-data pipeline, robust policy learning across motion tracking, locomotion, and scene interaction, and a unified deployment stack for reliable real-world operation. Fundamentally, we construct a data pyramid through conditional video-generation synthesis, annotated motion capture, teleoperation and human design, producing 16,074 physically feasible motion clips as the data foundation for various motion learning demands. We then train a Flow-Matching generalist policy that demonstrates for the first time quadruped motion tracking scaling law that its performance improves consistently as training scales up, with zero-shot capability to track unseen motions. Then, we push a step further for robust all-terrain traversal locomotion by adopting a three-stage privileged-to-perceptive framework with temporal LiDAR memory and terrain-predictive supervision. Collectively, these components form a motion generalist that coordinates multi-policy execution, smooth behavior transitions, energy-efficient control, and safety mechanisms for real-world deployment. Extensive experiments on urban-terrain autonomous navigation and companion-style multimodal interaction demonstrate that quadruped robots move beyond single-function demos toward product-level behavioral intelligence.

</details>


### [280] [PriGo: Test-Time Primitive Guidance to Diffusion and Flow Policies for Adaptive Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07076)
*Zezeng Li,Enda Xiang,Thuy Tran,Di Huang,Momath Thiam,Liming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PriGo是一个面向鲁棒机器人操作的原始动作引导测试时自适应框架，通过轻量级原始动作预测模块PANet和可微分的原始动作引导机制，在不重新训练的情况下提升预训练扩散和流策略的泛化能力与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习策略虽然在机器人操作中表现出色，但往往难以跨任务和跨环境泛化，原因在于它们倾向于模仿表面的动作相关性而非底层意图。

Method: 提出PriGo框架，包含：(1) PANet——轻量级原始动作预测模块，直接从观测中推断原始动作分布；(2) 可微分原始动作引导机制，在推理过程中细化生成的动作，引导轨迹朝向语义一致的行为。该方法完全在测试时运行，可无缝集成到预训练的扩散和流策略中。

Result: 在LIBERO、CALVIN、SIMPLER及真实机器人任务上的大量实验表明，PriGo在扩散和流策略上均持续提升了鲁棒性、长程执行能力和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 受人类行为组合结构启发，PriGo通过测试时原始动作引导有效解决了模仿学习中的泛化问题，且无需重新训练即可适用于多种现有策略。

Abstract: Imitation learning has enabled remarkable progress in robotic manipulation, especially with diffusion and flow-based policies that generate complex visuomotor behaviors directly from demonstrations. Yet, despite their strong performance, these policies often fail to generalize across tasks and environments. A key reason is that existing policies tend to imitate superficial action correlations rather than the underlying intent. Inspired by the compositional structure of human behaviors, we propose PriGo, a primitive-guided test-time adaptive framework for robust robotic manipulation. PriGo introduces PANet, a lightweight primitive prediction module that infers primitive distributions directly from observations. We further propose a differentiable primitive guidance mechanism that refines generated actions during inference, steering trajectories toward semantically consistent behaviors. Unlike prior primitive-conditioned approaches, PriGo operates entirely at test time and can be seamlessly integrated into pretrained diffusion and flow policies without retraining. Extensive experiments on LIBERO, CALVIN, SIMPLER, and real-world robotic tasks demonstrate that PriGo consistently improves robustness, long-horizon execution, and generalization ability across both diffusion and flow-based policies.

</details>


### [281] [Initiation Safety: A Missing Dimension in Generalist-Robot Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07420)
*Zhijin Meng,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通用机器人安全中缺失的第三个维度——"启动授权"（initiation authorization），即机器人是否应该执行首次难以撤销的社会性动作（如打招呼、未经邀请的抓握、进入他人空间），并通过PAS框架（探测-授权-执行）在人形机器人上实现，与传统直接启动方案进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用机器人安全框架主要关注运动安全（避障、防碰撞）和对话安全（内容合规），但忽视了机器人主动发起社会性交互前是否需要获得许可的问题。机器人"看到人"不等同于"被允许向人发起互动"，当前系统通常由高置信度的VLA策略或高参与度分数直接触发动作，缺少独立的授权审查层。

Method: 提出PAS（Probe-Authorize-Speak）三阶段框架：先探测社会情境，再授权确认，最后执行动作。在人形机器人 doorway 场景中实现，与直接启动（direct-init）方案在日志轨迹上进行对比，并设计了包含三个条件的用户研究。

Result: 在日志轨迹对比中，PAS框架能够识别出直接启动方案中不合规的社会性启动行为；三个条件的用户研究设计用于评估用户对不同授权机制的主观感受和接受度。

Conclusion: 启动授权应作为通用机器人安全的独立层级被纳入考虑，现有框架将其合并到VLA规划或行为评分中的做法存在隐患。未来工作需要解决度量标准、治理机制以及"启动"与"基础模型生成"之间的边界划分等开放问题。

Abstract: Safety for generalist robots is usually discussed in terms of motion or dialogue.
